SRENet introduces a Spectral Decomposition Block (SDeBlock) that performs wavelet-based analysis along temporal and spatial axes, disentangling features into low- and high-frequency components with frequency-specific attention. To recover residual dynamics and re-align temporal frequency structures distorted during semantic fusion, a Spectral Re-entry Block (SReBlock) performs secondary temporal decomposition. Furthermore, a spectral-aware learning strategy is devised to enhance discriminability in both frequency subspaces via contrastive loss and a curriculum schedule that gradually shifts focus from low- to high-frequency spaces in line with coarse to detailed motion patterns. Extensive experiments on MSR-Action3D, NTU-RGBD and NTU-RGBD120 demonstrate that SRENet achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating the effectiveness of frequency modeling in point cloud-based action understanding.

</details>


### [36] [JAVEDIT: Joint Audio-Visual Instruction-Guided Video Editing with Agentic Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03168)
*Yinan Chen,Chuming Lin,Zhennan Chen,Yuxiang Zeng,Junwei Zhu,Yali Bi,Xijie Huang,Chengming Xu,Donghao Luo,Zhucun Xue,Xiaobin Hu,Chengjie Wang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个大规模高质量联合音视频编辑数据集JAVEdit-100k（约10万编辑三元组，覆盖5类任务），配套标准化评估基准JAVEditBench，以及基线模型JAVEdit，在6项指标中的5项上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于指令的视频编辑虽有显著进展，但联合音视频编辑因缺乏专用数据集和基准而受限，现有工作无法同时编辑视频的视觉和音频轨道。

Method: 构建了JAVEdit-100k数据集（4种精心设计的生成管线+智能体质量控制机制），提出JAVEditBench标准化评估基准，并设计JAVEdit基线模型。

Result: JAVEdit在6项评估指标中的5项上超越所有基线方法。

Conclusion: JAVEdit-100k数据集和JAVEditBench基准填补了联合音视频编辑领域的空白，JAVEdit模型证明了该方向的有效性，为后续研究提供了基础资源。

Abstract: While instruction-based video editing has seen significant progress, joint audio-visual editing remains constrained by the absence of dedicated datasets and benchmarks. To bridge this gap, we present JAVEdit-100k, the first large-scale, high-quality dataset tailored for instruction-guided joint audio-visual editing. Focusing on human-centric videos, JAVEdit-100k comprises approximately 100K editing triplets spanning five distinct categories, including subject editing and speech editing. This dataset is rigorously constructed via four meticulously designed generation pipelines, seamlessly paired with an agent-in-the-loop quality control mechanism. Furthermore, to address the lack of standardized evaluation within the field, we introduce JAVEditBench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring curated source videos and human-aligned instructions across all editing categories. Finally, we propose JAVEdit, a pioneering baseline model for instruction-guided joint audio-visual editing. Experiments show that \model\ outperforms all baselines on five of six evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [37] [Ask When It Pays: Cost-Aware Open-Ended Interaction for Instance Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03175)
*Xunyi Zhao,Sihao Lin,Gengze Zhou,Zerui Li,Shijie Li,Wei Tao,Jiajun Liu,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种成本敏感的不确定性减少方法来解决实例目标导航（IGN）中的交互问题，通过信息增益分析确定最优提问类型，并构建了新基准和加权成功率指标来评估交互效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式IGN方法将轻量级澄清和路径级指导同等对待，导致智能体通过重复提问来提高成功率，而非高效解决底层歧义。

Method: 将交互式IGN建模为成本敏感的不确定性减少问题，通过信息增益分析导航语料库识别问题类型和权重；提出零样本MLLM导航器，仅在预期不确定性减少证明交互成本合理时才选择性提问。

Result: 构建了新的诊断基准和加权成功率（Weighted Success Rate）指标，每个查询按其推导成本进行惩罚。

Conclusion: 交互应聚焦于高效消除歧义而非频繁提问，成本敏感的设计能更真实评估导航效率。

Abstract: Instance Goal Navigation (IGN) requires an embodied agent to find a specific object instance among distractors from an underspecified natural-language description. Such ambiguity often cannot be resolved from perception and language alone, making interaction with an oracle a natural mechanism for disambiguation. Prior interactive methods allow oracle queries but treat lightweight clarification and route-level guidance alike, letting agents boost success rate through repeated high-information questions rather than by resolving the underlying ambiguity efficiently. We recast interactive IGN as a cost-sensitive uncertainty-reduction problem, where the agent should ask the question whose answer provides the largest reduction in navigation uncertainty relative to its penalty. To this end, we apply an information-gain analysis on existing navigation corpora to identify which cues reduce navigation uncertainty, yielding a compact set of question types and data-derived weights.However, existing interactive navigation benchmarks do not model the cost of different question types or evaluate how efficiently agents use interaction, making them unsuitable for studying cost-sensitive interaction. Based on this taxonomy, we construct a benchmark for diagnosing interaction behavior and efficiency, together with a Weighted Success Rate metric that penalizes each query by its derived cost. We further propose a zero-shot MLLM navigator that selectively queries at each decision step only when the expected uncertainty reduction justifies the interaction cost.

</details>


### [38] [GLINT: Sparsely Gated Vision-Language Alignment for Fine-Grained Radiology Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03180)
*Jonggwon Park,Seongeun Lee,Junhyun Park,Hannah Yun,Hyunwoong Kim,Sohyun Jeong,Hyewon Kang,Byungmu Yoon,Kyoyun Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLINT通过稀疏门控对齐和密集特征正则化，解决了放射学视觉-语言模型中图像与报告尺度不匹配的问题，实现了从自由文本查询的零样本分类、定位和分割，尤其在3D CT体积上首次实现零样本分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学VLM面临核心挑战：每个病灶仅占据图像的小区域，但监督信号只在全局图像-报告层级提供，导致先前的密集方法对无关区域也施加权重，未能聚焦于与查询相关的稀疏子集。

Method: 提出GLINT框架：(1) 稀疏门控对齐——在独立的门控嵌入空间上用sigmoid门控激活与文本查询相关的补丁，强制显式稀疏性；(2) 密集特征正则化——将可训练编码器的中间特征锚定到冻结的自监督学习教师模型，保留细粒度补丁特征。适用于2D CXR (DINOv3)和3D CT (V-JEPA 2.1)。

Result: GLINT实现了从自由文本查询的零样本分类、定位和分割，首次在3D CT体积上实现无掩码监督的零样本分割。在下游任务（分类、报告生成、分割）中优于SSL编码器和医学VLM，在需要稀疏、查询特定定位的零样本定位和分割任务上增益最显著。

Conclusion: GLINT通过显式建模稀疏对应关系，有效解决了放射学VLM中图像与文本的尺度不匹配问题，为医学影像分析提供了一种统一的、可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) for radiology have emerged as a scalable paradigm by leveraging image-report pairs naturally produced in clinical workflows. However, this pairing reveals a mismatch in scale: each finding occupies only a small region of the image, yet supervision is provided only at the global image-report level. This poses a central challenge: prior approaches spread weight densely across all patches rather than concentrating on the sparse subset relevant to a given query. To address this, we present GLINT (Gated Language-Image alignmeNT), a framework that explicitly models this sparse correspondence. On the alignment side, we introduce Sparsely Gated Alignment, a novel architecture in which a sigmoid gate over a separate gate embedding space activates only the patches relevant to each textual query, enforcing explicit sparsity. On the representation side, we add Dense Feature Regularization, which anchors the trainable encoder's intermediate features to a frozen self-supervised learning (SSL) teacher, preserving the fine-grained patch features that the gate relies on. The same recipe applies to both 2D chest X-ray (CXR) and 3D chest computed tomography (CT), built with DINOv3 and V-JEPA 2.1, respectively. GLINT enables zero-shot classification, grounding, and segmentation from free-text queries, and to our knowledge is the first to demonstrate zero-shot segmentation on 3D CT volumes without mask supervision. Notably, the most pronounced gains arise on zero-shot grounding and segmentation, where sparse, query-specific localization is required, consistent with our design intent. In downstream evaluation, GLINT outperforms both SSL encoders and medical VLMs on classification, report generation, and segmentation.

</details>


### [39] [Reinforcement Learning from Cross-domain Videos with Video Prediction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03201)
*Zhao Yang,Xinrui Zu,Jacob E. Kooi,Thomas Delliaux,He Liu,Shujian Yu,Kevin Sebastian Luck,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XIPER提出跨域视频预测奖励模型，通过将智能体观测映射到专家域并用预测似然作为奖励信号，成功解决了视觉不同域间的强化学习问题，在多个基准任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家视频与智能体所在域存在视觉差异（如颜色、形态、sim-to-real差距），导致无法直接获得奖励信号，现有方法难以跨域迁移。

Method: 训练一个跨域视频预测模型，将智能体的观测映射到专家域，然后使用该预测的似然（likelihood）作为奖励信号来指导强化学习。

Result: 在DMC Color Suite（8个任务）和DMC Body Suite（3个任务）上持续优于基线方法；在sim-to-real迁移数据集上也能为真实机器人观测产生有意义的奖励信号。

Conclusion: XIPER通过视频预测似然作为跨域奖励信号，有效克服了视觉域差距，实现了从专家视频中学习的目标。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from expert videos across visually distinct domains is challenging due to the absence of reward signals and the presence of domain gaps. We introduce XIPER (Cross-domain Video Prediction Reward), a reward model for learning from expert videos collected in a visually different domain, where the agent's appearance differs due to factors such as color, morphology, or the sim-to-real gap. More specifically, XIPER trains a cross-domain video prediction model that maps agent observations into the expert domain and uses the prediction likelihood as a reward signal. Experiments on the DMC Color Suite (8 tasks) and DMC Body Suite (3 tasks) show that XIPER consistently outperforms baselines despite domain gaps such as differences in agent color and morphology. We further analyze XIPER on a sim-to-real transfer dataset, demonstrating that it produces meaningful reward signals for real-robot observations given only simulated expert videos. Code, pretrained models, datasets and video demonstrations can be found on our project webpage: https://sites.google.com/view/xiper

</details>


### [40] [Follow-Your-Preference++: Rethinking Preference Alignment for Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03216)
*Junkun Yuan,Yutao Shen,Toru Aonishi,Hideki Nakayama,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了图像修复中的偏好对齐问题，基于直接偏好优化框架，利用公开奖励模型构建偏好数据，实验发现奖励模型存在亮度、构图等偏见问题，提出简单集成方法可缓解奖励黑客现象，在不改变模型架构下显著超越先前SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新从第一性原理审视图像修复中的偏好对齐问题，而非简单提出新方法，旨在理清核心挑战。

Method: 采用直接偏好优化（DPO）框架，使用九个公开奖励模型构建偏好训练数据，在两个基准测试和两种不同架构/生成机制的基线修复模型上进行实验，并探索奖励模型集成策略。

Result: 奖励模型多数有效但存在亮度、构图、色彩方面的偏见易导致奖励黑客；偏好数据在候选和样本缩放下呈现一致趋势；简单集成可缓解偏见；偏好对齐可迁移至物体移除任务；校准集成方法进一步提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 无需修改模型架构或引入额外数据集，所提方法在标准指标、大视觉语言模型评估和人工评估上均大幅超越先前SOTA。

Abstract: We study preference alignment for image inpainting. Rather than proposing yet another method, we revisit the problem from first principles and reassess its core challenges. We adopt the widely used direct preference optimization framework and construct preference training data with publicly available reward models. Our empirical study spans nine reward models, two benchmarks, and two baseline inpainting models that differ in architecture and generative mechanism. Our main findings are: (1) Most reward models provide valid signals for preference data construction, although some are unreliable as evaluators. (2) Across models and benchmarks, preference data exhibits consistent trends under both candidate and sample scaling. (3) Reward models display pronounced biases--particularly in brightness, composition, and color scheme--that make them prone to inducing reward hacking. (4) A simple ensemble of reward models mitigates such biases and yields robust, generalizable performance. {\color{rebuttal_blue}(5) Preference alignment is transferable to the object removal task, where the goal shifts from open-ended creative generation to coherent background completion. (6) Further analysis reveals that a calibrated ensemble method further mitigates hacking and improves robustness.} Without modifying model architectures or introducing additional datasets, our models substantially outperform prior state-of-the-art models on standard metrics, large vision-language model evaluations, and human assessments. Our code is available at: https://github.com/shenytzzz/Follow-Your-Preference.

</details>


### [41] [MemoGen: Can Past Experience Improve Future Text-to-Image Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03243)
*Wenshuo Chen,Kuimou Yu,Bowen Tian,Jianfei Song,Shaofeng Liang,Haozhe Jia,Kan Cheng,Haosen Li,Kaishen Yuan,Lei Wang,Jiemin Wu,Songning Lai,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoGen，一种无需训练的框架，通过代理演化层和可复用经验记忆，使文本到图像生成系统能够从自身经验中持续改进，在不更新底层生成器的情况下提升生成可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型在需要隐性视觉约束、关系推理或外部知识的提示下表现不可靠。当前的检索增强和代理生成方法将每次生成视为孤立事件，未能系统性地保留过往成功或失败经验以供未来使用。

Method: MemoGen是一个无需训练的框架，为现有图像生成器添加代理演化层。每个任务中，它显式推断视觉需求、检索外部证据和参考、将其转化为可执行的生成约束、评估生成结果，并将任务理解、参考选择、视觉反馈、成功策略和失败教训存储为可复用的经验记忆。在演化轮次中，代理检索相关经验以改进相似的未来生成，选择性修复失败案例，无需参数更新即可实现测试时自我演化。

Result: 在知识密集型和推理导向基准上，基于开源Qwen-Image骨干的MemoGen仅经过两轮演化，就在WISE和Mind-Bench上超越了Nano Banana Pro和GPT-Image-1等强商业系统。

Conclusion: 显式的经验记忆可以作为可靠的文本到图像生成中一种强大的持续学习信号，验证了无需参数更新的测试时自我演化范式的有效性。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image models have achieved strong visual synthesis, yet remain unreliable when prompts require implicit visual constraints, relational reasoning, or external knowledge. Existing retrieval-augmented and agentic generation methods mitigate this issue by acquiring external knowledge, references, or refined prompts for the current request, yet they typically treat each generation as an isolated episode and do not systematically preserve past successes or failures for future use. In this work, we ask whether a text-to-image system can continually improve from its own generation experience without updating the underlying generator. We propose MemoGen, a training-free framework that augments existing image generators with an agentic evolution layer. For each task, MemoGen explicitly infers visual requirements, retrieves external evidence and references when necessary, translates them into executable generation constraints, evaluates the generated result, and stores task understanding, reference choices, visual feedback, successful strategies, and failure lessons as reusable experience memory. Across evolution rounds, the agent retrieves relevant experience to improve similar future generations, selectively repairing previously failed cases while preserving successful ones, thereby enabling test-time self-evolution without parameter updates. Extensive experiments on knowledge-intensive and reasoning-oriented benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of this paradigm: after only two evolution rounds, MemoGen built upon the open-source Qwen-Image backbone surpasses strong proprietary systems such as Nano Banana Pro and GPT-Image-1 on WISE and Mind-Bench, showing that explicit experience memory can serve as a powerful continual learning signal for reliable text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [42] [MariData: One-Step Unpaired Image Translation for Maritime Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03246)
*Santeri Henriksson,Mehdi Asadi,Amin Majd,Juha Kalliovaara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于CycleGAN-turbo的框架，通过零卷积跳跃连接保留小目标细节，生成合成海事数据解决MASS感知系统训练数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事自主水面船（MASS）的鲁棒感知系统受限于多样化训练数据的稀缺，尤其是在恶劣天气和低光照条件下。由于动态海事环境中无法物理采集配对图像，无配对图像翻译生成合成数据成为关键解决方案，但现有生成模型因潜在压缩瓶颈无法保留小导航目标的精细结构细节。

Method: 采用CycleGAN-turbo单步无配对翻译架构，引入零卷积跳跃连接以绕过VAE瓶颈，保留小目标细节（如远处的船只和海上标志）。构建7000张海事图像数据集，训练Day-to-Foggy、Day-to-Sunset、Day-to-Night三个域翻译模型。

Result: 定性评估和变强度推理实验表明：方法能有效合成逼真的大气条件并保持场景语义结构；Day-to-Foggy和Day-to-Sunset模型结构保持良好；Day-to-Night模型因训练分布不平衡存在语义幻觉问题（如生成人造海岸灯光）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了高效、结构感知的数据合成管线，直接解决自主海事导航中的数据稀缺瓶颈。

Abstract: The development on robust perception systems for Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) is heavily constrained by the scarcity of diverse training data, particularly for adverse weather and low-light conditions. Because collecting paired images in dynamic maritime environments is physically impossible, synthetic data generation via unpaired image-to-image translation offers a critical solution. However, existing generative models suffer from failing to preserve the fine structural details of small navigational objects due to latent compression bottlenecks. In this paper, we introduce a framework for generating synthetic maritime data using CycleGAN-turbo, a one-step unpaired translation architecture. By incorporating zero-convolution skip connections to bypass the Variational Autoencoder (VAE) bottleneck, our approach explicitly preserves small object details (e.g., distant vessels and sea marks) during translation. We compiled a dataset of 7,000 maritime images to train and evaluate models for Day-to-Foggy, Day-to-Sunset, and Day-to-Night domain translations. Qualitative evaluations and variable-strength inference studies demonstrate that our method effectively synthesizes realistic atmospheric conditions while maintaining the underlying semantic structure of the scene. The Day-to-Foggy and Day-to-Sunset models exhibit great structural retention, whereas the Day-to-Night model highlights the challenge of semantic hallucination, such as generating artificial coastal lights, induced by unbalanced training distributions. Ultimately, this work establishes an efficient, structure-aware data synthesis pipeline that directly addresses the data scarcity bottleneck in autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [43] [FreeStreamGS: Online Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unposed Streaming Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03254)
*Ruiyang Chen,Feiran Li,Chu Zhou,Zonglin Li,Zhanyu Ma,Heng Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeStreamGS提出了一种鲁棒的在线前馈框架，用于从流式无位姿图像输入进行高效高质量的新视角合成。通过解耦内在恢复头和动态点精炼偏移策略，解决了相机内在偏差累积和位姿-深度耦合漂移问题，在无未来帧的情况下实现了与离线方法相当的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法依赖离线录制的有序图像序列，无法处理流式、无位姿的在线图像输入。虽然在线前馈几何估计方法可用于流式深度和点云恢复，但因渲染伪影严重而无法适用于新视角合成——新视角合成对高斯尺度的多视图一致性和位姿-几何对齐要求更严格，微小偏差会随时间累积并明显降低渲染质量。

Method: 提出了FreeStreamGS，包含两个关键机制：1) 解耦内在恢复头，消除长期流式中相机内在偏差的累积，防止场景尺度抖动；2) 动态点精炼偏移策略，放松刚性反投影以校正耦合的位姿-深度漂移。

Result: 大量实验表明，尽管无法访问未来帧，FreeStreamGS达到了与最先进的离线前馈式3DGS方法相媲美的渲染质量。

Conclusion: FreeStreamGS成功实现了从流式无位姿图像到高质量在线新视角合成的跨越，通过解耦内在恢复和动态点精炼有效解决了在线场景中的累积误差和漂移问题。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) allows efficient and high-fidelity novel view synthesis (NVS) from an offline recorded image sequence. However, achieving online NVS from streaming and unposed image inputs remains challenging. Although online feed-forward geometric estimation methods have been proposed for streaming depth and point cloud recovery, they cannot be adapted to NVS due to severe rendering artifacts. This is because NVS demands stricter multi-view consistency in Gaussian scales and pose-geometry alignment; even minor deviations would accumulate over time and visibly degrade rendering quality. To this end, we propose FreeStreamGS, a robust online feed-forward framework for efficient and high-quality NVS. We introduce two key mechanisms: a Decoupled Intrinsic Recovery Head that removes cumulative camera intrinsic bias and prevents scene scale jitter during long-term streaming, and a Dynamic Point Refinement Offset strategy that relaxes rigid unprojection to correct coupled pose-depth drift. Extensive experiments show that FreeStreamGS achieves rendering quality competitive with state-of-the-art offline feed-forward 3DGS methods, despite operating without access to future frames.

</details>


### [44] [PaddleOCR-VL-1.6: Expanding the Frontier of Document Parsing with Under-Optimized Region Refinement and Progressive Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03264)
*Zelun Zhang,Hongen Liu,Suyin Liang,Yubo Zhang,Yiqing Xiang,Jiaxuan Liu,Ting Sun,Manhui Lin,Yue Zhang,Changda Zhou,Tingquan Gao,Cheng Cui,Yi Liu,Dianhai Yu,Yanjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PaddleOCR-VL-1.6，一个紧凑的文档解析模型升级版本。它不盲目扩大训练数据，而是通过区域感知数据优化框架识别并增强之前模型的薄弱区域，并采用基于数据筛选和强化学习的分阶段后训练策略，在OmniDocBench v1.6上达到96.33%的SOTA分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PaddleOCR-VL-1.5虽已建立0.9B的强基线，但残留错误集中在模型行为不稳定、数据覆盖稀疏或监督信号不可靠的区域。论文旨在不盲目扩大训练语料的情况下，通过针对性优化这些薄弱区域来提升性能。

Method: 1) 区域感知数据优化框架：识别前模型的薄弱区域，进行针对性增强并改进监督信号的可靠性。2) 渐进式后训练策略：基于精心筛选的数据和强化学习，分阶段优化以提升模型性能。

Result: 在OmniDocBench v1.6上达到96.33%的新SOTA分数，展现出与顶级VLM的强竞争力。

Conclusion: PaddleOCR-VL-1.6提供了一种实用的后训练方案，在不扩大模型规模的情况下实现了性能突破，为PaddleOCR-VL系列提供了有效的优化范式。

Abstract: We introduce PaddleOCR-VL-1.6, an upgraded compact document parsing model built upon PaddleOCR-VL-1.5. Although PaddleOCR-VL-1.5 establishes a strong 0.9B baseline, its remaining errors concentrate in under-optimized regions where model behavior is unstable, data coverage is sparse, or supervision is unreliable. Rather than expanding the training corpus indiscriminately, PaddleOCR-VL-1.6 introduces a region-aware data optimization framework that identifies weak regions from the previous model, applies targeted enhancement to these regions, and improves the reliability of supervision signals. It further adopts a progressive post-training recipe based on curated data selection and reinforcement learning, pushing model performance to a higher level through staged optimization. PaddleOCR-VL-1.6 achieves a new state-of-the-art score of 96.33% on OmniDocBench v1.6, demonstrates strong competitiveness against top-tier VLMs, and provides a practical post-training recipe for the PaddleOCR-VL series.

</details>


### [45] [VistaHop: Benchmarking Multi-hop Visual Reasoning for Visual DeepSearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03273)
*Hang He,Chuhuai Yue,Chengqi Dong,Chengcheng Wan,Ting Su,Haiying Sun,Jiajun Chai,Xiaohan Wang,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VistaHop基准测试和VistaArena评估环境，用于评估多模态大推理模型在视觉深度搜索中的多跳视觉推理能力。实验表明，现有最优模型仅达到24.31% Pass@1，揭示出视觉定位、证据回溯、长链推理和多锚点信息融合等方面的持续局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要聚焦于单步视觉理解或静态图文问答，缺乏对迭代图像检查、视觉锚点定位和多跳证据整合能力的评估，无法有效衡量模型在视觉深度搜索场景中的表现。

Method: 构建VistaHop基准（300张高分辨率图像、25个视觉搜索场景、350个多跳QA任务），要求模型从视觉锚点出发追踪证据链或融合多条图像推理路径；同时开发VistaArena统一评估环境，支持文本搜索、图像搜索、图像裁剪和基于证据的答案验证等工具增强推理。

Result: 对7种代表性多模态大推理模型进行测试，最优模型SenseNova-MARS-32B仅达到24.31% Pass@1，表现远未令人满意。

Conclusion: 当前模型在视觉定位、证据回溯、长链推理和多锚点信息融合方面存在持续局限性，亟需更强的基准和训练方法来推动视觉深度搜索的发展。

Abstract: Visual DeepSearch requires multimodal large reasoning model (MLRM) agents to answer complex visual queries by repeatedly inspecting image regions, grounding intermediate reasoning in visual evidence, and connecting fine-grained clues across long reasoning chains. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on single-step visual understanding or static image-question answering, offering limited evaluation of iterative image inspection, visual-anchor grounding, and multi-hop evidence integration. In this work, we introduce VistaHop, a benchmark for evaluating vision-centric search and multi-hop visual reasoning in Visual DeepSearch. VistaHop contains 300 high-resolution images, 25 visual search scenarios, and 350 multi-hop QA tasks that require models to follow evidence chains from visual anchors or fuse information across multiple image-grounded reasoning paths. We further develop VistaArena, a unified evaluation environment that supports tool-augmented reasoning with text search, image search, image cropping, and evidence-based answer validation. Experiments on seven representative MLRMs show that current models remain far from solving VistaHop: the best model, SenseNova-MARS-32B, achieves only 24.31% Pass@1. These results reveal persistent limitations in visual grounding, evidence revisiting, long-chain reasoning, and multi-anchor information fusion, highlighting the need for stronger benchmarks and training methods for Visual DeepSearch.

</details>


### [46] [BA-T: An Iterative Transformer for Two-View Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03287)
*Ganlin Zhang,Weirong Chen,Daniel Cremers,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BA-T，一种受经典束调整启发的迭代Transformer，通过轻量级可重复层在隐式token空间中实现结构化的几何优化，在仅用16%解码器参数的情况下达到甚至超越大模型的3D重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建模型依赖深层跨视角注意力解码器，缺乏结构化几何优化机制，导致多视角一致性差。

Method: 受经典束调整（BA）启发，设计BA-T迭代Transformer，将BA的结构化更新作为可重复层在隐式token空间中实现，通过单一轻量层基于潜在残差逐步优化姿态和重建结果。

Result: BA-T逐步提升姿态和重建精度，跨视角一致性优于传统解码器，仅用16%解码器参数即匹配或超越大模型性能。

Conclusion: BA-T提供了一种紧凑、高效、结构化的替代方案，可在轻量架构中实现精确的3D重建。

Abstract: Feed-forward models for 3D reconstruction have achieved strong performance using deep cross-view attention to exchange information across images. However, these approaches often depend on heavy decoder stacks and lack a structured mechanism for geometry refinement, resulting in poor multi-view consistency. We address this by drawing inspiration from classical bundle adjustment (BA), which can be viewed as an iterative information propagation process between poses and local geometry. Inspired by BA, we propose BA-T, an iterative Transformer that implements BA-style structured updates as a repeatable layer in implicit token space. Instead of relying on deep attention stacks, BA-T refines predictions based on latent residual by a single lightweight layer. Experiments demonstrate that BA-T progressively improves pose and reconstruction accuracy across iterations, achieves stronger cross-view consistency than conventional decoders, and matches or surpasses substantially larger models while using only 16% of their decoder parameters. BA-T provides a compact, efficient, and structural alternative to depth-heavy attention, enabling accurate 3D reconstruction within a lightweight architecture. The code will be made publicly at https://github.com/zhangganlin/BA-T.

</details>


### [47] [TASE: Truncation-Aware Semantic Embeddings for 3D Scene Understanding and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03314)
*Tim-Felix Faasch,Jochen Kall,Lucas Nunes,Jens Behley,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TASE通过将预训练的2D语义特征投影到截断感知的嵌入空间，实现了灵活可控的3D场景编辑，支持文本驱动的编辑并允许不同程度的修改强度，在大几何修改场景下显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真语义3D场景表示对机器人、自动驾驶和仿真至关重要，但现有方法对可控编辑的支持有限，缺乏灵活且精细的编辑控制能力。

Method: 1) 将预训练2D语义特征投影到截断感知嵌入空间，渐进减少特征通道获得抽象语义，保留更多通道保持细节；2) 使用尺度和平移等变损失改进多视角一致性；3) 提出微调阶段编辑扩散模型以缓解几何变化引起的伪影。

Result: 在3D场景编辑任务上表现具有竞争力，特别是在涉及大几何修改的编辑中显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: TASE提供了显式的编辑强度控制，允许比先前方法更大幅度的场景修改，同时通过微调策略减轻几何变化带来的伪影问题。

Abstract: High-fidelity semantic 3D scene representations are crucial for numerous applications, including robotics, autonomous driving, and simulation. Beyond this, the ability to edit such representations enables developers to adapt these applications more easily to specific target scenarios. Current approaches provide limited support for controllable editing. We introduce TASE, a method that projects pretrained 2D semantic features into a truncation-aware embedding space to enable flexible 3D scene editing. Our method explicitly optimizes a feature space in which progressively reducing feature channels yields increasingly abstract semantic representations, while retaining more channels preserves fine-grained detail. Additionally, we improve multi-view consistency of the features using a scale- and translation-equivariance loss. The resulting truncation-aware embedding space enables text-driven edits to 3D scenes, providing explicit control over how strongly edits adhere to the original scene content and allowing more substantial modifications than prior methods. Moreover, we propose a finetuning stage for the editing diffusion model to mitigate artifacts caused by geometric changes. Experimental results demonstrate competitive performance in 3D scene editing, substantially outperforming prior methods on edits involving large geometric modifications.

</details>


### [48] [Cross-Modality Feature Fusion Based on Structured State Space Duality for Multimodal Image Registration Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03341)
*Zhikang Li,Yan Wu,Xin Hu,Yi Dai,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RegNetMamba-2，一种基于结构化状态空间对偶（SSD）的多模态图像配准算法，通过粗到细的匹配流程提取局部和全局结构特征，在VIS-SAR、VIS-IR和VIS-NIR数据集上实现了性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像配准的核心挑战在于共享结构信息的提取。相比Transformer，SSD在训练和推理过程中能以更高效率提取全局结构特征，因此作者受此启发提出新算法。

Method: 1) 在三个不同尺度应用SSD进行多模态特征提取，并通过特征缩放函数增强前景边缘和结构信息；2) 提出基于SSD的跨模态特征融合模型，包含跨模态特征交互（CMI）模块和多尺度特征融合（MSF）模块；3) 采用粗到细策略，收集1/8尺度和1/2尺度的特征计算匹配概率分数，建立像素级对应匹配。

Result: 在VIS-SAR（OSDataset）、VIS-IR（LGHD/RoadSence）和VIS-NIR（RGB-NIR sense）三个数据集上，与基于深度学习的SOTA算法相比，RegNetMamba-2在性能和效率方面均取得了良好效果。

Conclusion: RegNetMamba-2通过引入SSD有效解决了多模态图像配准中的共享结构提取问题，在多个多模态数据集上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: In multi-modal image registration, the primary challenge lies in shared structural information extraction. Compared to Transformers, Structured State Space Duality (SSD) offers greater global structural feature extraction with higher efficiency during training and inference. Inspired by these advantages, we propose a novel algorithm for multi-modal image registration, named RegNetMamba-2. Our algorithm incorporates SSD into coarse-to-fine matching process to extract local and global structural features effectively. Firstly, SSD is applied in three different scales for multi-modal feature extraction in our network. To strengthen local representation, we pay more attention on foreground edge and structural information by feature scaling function of SSD. Secondly, for shared feature extraction of input images and multi-modal feature fusion in all scales, we propose cross-modality feature fusion model based on SSD, consisting of Cross-Modality feature Interaction (CMI) module and Multi-Scale feature Fusion (MSF) module. CMI module is designed for cross-modality feature extraction of each scale by SSD in cross form. MSF module is designed to employ a progressive upward fusion in feature-level to obtain fine features, consisting of multi-modal features in all scales. Following coarse-to-fine, the features in 1/8 scale from CMI and 1/2 scale from MSF are collected to calculate matching probability scores. Then we respectively establish matching process by correspondences of pixel-wise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that comparing with state-of-the-art deep-learning based algorithms, RegNetMamba-2 has achieved good effects in both performance and efficiency for multi-modal image registration on the following datasets: VIS-SAR (OSDataset), VIS-IR (LGHD/RoadSence) and VIS-NIR (RGB-NIR sense).

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Semantics: Modeling Factual and Affective Perceptual Experiences from Vision-Language Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03345)
*Youssef Mohamed,Kenneth Ward Church,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P-Topics建模新问题，旨在发现图像在不同文化中的情感感知体验，并设计了PercepT架构（两阶段：形成阶段发现视觉-文本聚类，映射阶段学习图像到聚类的映射），在ArtELingo数据集上显著优于基线方法（轮廓分数0.97 vs 0.37，AUC 0.94 vs 0.77）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对图像跨文化情感感知体验的系统建模，无法同时捕捉图像的客观事实与主观情感两个维度，以及不同文化背景下感知差异。

Method: 提出PercepT两阶段架构：(1) 形成阶段——通过无监督训练目标发现P-Topics（视觉-文本聚类），并动态选择聚类数量；(2) 映射阶段——通过注意力池化学习P-Topic映射函数，将图像关联到对应聚类。

Result: 在ArtELingo数据集上，PercepT轮廓分数0.97（基线0.37），AUC 0.94（基线0.77），人类评估也证实其能捕获语义上有意义的感知体验。

Conclusion: PercepT在跨文化图像情感感知建模上显著优于现有方法，能够有效发现并映射不同文化背景下的感知体验。

Abstract: We present P-Topics (Perception Topics) modeling, a novel problem for understanding how images are perceived affectively and across cultures. The goal is to (1) discover and model the different perception experiences in a dataset of images and captions, where each experience is defined by an objective factual and a subjective affective aspect, and (2) associate images to their relevant perception experiences. We introduce **PercepT** (**Percep**tion topic **T**ransformer), a two-stage architecture that tackles P-Topics modeling. In the formation stage, percepT discovers *P-Topics* as visual-textual clusters using an unsupervised training objective, and dynamically selects the number of clusters to match the perceptual richness of the dataset. In the mapping stage, it learns *P-Topic mapping functions* via attention pooling to associate images to their respective clusters. On ArtELingo, PercepT achieves a silhouette score of **0.97** compared to **0.37** from the closest baseline reflecting better perceptual clusters. PercepT also achieves an AUC score of **0.94** compared to **0.77** showing better mapping to perceptual clusters. Human evaluation confirms that PercepT captures semantically meaningful perception experiences and significantly outperforms existing methods. Our implementation will be made public.

</details>


### [50] [SynCred-Bench: Benchmarking Synthetic Credibility in AI-Generated Visual Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03348)
*Junxiao Yang,Minghao Zhang,Xiaoce Wang,Haoran Liu,Shiyao Cui,Hongning Wang,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SYNCRED-Bench基准（600张AI生成 misinformation 图像）和FP450真实图像集，评估发现现有检测系统在5%假阳性率下表现极差：多模态大模型仅10.5%真阳性率，开源AIGC检测器不足5%，商业API达57.6%，人类仅63%。证实"合成可信性"是严重且被低估的视觉 misinformation 挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型可产生具备逼真文本和布局的视觉内容，构成名为"合成可信性"（synthetic credibility）的新型 misinformation 威胁，而现有检测系统对此缺乏有效应对。

Method: 构建SYNCRED-Bench（600张AI生成 misinformation 图像，覆盖6种可信格式类别和7种传播风格）及FP450真实图像负样本集，系统评估15个MLLM、开源AIGC检测器和商业API的性能。

Result: 在5%假阳性率约束下，15个MLLM仅10.5%真阳性率，开源检测器低于5%，商业API达57.6%，人类注释者仅63%真阳性率。

Conclusion: 合成可信性是一个严峻且未被充分探索的视觉 misinformation 挑战，现有检测方法均不可靠，亟需能推理超越表面可信线索的新型检测器。

Abstract: Recent generative models can now produce visual artifacts with realistic embedded text and layouts, creating a new misinformation threat: synthetic credibility. We introduce SYNCRED-Bench, a benchmark of 600 AI-generated misinformation images balanced across six credible-form categories and seven fine-grained circulation styles, together with FP450, a real-image negative set for measuring false positives. Extensive evaluation shows that existing systems remain unreliable: under a 5% false-positive-rate constraint, 15 MLLMs achieve only 10.5% true positive rate (TPR), open-source AIGC detectors achieve less than 5%, and commercial APIs reach 57.6%. Human annotators also struggled to identify synthetic credibility, reaching only 63% TPR. These findings establish synthetic credibility as a severe and underexplored visual misinformation challenge, and provide a benchmark for developing detectors that reason beyond superficial credibility cues.

</details>


### [51] [P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO: Grounding Hallucination in Perceptual Processing via Calibration Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03376)
*Ruipeng Zhang,Zhihao Li,Haozhang Yuan,C. L. Philip Chen,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P²-DPO方法，通过让模型自己生成并学习偏好对，解决LVLM中的幻觉问题，特别针对感知瓶颈和视觉鲁棒性不足，在无需昂贵人工反馈的条件下超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法未针对性解决LVLM中的感知瓶颈（attention区域）和视觉鲁棒性（图像退化）问题，且偏好对通常是视觉无关和off-policy的，限制了学习效果。

Method: (1) 构建on-policy偏好对，针对Focus-and-Enhance感知和视觉鲁棒性；(2) 设计Calibration Loss，精确对齐视觉信号与文本的因果生成。

Result: 在同等训练数据和成本下，P²-DPO超越依赖昂贵人工反馈的强基线；在Attention Region Fidelity和图像退化场景评估中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 通过让模型从自身生成on-policy偏好对进行学习，能有效缓解感知瓶颈和视觉鲁棒性不足问题，避免视觉无关和off-policy数据带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Hallucination has recently garnered significant research attention in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aims to learn directly from the corrected preferences provided by humans, thereby addressing the hallucination issue. Despite its success, this paradigm has yet to specifically target the perceptual bottleneck in attended regions or address insufficient Visual Robustness against image degradation. Furthermore, existing preference pairs are often vision-agnostic and their inherently off-policy nature limits their effectiveness in guiding model learning. To address these challenges, we propose Perceptual Processing Direct Preference Optimization (P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO), a novel training paradigm in which the model generates and learns from its own preference pairs, thereby directly addressing the identified visual bottlenecks while inherently avoiding the issues of vision-agnostic and off-policy data. It introduces: (1) an on-policy preference pairs construction method targeting Focus-and-Enhance perception and Visual Robustness, and (2) a well-designed Calibration Loss to precisely align visual signals with the causal generation of text. Experimental results demonstrate that with a comparable amount of training data and cost, P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO outperforms strong baselines that rely on costly human feedback on benchmarks. Furthermore, evaluations on Attention Region Fidelity (ARF) and image degradation scenarios validate the effectiveness of P\textsuperscript{2}-DPO in addressing perceptual bottleneck in attended regions and improving Visual Robustness against degraded inputs.

</details>


### [52] [Towards Characterizing Scientific Image Utility and Upgradability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03401)
*WenZhe Li,Qihang Yan,Liang Chen,Junying Wang,Farong Wen,Yijin Guo,Chunyi Li,Zicheng Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIU²A框架，从Utility（错误检测+可修复性）和Upgradability（修复质量）两个维度评估科学图像，并构建了包含四种错误类型的基准数据集。实验表明现有多模态系统在科学图像评估和修正方面存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图像作为研究交流的关键证据，正面临AI生成内容带来的前所未有威胁——引入细微但影响深远的错误。现有评估范式存在不足：感知质量指标与科学有效性相关性差，语言模型缺乏领域特定验证能力。

Method: 1. 提出SIU²A框架，定义Utility（错误检测+修正可行性）和Upgradability（修正质量）两个维度；2. 将科学图像错误分为四类：细节扭曲、不完整、虚假内容、实体混淆；3. 构建SIU²A-Benchmark数据集（含专家标注）；4. 设计两阶段评估协议：Utility阶段评估错误检测和修复指令生成，Upgradability阶段评估修正是否忠实恢复科学有效性。

Result: 实验揭示当前多模态系统在科学错误评估和忠实修正方面均存在显著局限，暴露了视觉感知与科学可用性之间的根本差距。

Conclusion: 现有AI系统无法可靠地评估和修复科学图像中的错误，SIU²A框架为弥合这一差距提供了系统性评估方法论。

Abstract: Scientific images function as critical evidence in research communication, yet their integrity faces unprecedented threats from AI-generated content that introduces subtle but consequential errors. Existing evaluation paradigms prove inadequate: perceptual quality metrics poorly correlate with scientific validity, while language models lack domain-specific verification capabilities. To address this gap, we propose the \textbf{S}cientific \textbf{I}mage \textbf{U}tility and \textbf{U}pgradability \textbf{A}ssessment (\textbf{SIU$^2$A}) framework, which introduces two complementary dimensions for scientific image evaluation. \textbf{Utility} encompasses \textit{error detection} (identifying scientific inaccuracies) and \textit{correction feasibility} (assessing whether errors can be reliably repaired). \textbf{Upgradability} measures the quality of correction. We categorize scientific image corruption into four fundamental types: Detail Distortion, Incompleteness, False Content, and Entity Confusion. Based on this taxonomy, we construct SIU$^2$A-Benchmark, a dataset with expert annotations for error identification and repair. The framework implements a two-stage evaluation protocol: the \textit{Utility} stage evaluates error detection capability and repair instruction generation, while the \textit{Upgradability} stage assesses whether corrections faithfully restore scientific validity without compromising existing accurate information. Experiments reveal that current multimodal systems exhibit significant limitations in both scientific error assessment and faithful correction, exposing a fundamental gap between visual perception and scientific usability.

</details>


### [53] [Mamba-Enhanced Implicit Motion Learning for Audio-Driven Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03402)
*Xuan Wei,Jiahui Chen,Kaiheng Li,Mingyu Shao,Qingqi Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种从单张静态图像和音频生成逼真人运动视频的两阶段隐式运动框架，第一阶段使用区域感知注意力机制建模潜在运动特征，第二阶段采用Mamba增强扩散模型预测运动，在380小时高质量数据集上训练，达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于关键点的方法难以捕捉细微运动动态，需要更灵活的框架来生成真实且时间连贯的人体运动视频。

Method: 两阶段流水线：1）集成外观先验和层次深度线索的区域感知注意力机制，建模潜在运动特征；2）Mamba增强的扩散模型，从音频和源图像直接预测运动特征，实现无监督学习细粒度运动模式。

Result: 在多个公开基准和自收集数据上，在准确性、自然性和时间连贯性方面均优于先前方法，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 提出的解耦架构增强了生成灵活性和效率，380小时高质量数据集训练有效支撑了模型性能提升。

Abstract: Audio-driven human motion video generation aims to synthesize realistic and temporally coherent human animations from a single static image, with applications in talking-head synthesis, co-speech gesture generation, and dynamic presentations. Moving beyond conventional keypoint-based methods that often struggle to capture subtle motion dynamics, We propose a novel implicit-motion framework for generating realistic and temporally coherent human motion videos from a single static image and audio. Our approach uses a two-stage pipeline that decouples motion prediction from rendering. The first stage integrates appearance priors and hierarchical depth cues into a region-aware attention mechanism to model latent motion features. The second stage employs a Mamba-enhanced diffusion model to directly predict these features from audio and the source image, enabling unsupervised learning of fine-grained motion patterns. This decoupled architecture enhances flexibility and efficiency. Trained on a new 380-hour high-quality dataset, our method outperforms prior work across multiple public benchmarks and our collected data in accuracy, naturalness, and temporal coherence, setting a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [54] [SAMatcher: Co-Visibility Modeling with Segment Anything for Robust Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03406)
*Xu Pan,Qiyuan Ma,Mingyue Dong,He Chen,Wei Ji,Xianwei Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAMatcher框架，利用SAM模型通过共可见性建模（预测共可见区域掩码和边界框）来改进特征匹配，在视角和尺度变化大的场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习方法在像素或patch层面进行特征匹配，缺乏对多视图间共可见区域的显式建模，导致大视角和尺度变化下匹配性能不足。

Method: 基于SAM构建对称跨视图交互机制，先预测共可见区域掩码和边界框作为结构化先验，再统一优化掩码学习、边界框回归和掩码-框一致性约束。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准上显著优于现有匹配管线，尤其在大视角和尺度变化下表现突出。

Conclusion: 证明原用于单目分割的基础模型（SAM）可通过显式共可见性建模有效扩展到多视图对应推理，为图像匹配的结构化表示学习提供了新视角。

Abstract: Reliable correspondence estimation is a fundamental problem in image processing, underpinning applications such as Structure from Motion, visual localization, and image registration. Existing learning-based methods have significantly improved local feature representations, yet most still operate at the pixel or patch level and lack explicit modeling of regions that are jointly visible across views. We propose SAMatcher, a feature matching framework that formulates correspondence estimation through co-visibility modeling. Instead of directly matching local features, SAMatcher first predicts co-visible region masks and bounding boxes as structured priors for correspondence estimation. Built upon the Segment Anything Model (SAM), it introduces a symmetric cross-view interaction mechanism that enables bidirectional feature exchange and cross-view semantic alignment. We further develop a unified supervision scheme that jointly optimizes mask prediction and box localization through mask learning, box regression, and mask-box consistency constraints. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate substantial improvements over existing matching pipelines, particularly under large viewpoint and scale variations. Our results show that foundation models originally designed for monocular segmentation can be effectively extended to multi-view correspondence reasoning through explicit co-visibility modeling, offering a new perspective on structured representation learning for image matching. Code and project page: https://xupan.top/Projects/samatcher

</details>


### [55] [Enginuity: A Dataset and Benchmark for Vision-Language Understanding of Engineering Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03410)
*Abhishek Kumar,Isha Motiyani,Tilak Kasturi,Ethan Seefried,Prahitha Movva,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Enginuity，首个用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在工程图纸上能力的公开数据集和基准。通过两个任务（结构化零件表提取和自由形式视觉问答）评估了四个前沿VLM，发现模型在零件识别与描述准确性之间存在系统性差距，并揭示了传统的token重叠指标在技术描述上低估模型能力2-6倍的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程图纸在服务、维修和设计工作流中至关重要，但现有基准主要关注流程图、科学图表或商业文档，缺乏专门评估VLM在复杂工程图纸上能力的公开基准。

Method: 基于美国军用服务和维修手册构建语料库，定义了两个任务：结构化零件表提取（Task 1）和自由形式视觉问答（Task 2）。在零样本和链式思维提示下评估了GPT-5.2 Chat、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemma 4和Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct四个模型。

Result: Task 1中Recall@all达到0.61-0.87，但Token F1pen仅为0.03-0.18，表明零件识别与描述准确性之间存在系统性差距。Task 2揭示了所有模型都存在一致的事实推理差距。token重叠指标相比语义相似度将模型能力低估了2-6倍。

Conclusion: 现有的token重叠指标不适合评估技术性描述，建议使用LLM-as-judge校准进行领域特定评估。发布了数据集、标注、评估框架和样本输出以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Engineering diagrams pose a distinct challenge for vision-language models: unlike natural images or general documents, they encode information through dense spatial layouts, domain-specific symbols, and cross-references between visual callouts and structured parts tables. Despite their centrality to service, repair, and design workflows, there is no public benchmark for measuring VLM capabilities in this domain; existing datasets primarily focus on flowcharts, scientific figures, or business documents. To address this gap, we introduce Enginuity, the first open dataset and benchmark for evaluating VLMs on complex engineering diagrams. We define two tasks over a corpus of U.S. military service and repair manuals: structured parts-table extraction (Task 1) and free-form visual diagram question answering (VQA)(Task 2) for benchmarking. We evaluate four frontier VLMs (GPT-5.2 Chat, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemma 4, Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct) under zero-shot and chain-of-thought prompting. On Task 1, models reach Recall@all of 0.61-0.87 but Token F1pen of only 0.03-0.18, exposing a systematic gap between part identification and description fidelity. Task 2 reveals a consistent factual-reasoning gap across all models. A supporting analysis shows that token-overlap metrics under-report model capability on technical descriptions by 2-6x relative to semantic similarity, motivating LLM-as-judge calibration for domain-specific evaluation. We release the dataset, annotations, evaluation harness, and per-sample model outputs to support a reproducible study of VLM capability on engineering content.

</details>


### [56] [A unified multi-task framework enables interpretable chest radiograph analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03417)
*Lijian Xu,Ziyu Ni,Xinglong Liu,Xiaosong Wang,Hongsheng Li,Shaoting Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMT-CXR框架，通过可解释的多任务Transformer模拟放射科医生工作流程，包含疾病识别、属性表征、证据整合报告生成三个阶段，在10个CXR基准上表现优异，66%的AI生成报告在诊断清晰度上媲美或超越原始临床报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像黑箱系统局限于孤立任务，忽视了临床诊断作为多任务过程的信任敏感特性，缺乏可解释性和可追溯的诊断路径。

Method: 采用统一Transformer架构，通过医学领域指令微调，顺序执行四个临床任务：多标签疾病分类、病灶定位、解剖分割和放射报告生成，构建三个证据驱动阶段（疾病识别→属性表征→证据整合报告生成）。

Result: 在10个CXR基准上直接推理和微调设置均表现优异；160份历史报告的盲评中，三位放射科医生认为66%的AI生成报告在诊断清晰度上可比或优于原始临床报告。

Conclusion: 通过建立从解剖发现到结论的可追溯诊断路径，弥合了AI技术指标与临床实用性之间的差距，推动了可信赖AI系统在医学影像中的应用。

Abstract: While multimodal deep learning has advanced medical imaging analysis, existing black-box systems \textcolor{black}{may remain confined to isolated tasks, often overlooking} the trust-sensitive nature of clinical diagnosis as a multi-task process. We propose IMT-CXR (Interpretable Multi-task Transformer for Chest X-ray Analysis), a framework that emulates radiologists' diagnostic workflow through three evidence-driven stages: 1) Disease recognition; 2) Attribute characterization (e.g., size, location, severity quantification); 3) Evidence-integrated report generation with traceable decision pathways. The framework employs a unified transformer architecture optimized via medical-domain instruction tuning, sequentially executing four clinical tasks: multi-label disease classification, lesion localization, anatomical segmentation, and radiology report generation. Experimental validation demonstrates competitive performance on ten CXR benchmarks under direct inference and fine-tuning settings. In a blinded evaluation of 160 historical reports from four medical centers, three radiologists rated 66\% of AI-generated reports as comparable to or surpassing original clinical reports in diagnostic clarity, highlighting the framework's translational potential. By establishing traceable diagnostic pathways from anatomical findings to conclusions, this work bridges the gap between AI technical metrics and clinical utility, advancing trustworthy AI systems in medical imaging.

</details>


### [57] [IDO: Incongruity-aware Distribution Optimization for Multimodal Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03418)
*Hengyang Zhou,Rongman Hong,Yuxuan Zhou,Jing Wang,Zhaoyan Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于不一致性感知分布优化的多模态假新闻检测方法，通过显式建模事实不一致性和模态不一致性来提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注跨模态一致性，但未能显式建模多模态假新闻中常见的语义不一致性，导致检测效果受限。

Method: 1）事实不一致性：采用通道级重加权策略获取语义判别性嵌入，并用高斯分布建模不确定性；2）模态不一致性：利用不一致性对比学习学习跨模态语义信息。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法达到了最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: 显式建模语义不一致性是提升多模态假新闻检测效果的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal fake news detection aims to identify the authenticity of news. Existing multimodal fake news detection methods mainly focus on cross-modal consistency, but often fail to explicitly model the semantic incongruity that characterizes deceptive multimodal content. However, misinformation often contains semantic information incongruity with the facts. To address these challenges, we propose Incongruity-aware Distribution Optimization (IDO) to improve the performance of fake news detection from the perspectives of factual incongruity and modality incongruity. For factual incongruity, we introduce a channel-wise reweighting strategy to obtain semantically discriminative embeddings and utilize gaussian distribution to model the uncertain correlation caused by factual incongruity. For modality incongruity, we utilize incongruity contrastive learning to learn cross-modal semantic information. Experiments demonstrate that IDO achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [58] [PHAF-Personalized Hand Avatars in a Flash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03420)
*Meghana Shankar,Akanxit Upadhyay,Anmol Namdev,Green Rosh KS,Pawan Prasad BH*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PHAF提出了一种从仅两张手部图像（背侧和掌侧）快速生成个性化逼真手部化身的方法，相比传统优化方法速度快30倍，适用于AR/VR等边缘设备实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部化身方法依赖慢速优化，无法在边缘设备上实时生成个性化纹理，限制了AR/VR等实际应用场景的部署。

Method: 结合语义引导网格对齐与稠密纹理提取以传递高频细节，采用基于视图的修复网络优化纹理连续性，利用参数化手部模型支持精准关节运动。

Result: 在视觉保真度上与现有方法相当，但纹理生成时间缩短30倍，支持实时多视角渲染与推理。

Conclusion: PHAF实现了从两张图像快速生成高质量个性化手部化身，显著提升效率，为AR/VR等实时应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present PHAF-Personalized Hand Avatars in a Flash, a personalized photo-realistic hand avatar which provides high quality multi-view renders from just two images (dorsal and palmar views).Unlike slow optimization-based techniques, PHAF generates fast personalized textures for real-time deployment on edge devices. Our approach combines semantic guided mesh alignment and densified texture extraction to transfer high-frequency details efficiently. A view-based inpainting network refines textures ensuring smooth, continuous appearance. PHAF generalizes to novel viewpoints and leverages a parametric hand model for accurate articulations, making it compatible with standard graphics engines. Experiments show it is comparable to existing methods in visual fidelity while drastically reducing texture generation time by 30 times, enabling practical AR/VR applications.

</details>


### [59] [PRISM: Synergizing Vision Foundation Models via Self-organized Expert Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03444)
*Ying Tang,Dong Li,Youjia Zhang,Zikai Song,Junqing Yu,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一个双流MoE框架，通过两阶段范式（知识解构+动态重组）将多种视觉基础模型（VFM）的优势整合到单一高效模型中，在PASCAL-Context和NYUD-v2数据集上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将多种VFM整合到单一模型时存在负迁移问题（特征冲突），因为不同VFM的表示空间存在差异，直接蒸馏会导致相互干扰。

Method: 提出PRISM双流MoE框架：第一阶段（知识解构）使用教师条件路由器引导不同专家专注于不同的表示子空间以减少干扰；第二阶段（动态重组）路由器学习为下游任务动态组装专家，形成定制化的计算路径。

Result: 在PASCAL-Context和NYUD-v2基准上达到新的SOTA，验证了稀疏的涌现式专家特化是整合多样化视觉知识的可扩展方法。

Conclusion: 通过模块化特化和稀疏激活的MoE范式，可以有效解决多源视觉知识整合中的负迁移问题，为构建统一的视觉基础模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unifying the complementary strengths of diverse Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) into a single efficient model is highly desirable but challenged by the negative transfer inherent in monolithic distillation. To address these feature conflicts, we introduce \textbf{PRISM}, a novel dual-stream Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework that synergizes VFMs via modular specialization. We propose a two-stage paradigm: (1) expertise deconstruction, where a teacher-conditional router guides experts to specialize in distinct representational subspaces to mitigate interference, followed by (2) dynamic recomposition, where the router learns to assemble these experts into tailored computational pathways for downstream tasks. Experiments on PASCAL-Context and NYUD-v2 show that \textbf{PRISM} establishes a new state of the art, validating that sparse, emergent specialization is a scalable approach for integrating diverse visual knowledge.

</details>


### [60] [From 3D Perception to Safety Reasoning: A Graph-Based Framework for Real-Time Underground Mine Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03460)
*Pasindu Ranasinghe,Simit Raval,Dibyayan Patra,Bikram Banerjee,Ismet Canbulat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合3D语义感知、不确定性异常检测、规则检查、LLM推理和GraphRAG记忆分析的井下煤矿连续监控框架，在115个危险场景中实现了93%的推理覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监控系统缺乏3D场景理解和上下文记忆，难以识别复杂或演变的危险（如设备接近违规、结构失稳、盲区遮挡），尤其是在地下煤矿这种共享、受限、光照不良的空间中。

Method: 框架将彩色3D点云转化为结构化的安全推理输出，融合3D语义感知、不确定性驱动的异常检测、规则推理、设备端LLM推理和GraphRAG记忆分析；场景图和时间图作为显式知识结构连接各推理阶段；通过真实巷道扫描、控制物体放置和高保真长壁仿真生成多样数据，结合自监督预训练提升分割性能。

Result: 感知模型在30 FPS下达92.7%准确率，内存占用低；115个危险场景中，规则检查覆盖57%，加入LLM推理后达76%，结合记忆推理后达93%；不确定性异常信号支持识别未预定义的分布外危险。

Conclusion: 基于图的知识表示结合3D感知与分层安全推理，为井下煤矿监控的智能决策支持提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Underground coal mining requires personnel and heavy equipment to operate within shared, confined, and poorly illuminated spaces where hazards such as equipment proximity violations, structural instabilities, and occluded blind spots are difficult to anticipate. Conventional monitoring systems, including fixed cameras and rule-based proximity alerts, can detect predefined events but lack the 3D scene understanding and contextual memory needed to identify complex or evolving hazards. This paper presents a continuous monitoring framework that converts colourised 3D point clouds into structured and traceable safety reasoning outputs. The framework combines 3D semantic perception, uncertainty-based anomaly detection, rule-based hazard checks, on-device LLM reasoning, and GraphRAG -based memory analysis to identify immediate hazards and interpret longer-term safety patterns. Scene and temporal graphs serve as the explicit knowledge structure, linking perception outputs across reasoning stages. To overcome the scarcity of labeled underground data, real roadway scans, controlled object placement, and high-fidelity longwall simulation were combined to generate diverse hazard scenarios, while self-supervised pretraining improved segmentation from limited annotations. The perception model achieved 92.7% accuracy at 30 FPS with low memory usage. Across 115 hazard scenarios, rule-based checks achieved 57% coverage, increasing to 76% with contextual LLM reasoning and 93% with memory-based reasoning using historical records. Qualitative results show uncertainty-derived anomaly signals support the interpretation of out-of-distribution hazards beyond predefined classes. Overall, graph-based knowledge representation combined with 3D perception and layered safety reasoning provides a practical foundation for intelligent decision support in underground mine monitoring.

</details>


### [61] [Mixed-Modality Dual Face-Hair Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03470)
*Quoc-Anh Bui-Huynh,Mai-Tuyen Lam,Dai-Anh-Tuan Nguyen,Thanh Duc Ngo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向混合模态的 Dual Face-Hair Retrieval (DFHR) 任务，构建了首个基准 DFHR-Bench，并提出了统一框架 MFHC，实现了跨模态的身份与发型属性解耦与组合检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像检索任务无法同时处理来自不同模态（图像 vs 文本）的身份和发型属性，缺乏跨组件推理、局部特征解耦和跨模态语义对齐的能力。

Method: 构建 DFHR-Bench 基准（18万+三元组），提出 MFHC 框架，通过 token injection 和多视角监督实现解耦的身份与发型嵌入融合。

Result: 建立了首个混合模态人脸-发型检索基准 DFHR-Bench；提出的 MFHC 框架在该任务上取得了有效结果。

Conclusion: DFHR 和 DFHR-Bench 为身份感知、属性可控的跨模态视觉检索建立了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Dual Face-Hair Retrieval (DFHR), a new mixed-modality dual-reference task in image retrieval where a query consists of a face image specifying identity and a hairstyle reference expressed as either an image or text. Unlike prior retrieval settings, DFHR requires cross-component reasoning between two semantically independent attributes -- identity and hairstyle -- originating from heterogeneous modalities. This formulation demands localized feature disentanglement, cross-modal semantic alignment, and mixed-modality composition within a unified embedding space. We construct DFHR-Bench, the first benchmark for mixed-modality face-hair retrieval, comprising over 180K annotated triplets across dual-image and image-text settings, built via a multi-stage annotation protocol ensuring semantic and identity integrity. We further propose MFHC (Multimodal Face-Hair Combiner), a unified framework that fuses disentangled identity and hairstyle embeddings through token injection and multi-view supervision. DFHR and DFHR-Bench together establish a new paradigm for identity-aware, attribute-controllable visual retrieval across modalities.

</details>


### [62] [PersistGS: Differentiable Physics for Object Permanence in 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03479)
*Adrian Ramlal,John S. Zelek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PersistGS将可微刚体仿真与3D高斯泼溅结合，解决动态场景中物体被完全遮挡时的重建问题，通过物理轨迹预测实现物体恒存性，在遮挡期间保持高质量渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3DGS方法在物体被完全遮挡时失去光度监督信号，导致高斯退化；现有基于生成先验的方法只追求视觉合理性而非物理正确性。

Method: 场景分解为逐物体高斯和碰撞网格；通过可微仿真从遮挡前轨迹估计摩擦和速度；利用SE(3)轨迹在遮挡期间定位物体高斯；引入质心轮廓损失分离位置梯度与外观噪声。

Result: 轨迹误差降低40%（相比光度监督）；PSNR比恒速外推高2.46dB；与真实轨迹上限仅差0.19dB。

Conclusion: 融合物理仿真与神经渲染是处理遮挡问题的有效路径，在保持物理正确性的同时实现了高质量重建。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods reconstruct time-varying scenes from synchronized multi-camera video using photometric supervision. When a moving object becomes fully occluded from all training cameras, this supervision vanishes: the Gaussians representing it receive no gradient signal and degrade. Existing approaches to incomplete observations in neural reconstruction rely on learned generative priors that prioritize visual plausibility over physical correctness.
  We propose $\textbf{PersistGS}$, a method that restores object permanence during occlusion by coupling differentiable rigid body simulation with 3D Gaussian Splatting. Our approach decomposes the scene into per-object Gaussians and collision meshes, estimates friction and velocity from the observed pre-occlusion trajectory via differentiable simulation, and uses the resulting SE(3) trajectory to position object Gaussians throughout the occlusion period. Because the predicted trajectory satisfies the governing equations of rigid body dynamics, it faithfully captures contact events (bounces, friction-based deceleration, direction changes) that kinematic extrapolation cannot model. We introduce a centroid silhouette loss that isolates positional gradients from appearance noise, yielding 40% lower trajectory error than photometric supervision. We evaluate using cameras withheld from training that observe the object during its occlusion. Experiments on synthetic scenes show that PersistGS outperforms constant velocity extrapolation by +2.46dB PSNR and comes within 0.19dB of a ground-truth trajectory upper bound.

</details>


### [63] [Learned Non-Maximum Suppression for 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03568)
*Timo Osterburg,Stefan Schütte,Torsten Bertram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于学习的后处理模块（D2D-Rescore和GossipNet3D），用于替代LiDAR 3D检测中传统的启发式NMS，通过检测间的关系建模提升检测性能，尤其对小众类别效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMS作为后处理步骤存在启发式缺陷，难以处理密集重叠的3D检测框，导致小目标和稀有类别性能不佳，因此需要更智能的、基于关系学习的过滤方法。

Method: 提出两种学习型过滤模块：1) D2D-Rescore利用Transformer的检测间注意力机制；2) GossipNet3D将2D GossipNet扩展到3D，在鸟瞰图上进行局部消息传递。同时引入与nuScenes评估协议一致的度量感知匹配策略。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，两个方法相比CircleNMS均提升了mAP、NDS和真阳性质量，对小众类别的改进尤为明显，且计算开销极低。

Conclusion: 基于学习的检测级过滤可以在不修改基础检测网络的前提下，有效提升3D检测器的可靠性，为启发式抑制提供了有原则的替代方案。

Abstract: Post-processing is a critical stage in LiDAR-based 3D object detection, where dense and overlapping proposals must be filtered for compact and reliable perception. This work introduces two learned filtering modules that replace heuristic non-maximum suppression (NMS) by leveraging relations among detections. D2D-Rescore employs transformer-based detection-to-detection (D2D) attention, while GossipNet3D adapts the 2D GossipNet concept to 3D through localized message passing in bird's-eye view. A metric-aware matching strategy aligned with the nuScenes evaluation protocol ensures consistent training and validation behavior, improving overall detection performance. Both approaches improve mean average precision (mAP), nuScenes detection score (NDS), and true positive quality compared to CircleNMS, particularly for small and infrequent classes, while adding minimal computational overhead. These results demonstrate that learned, detection-level filtering can enhance 3D detector reliability without modifying the base network, offering a principled alternative to heuristic suppression. Code is available at https://github.com/rst-tu-dortmund/learned-3d-nms .

</details>


### [64] [TrAction: Action Recognition with Sparse Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03490)
*Jan F. Meier,Felix B. Mueller,Alexander Ecker,Timo Lüddecke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏点轨迹（2.5D）的Transformer架构用于动作识别，通过掩码轨迹预训练方法，在仅使用少量密集RGB输入的情况下达到不错性能，并能与现有外观特征模型互补融合提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作识别模型依赖密集RGB视频体积，计算和内存开销大，且容易利用外观和背景的捷径（如根据物体或场景而非动作本身进行预测）。本文旨在探索一种高效且天然避免此类偏差的替代输入模态——稀疏点轨迹。

Method: 1) 提出一个适用于2.5D轨迹的简单Transformer架构（TrAction）；2) 引入掩码轨迹预训练策略，提升下游动作识别性能；3) 将预训练模型与DINOv2、V-JEPA 2等外观特征模型进行融合。

Result: Something-Something V2上达到45% top-1准确率，EPIC-Kitchens-100上达到54%；在时间反转敏感性上超越V-JEPA；与DINOv2融合可将Something-Something V2 top-1提升8.7个百分点，与V-JEPA 2融合提升1.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 轨迹特征与外观特征具有互补性，融合后可显著提升动作识别性能，表明稀疏点轨迹是一种高效且具有竞争力的动作识别输入模态。

Abstract: Modern action recognition models operate on memory- and compute-intensive dense RGB video volumes and frequently exploit appearance and background shortcuts, for example, predicting actions from objects or scenes instead of characteristic motion. We investigate an efficient alternative input modality that is largely free of such biases by construction: sparse point trajectories. To this end, we develop a simple transformer architecture for 2.5D trajectory-based recognition together with a masked-trajectory pretraining, which we show to substantially improve downstream action recognition accuracy. Despite using only a fraction of the dense RGB input, our method reaches 45% top-1 on Something-Something V2 and 54% on EPIC-Kitchens-100, and surpasses V-JEPA on time-reversal sensitivity. More importantly, we find trajectory features to be complementary to state-of-the-art appearance-based features. Fusing our pretrained model with DINOv2 and V-JEPA 2 improves top-1 accuracy on Something-Something V2 by 8.7 and 1.6 points, respectively. Code: https://github.com/ecker-lab/TrAction

</details>


### [65] [UnsOcc: 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction in Unstructured Scene via Rendering Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03581)
*Ye Wu,Ruiqi Song,Baiyong Ding,Nanxin Zeng,Junjie Cheng,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UnsOcc，一种面向非结构化场景（如露天矿）的多模态3D语义占据预测框架，通过RenderFusion双向渲染融合模块和GSRefinement高斯泼溅辅助监督，有效解决了场景稀疏和长尾分布问题，在露天矿和nuScenes数据集上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化场景中不规则障碍物和稀疏布局使传统3D目标检测失效，而现有3D语义占据预测方法在非结构化场景中面临场景稀疏阻碍跨模态融合、长尾分布导致预测性能下降两大挑战。

Method: 提出UnsOcc框架，包含两个核心模块：1) RenderFusion，基于渲染的融合模块，通过双向渲染监督增强跨模态特征对齐；2) GSRefinement，基于高斯泼溅的细节感知辅助监督方法，将稀疏3D占据预测投影为密集2D语义分割图，为长尾类别提供有效监督。

Result: 在自建的露天矿数据集和nuScenes数据集上，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过RenderFusion和GSRefinement两大创新模块，UnsOcc有效提升了非结构化场景下3D语义占据预测的鲁棒性，验证了多模态融合和长尾类别监督策略的有效性。

Abstract: Unstructured scenes present unique challenges for autonomous driving, as irregular obstacles and sparse scene layouts undermine the effectiveness of traditional perception methods such as 3D object detection. 3D semantic occupancy prediction has emerged as a prominent focus due to its ability to provide dense spatial representations by assigning semantic labels to individual voxels in 3D space. However, directly applying 3D semantic occupancy prediction to unstructured scenes remains challenging because scene sparsity hinders effective cross-modal fusion and the more severe long-tail distribution in these scenarios further degrades prediction performance. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we construct a dedicated dataset of unstructured scenes collected from open-pit mines. Based on this, we propose UnsOcc, a multi-modal 3D semantic occupancy prediction framework that improves robustness in unstructured environments. At its core, we introduce a rendering-based fusion module, RenderFusion, which enhances cross-modal feature alignment through bidirectional rendering supervision. Furthermore, we propose GSRefinement, a detail-aware auxiliary supervision method based on Gaussian Splatting that projects sparse 3D occupancy predictions into dense 2D semantic segmentation maps, enabling effective supervision for long-tail categories. Extensive experiments on both the open-pit mine dataset and the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [66] [Low-Frequency Shortcuts in Texture-Driven Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03493)
*Utku Şirin,Cathy Hou,David Alvarez-Melis,Stratos Idreos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了纹理驱动领域中的捷径学习现象，发现模型依赖低频成分（LFCs）做决策，而分类信息实则位于高频细节中。修剪LFCs可消除捷径，提高ID准确率（最高8%），并改善对低频损坏的鲁棒性（最高40%），但会引入高频损坏的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有捷径学习研究主要基于形状驱动的标准基准，而许多实际应用领域是纹理驱动的，这种差异导致对纹理驱动领域捷径学习行为的理解不足。

Method: 通过对比纹理驱动领域与标准基准（形状驱动），分析频谱行为差异；采用低频成分（LFCs）修剪方法，从训练和测试集中移除低频特征，观察模型性能变化。

Result: 纹理驱动领域依赖低频捷径；修剪LFCs使ID准确率提升最高8%，对低频损坏的鲁棒性提升最高40%；但高频损坏鲁棒性因过度依赖高频特征而下降，存在权衡。

Conclusion: 纹理驱动领域的捷径学习呈现低频偏斜特性，修剪LFCs可有效缓解；OOD性能由频谱平衡与高频依赖共同决定，需根据实际损坏类型权衡使用。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from shortcut learning, where learned features generalize well to the training set but not to in-distribution (ID) or out-of-distribution (OOD) test sets. Existing studies are all based on a few standard benchmarks, which are shape-driven. Numerous application domains, however, are texture-driven. In this work, we present shortcut learning analysis for texture-driven domains, and compare it with that of a standard benchmark. We show that texture-driven domains suffer from low-frequency shortcuts. They make the majority of their decisions based on a few low-frequency components (LFCs) with a skewed spectral behavior, despite that their classification information is in higher-frequency, fine-grained details. Pruning LFCs from training and test sets eliminates the shortcut and provides a more balanced spectral behavior, improving the ID accuracy by up to 8%. We show that low-frequency shortcuts make the models highly vulnerable to OOD corruptions, leading up to 70% accuracy drop compared to the ID accuracy. Pruning LFCs significantly improves robustness to low-frequency corruptions, by up to 40%, and introduces a trade-off for high-frequency corruptions; the balanced spectral behavior provides a better generalization performance, whereas the increased dependence on high-frequency features reduces it. OOD accuracy depends on the interaction between these two factors.

</details>


### [67] [DyaPlex: Full-Duplex Speech-Motion Model for Dyadic Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03874)
*Koki Nagano,Hongyu Liu,Seonwook Park,Tianye Li,Amrita Mazumdar,Christian Jacobsen,Shengze Wang,Michael Stengel,Rajarshi Roy,Ka Chun Cheung,Simon See,Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DyaPlex是一个用于二元交互的流式全双工语音-运动模型，通过双塔Transformer架构和统一令牌交织机制，实现了同步多模态交互，在4,000小时数据集上训练并取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互模型多为半双工或仅处理单一模态，无法捕捉人类通信中连续、互惠的特性，缺乏同时感知和生成语音与物理运动的流式全双工能力。

Method: 提出双塔Transformer架构，冻结基础全双工语音模型的零样本对话推理能力，构建深度耦合的流式运动通路；引入统一二元令牌交织机制，通过时间对齐的语音-运动RoPE引导交叉注意力，对齐自回归运动与潜在语音特征。

Result: 在4,000小时Seamless Interaction数据集上训练，有效捕捉跨说话者依赖关系，在单体与二元人类交互基准测试中均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: DyaPlex通过创新的架构设计成功实现了全双工多模态同步交互，为更自然的人机交互奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present DyaPlex, a streaming, full-duplex speech-and-motion model designed for dyadic interaction. To capture the continuous and reciprocal nature of human communication, this full-duplex capability empowers the agent to simultaneously perceive and generate both speech and physical motion in a streaming fashion. At its core, our method leverages the strong priors of a foundational full-duplex speech model and integrates a novel motion pathway, thereby achieving fully synchronized multi-modal interaction. Specifically, we design a dual-tower Transformer architecture that preserves the zero-shot conversational reasoning of a frozen base speech model while constructing a deeply coupled, streaming motion pathway. By introducing a unified dyadic token interleaving mechanism and guiding cross-attention via a time-aligned speech-motion RoPE, our model effectively aligns autoregressive motions with rich latent speech features. Trained on the 4,000-hour Seamless Interaction dataset, our model effectively captures cross-speaker dependencies and establishes new state-of-the-art performance across both monadic and dyadic human interaction benchmarks.

</details>


### [68] [Characterizing Detectability in 3DGS Poisoning: A Stage-wise Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03499)
*Quoc-Anh Bui-Huynh,Thanh Duc Ngo,Xue Geng,Kaixin Xu,Wang Zhe,Xulei Yang,Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Poison-3DGS基准，系统研究了3D高斯泼溅重建流程中不同阶段的投毒攻击可检测性，发现检测效果依赖于攻击类型和观测阶段，后期信号（如训练动态和高斯参数统计）提供了早期阶段无法观察到的强线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS面临多种投毒攻击威胁，但现有研究主要关注攻击成功性，防御与检测尚未充分探索。由于3DGS重建管线具有多阶段特性，不同阶段产生的中间表示各异，取证信号具有阶段依赖性，需要超越单阶段评估的逐阶段可检测性分析。

Method: 提出Poison-3DGS基准，涵盖多视角图像、几何、训练动态和高斯参数等阶段特定伪影，在不同场景和攻击类型下系统研究各阶段的可检测性。

Result: 1）可检测性在不同阶段差异显著，没有任何单一阶段在所有攻击类型上一致占优；2）不同攻击展现出不同的阶段特定取证信号，检测效果高度依赖于观测阶段；3）后期信号（训练动态和高斯参数统计）提供了早期阶段无法观察到的强检测线索。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个3DGS中阶段依赖可检测性的系统表征基准，为未来构建鲁棒可靠的3DGS系统奠定基础。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has rapidly emerged as a leading representation for real-time novel view synthesis, but recent work shows it is vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks, including illusory object injection, computation cost amplification, and post hoc model watermarking. Despite this expanding threat surface, existing studies focus mainly on attack success, while defense and detection remain underexplored. From a detection perspective, a key challenge and opportunity arise from the multi-stage nature of the 3DGS reconstruction pipeline, which produces heterogeneous intermediate representations. Forensic signals for detecting poisoning are inherently stage dependent: an attack introduced at one stage may produce signals that emerge only at later stages. This motivates a stage-wise view of detectability that goes beyond single-stage evaluation. We introduce Poison-3DGS, a benchmark for stage-wise characterization of poisoning detection in 3DGS. It exposes stage-specific artifacts, including multi-view images, geometry, training dynamics, and Gaussian parameters, across a diverse set of scenes and attacks. Using it, we conduct a systematic study of detectability across pipeline stages. Our analysis reveals several insights. First, detectability varies significantly across stages, and no single stage consistently dominates across attack types. Second, different attacks exhibit distinct stage-specific forensic signals, so detection effectiveness depends critically on where signals are observed. Third, later-stage signals such as training dynamics and Gaussian parameter statistics provide strong cues not observable at earlier stages. Overall, our work provides a principled benchmark and the first systematic characterization of stage-dependent detectability in 3DGS, offering a foundation for future research on robust and reliable 3DGS systems.

</details>


### [69] [VLESA: Vision-Language Embodied Safety Agent for Human Activity Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03954)
*Hanjiang Hu,Yiyuan Pan,Jiaxing Li,Xusheng Luo,Alexander Robey,Na Li,Yebin Wang,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLESA是一个基于视觉-语言的安全智能体框架，通过分析第一人称视频预测危险动作，并利用GRPO训练的目标条件安全Q过滤器实现上下文感知的实时干预，在ASIMOV-2.0基准上显著提升了干预准确率和动作安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界的AI辅助任务中，动作错误具有即时且不可逆的后果。同一动作在不同意图下安全性截然不同（如递刀给厨师vs递刀给儿童），现有方法缺乏对这种意图依赖型安全的处理能力。

Method: 1) 构建了配对第一人称视频帧与目标条件安全标注的数据集；2) 提出目标条件安全Q过滤器，通过GRPO训练，无需重新训练即可基于推断意图评估动作安全性；3) 设计意图-动作预测智能体，联合推断目标并预测未来动作。

Result: 在ASIMOV-2.0基准上，VLESA在精确时间点上的干预准确率优于基线；GRPO训练的Q过滤器通过目标条件约束解码，将动作安全性提升了超过41个百分点。

Conclusion: VLESA证明了结合意图推断与目标条件安全过滤在物理世界AI安全干预中的有效性，为具身智能体的安全部署提供了可行框架。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly assist humans in physical tasks, ensuring safety becomes paramount -- physical actions carry immediate and irreversible consequences that digital errors do not. We introduce the Vision-Language Embodied Safety Agent (VLESA), a framework that monitors human activities from egocentric video and triggers real-time safety interventions when dangerous actions are predicted. VLESA addresses intent-dependent safety where identical actions can be safe or dangerous depending on context. A dataset pairing egocentric frames with goal-conditioned safety annotations is introduced, enabling a goal-conditioned safety Q-filter trained via GRPO that evaluates actions with respect to inferred intent without retraining. On top of that, an intent-action prediction agent is proposed to jointly infer goals and predict future actions from video. On the ASIMOV-2.0 benchmark, VLESA achieves higher intervention accuracy at the exact ground-truth frame compared to baselines, while the GRPO-trained Q-filter improves action safety by over 41 percentage points through goal-conditioned constrained decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/HanjiangHu/VLESA.

</details>


### [70] [AvatarMix: Identity-Preserving Cross-Avatar Composition for Outfit Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03506)
*Zhaorong Wang,Yoshihiro Kanamori,Yuki Endo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AvatarMix 提出了一种组合式3D虚拟形象服装迁移范式，通过直接组合两个高保真高斯头像的头和身体，结合SeamFix和FullbodyFix两级精化策略，在避免穿模和身份质量下降的同时实现高保真服装个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D虚拟形象服装迁移方法要么因2D升3D导致服装/身份质量下降，要么因分别建模身体和服装层而产生穿模伪影。作者希望绕过这些问题，提出一种直接组合头部和身体的全新范式。

Method: 采用组合式范式直接融合两个高斯头像的头与身体；提出两级精化策略：SeamFix（局部扩散模块精化头发和颈部实现无伪影连接）和FullbodyFix（全身体精化恢复重定向后的服装外观）；基于网格的高斯表示支持鲁棒的网格重定向技术以适应不同体型。

Result: 在服装保真度和身份保持方面达到SOTA效果，有效避免了穿模伪影和多视角不一致问题。

Conclusion: AvatarMix为真实感3D服装个性化提供了新视角，通过组合式范式而非分层建模规避了传统方法的固有问题。

Abstract: Existing 3D avatar outfit transfer methods face distinct challenges: approaches that lift 2D edits to 3D often suffer from outfit or identity quality degradation, while those that separately model body and clothing layers are prone to intersection artifacts. We introduce AvatarMix, a compositional paradigm that bypasses these issues by directly composing the head and body from two high-fidelity Gaussian avatars. While this paradigm inherently preserves outfit quality and avoids intersections, it introduces challenges in creating a seamless join and maintaining appearance fidelity after body reshaping. To this end, we propose a two-tier refinement strategy: SeamFix, a localized diffusion module that refines hair and neck to ensure an artifact-free join, and an optional full-body refinement, FullbodyFix, that restores garment appearance when retargeting degrades the clothed body. Both operate on renders from an already 3D-consistent Gaussian avatar, which limits multi-view artifacts compared to 2D-to-3D lifting. To preserve the user's body identity, our mesh-based Gaussian representation enables the adaptation of a robust mesh retargeting technique, precisely reshaping the clothed body to the user's physique and robustly handling diverse body shapes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art results in outfit fidelity and identity preservation, providing a new perspective for realistic 3D outfit personalization. Project page: https://larsph.github.io/avatarmix/

</details>


### [71] [Exploring Easy Boosts for Lidar Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03992)
*Tetiana Martyniuk,Jonathan Seele,Alexandre Boulch,Gilles Puy,Renaud Marlet,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种无需复杂架构重设计的“免费午餐”策略——语义伪标签和可见性信息——来提升激光雷达语义场景补全（SSC）性能，使旧模型能媲美甚至超越当前SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在不重新设计复杂网络架构的前提下，通过简单有效的策略提升激光雷达语义场景补全（SSC）性能。

Method: 1) 利用现成分割器为输入点云提供语义伪标签；2) 为激光雷达扫描添加可见性信息（区分空空间与未知空间）。

Result: 高质量语义先验是mIoU提升的主要驱动力；可见性信息提供次要性能提升；采用这些简单增强后，旧模型可以保持与SOTA系统竞争力甚至超越它们。

Conclusion: 通过输入层面的简单增强（语义伪标签+可见性信息），无需复杂架构改进即可显著提升SSC性能，揭示了高质量语义先验的关键作用。

Abstract: This paper investigates "free lunch" strategies to boost the performance of lidar semantic scene completion (SSC) without requiring complex architectural redesigns. We first demonstrate that endowing input point clouds with semantic pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf segmentors significantly improves the performance of existing architectures. By evaluating these models against an oracle, we establish that high-quality semantic priors are a primary driver of mIoU gains. Furthermore, we equip the input lidar scan with visibility information that distinguishes between empty and unknown spaces, which provides a secondary performance boost across the tested architectures. Using these simple enhancements, we observe that older models remain competitive with state-of-the-art systems, and can even outperform them. Our code is available at https://github.com/astra-vision/SSC-Priors.

</details>


### [72] [Structure-Guided Mixed Masked Pretraining and Spatial Continuity Regularization for Printed Circuit Board Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03508)
*Peitong Wang,Nuo Wang,Enxin Qin,Chengjin Yu,Hanyu Xuan,Yuanting Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段PCB缺陷检测框架，结合结构引导混合掩码预训练与空间连续性正则化，在DsPCBSD+数据集上达到85.5% mAP0.5和52.3% mAP0.5:0.95，优于多个强基线检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCB缺陷检测中，许多缺陷微小、低对比度且嵌在密集电路背景中，现有方法难以有效检测。

Method: 两阶段框架：（1）预训练阶段：稀疏卷积掩码预训练，使用结构引导混合掩码构建信息丰富的掩码输入，学习PCB结构先验；（2）微调阶段：引入空间连续性正则化项，约束分散的正预测，促进缺陷区域的紧凑定位。

Result: 在DsPCBSD+数据集上，mAP0.5达85.5%，mAP0.5:0.95达52.3%，优于多个强基线；消融和定性实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效提升了工业AOI场景下PCB微小、低对比度缺陷的检测性能。

Abstract: Printed circuit board (PCB) defect detection is an essential part of automated optical inspection (AOI); yet it remains challenging in practice because many defects are tiny, low-contrast, and embedded in dense circuit backgrounds. To address these issues, this paper presents a two-phase PCB defect detection framework that combines structure-guided mixed masked pretraining with spatial continuity regularization. In the pretraining stage, we design a sparse convolutional masked pretraining scheme to exploit unlabeled PCB images, where structure-guided mixed masking is used to construct informative masked inputs. The sparse convolutional reconstruction pipeline suppresses invalid responses from masked regions and enables the detector backbone to infer missing PCB structures from visible conductive patterns, thereby learning PCB structural priors. In the fine-tuning stage, the pretrained backbone is transferred to the downstream defect detection task. For the task, a spatial continuity regularization term is introduced during fine-tuning. This term constrains dispersed positive predictions assigned to the same defect instance and promotes more compact localization on elongated defect regions. Experiments on the DsPCBSD+ dataset show that the proposed method achieves 85.5% mAP0.5 and 52.3% mAP0.5:0.95, outperforming several strong baseline detectors. Ablation studies and qualitative results further confirm the effectiveness of the proposed framework for robust PCB defect detection in industrial AOI scenarios.

</details>
