### [73] [SimuScene: Simulation-Ready Compositional 3D Scene Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03994)
*Inhee Lee,Sangwon Baik,Sungjoo Kim,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hyunsoo Cha,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimuScene提出了一种物理感知的3D场景重建方法，在形状和布局估计过程中将物理引擎作为诊断工具，通过重力模拟检测穿透、悬空等问题并生成校正信号，从而产出可直接用于机器人操控的稳定仿真场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D重建方法虽能恢复物体形状，但组合后存在严重物理冲突（穿透、悬空、下沉），而现有物理感知方法仅将物理修正作为后处理，未能解决底层几何误差的累积问题。

Method: 物理引擎作为诊断测量工具嵌入生成过程：对重建物体进行重力模拟仿真，将穿透和支撑失败转化为定量校正信号，驱动重力轴拉伸和非完整形状重采样，形成物理反馈循环。

Result: 在物理稳定性和几何对齐基准上达到最先进性能，并在人形控制、机械臂操控任务中验证了实用价值。

Conclusion: 将物理感知前置到重建过程而非后处理，能有效解决场景组合后的物理不一致性，产生仿真就绪的3D场景。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactive, simulation-ready 3D scenes from a single image is a critical bottleneck for robotic manipulation. While recent single-image lifters recover plausible per-object shapes, composing them yields scenes that collapse under physical simulation due to interpenetrating, hovering, or sinking objects. Existing physics-aware methods address this strictly as a post-hoc layout correction, leaving the underlying geometric errors unresolved. To address this, we introduce SimuScene, a compositional 3D reconstruction pipeline that puts physics in the loop of shape and layout estimation. Rather than using physics merely for layout cleanup, we utilize the physics engine as a diagnostic measurement tool during the generative process itself. By diagnostically simulating reconstructed objects under gravity, we convert penetration and support failures into quantitative correction signals that drive gravity-axis stretching and amodal shape resampling. This physics-informed feedback loop mitigates accumulated reconstruction errors and produces a stable, simulation-ready compositional 3D scene. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on physical stability and geometric alignment benchmarks. We further highlight SimuScene's utility by deploying reconstructed environments in humanoid control and robot-arm manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [74] [EvoMemNav: Efficient Self-Evolving Fine-Grained Memory for Zero-Shot Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03509)
*Zuhao Ge,Xiaosong Jia,Chao Wu,Yuchen Zhou,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoMemNav提出了一种自演进的细粒度记忆框架，通过视觉-语义记忆图（VSMGraph）和预算驱动的粗到细策略，实现零样本具身导航中的高效长程规划，在GOAT-Bench和HM3D上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么使用检测器为中心的稀疏场景图（丢失细节、累积噪声），要么依赖计算昂贵的3D重建，无法在零样本导航中高效构建长程记忆。

Method: 1) 构建VSMGraph，将原始视图作为一等记忆，辅以轻量语义线索和拓扑关系形成room-view-object层级结构；2) 预算驱动的粗到细策略：粗阶段压缩搜索空间，细阶段用VLM做精确验证；3) 反射驱动写回机制，在每个子任务后更新图附着的先验知识。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench和HM3D上的物体、文本描述、图像目标三种模态下，SR/SPL指标一致提升，多实例消歧能力更强，提前停止更少，零样本泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 所提的细粒度、自演进记忆框架在无需重新训练的情况下，通过保留视觉细节和动态更新先验知识，有效提升了零样本长程导航的性能。

Abstract: Building memory is essential for long-horizon planning in zero-shot embodied navigation. Detector-centric scene graphs often compress observations into sparse nodes, discarding fine-grained visual evidence and accumulating noise, while 3D reconstruction-based methods remain computationally prohibitive. We present EvoMemNav, an efficient, self-evolving, fine-grained memory framework for zero-shot embodied navigation. EvoMemNav constructs a Visual-Semantic Memory Graph (VSMGraph) that keeps raw views as first-class memory and organizes them with lightweight semantic cues and topological relations into a room-view-object hierarchy, preserving fine-grained details for disambiguation and Stop verification. To scale to growing memory, we introduce a budgeted coarse-to-fine policy: a coarse stage compresses the search space into promising regions, and a fine stage invokes a VLM only for targeted verification and decision. Beyond static memories, EvoMemNav performs reflection-driven write-back after each subtask, updating graph-attached priors that encode accumulated environmental knowledge to refine future decisions without retraining. Experiments on GOAT-Bench and HM3D across object, text-description, and image-goal modalities show consistent gains in SR/SPL, with better multi-instance disambiguation, fewer premature stops, and stronger zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [75] [Knowledge-Preserved Model Tuning in Null-Space for Robust Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03539)
*Haoxuan Chen,Xianqin Liu,Jian-Fang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Null-Space Tuning (NST)框架，利用零空间几何特性，在冻结预训练权重的条件下，通过将高质量输入的成分约束在零空间内、低质量输入的恢复成分引导至非零空间，实现对退化视频输入的矫正，同时保持预训练知识不变，在混合质量基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空视频定位方法主要针对高质量输入设计，忽略了真实场景中广泛存在的低质量视频。虽然LoRA等微调方法可以适配退化输入，但会不可避免地破坏预训练知识。

Method: 提出Null-Space Tuning (NST)框架，利用冻结权重的零空间几何特性（零空间向量加至层输入不影响输出），结合Quality-Adaptive Unit和Dual-Space Reparameterization，将高质量输入的成分约束在零空间内，低质量输入的恢复成分引导至非零空间，从而在矫正退化输入的同时保留预训练知识。

Result: 在混合质量基准上，NST超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: NST通过零空间调优有效解决了低质量视频输入下的时空视频定位问题，在不破坏预训练知识的前提下实现了对退化输入的矫正。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding aims to localize object tubes based on textual queries. While recent methods have achieved remarkable success, they mainly focus on high-quality(HQ) inputs, neglecting the widespread presence of low-quality(LQ) videos in real-world scenarios. Although tuning methods like LoRA can adapt to degraded inputs, they inevitably disrupt pre-trained knowledge. To address this, we propose Null-Space Tuning (NST). This framework exploits the geometric property that adding vectors within the null-space of frozen weights to the layer input does not affect the output. Leveraging this, NST injects learnable residuals into input features that can be selectively invisible to the pre-trained backbone. Specifically, NST combines the Quality-Adaptive Unit and Dual-Space Reparameterization to synthesize these residuals by confining components for HQ inputs to the null-space, while directing restoration components for LQ inputs to the non-null space. As the frozen weights eliminate null-space components, we effectively rectify degraded inputs while preserving pre-trained knowledge for HQ inputs. Extensive experiments show that NST outperforms state-of-the-art methods on our Mixed-Quality benchmark.

</details>


### [76] [Attend to Anything: Foundation Model for Unified Human Attention Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03540)
*Wenzhuo Zhao,Ronghao Xian,Keren Fu,Qijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AAM，一个统一的多模态注意力基础模型，通过层次化语言提示嵌入双曲空间及Fokker-Planck方程建模视频动态注意力，在16个基准上平均提升6%并实现4倍视频推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力（显著性）建模方法在不同模态、场景和任务间高度碎片化，模型容量和数据规模增加后仍局限于特定场景和任务，无法在实际应用中泛化。

Method: 1) 将注意力重新定义为认知蕴含关系，构建从一般到特殊的层次结构，通过双曲空间中的层次化嵌入语言提示实现；2) 采用流体动力学视角，用Fokker-Planck方程将视频帧注意力建模为扩散时域演化过程。

Result: 在16个基准数据集上平均超越SOTA 6%，视频推理速度提升约4倍，证明AAM为注意力与显著性相关任务提供了原则性基础。

Conclusion: AAM统一了图像、视频和音视频场景下的注意力建模，克服了现有方法的碎片化局限，为未来注意力研究奠定了原则性基础。

Abstract: Existing human attention (saliency) modeling methods persist as highly fragmented across modalities, scenes, and task formulations. Consequently, even with increasing model capacity and data scale, current models predominantly remain scene-dependent and task-specific, failing to practically generalize in real-world applications. To address the fundamental limitations, we present the Attend to Anything Model (AAM), a multi-modal foundation model that unifies attention modeling across various image, video, and audio-visual tasks and scenes. AAM reformulates attention as a cognitive entailment relationship organized in a general-to-specific hierarchy, implemented through language prompts with hierarchical embeddings in hyperbolic space. Furthermore, to unify static image and dynamic video attention, we adopt a fluid-dynamics perspective, formulating video-frame attention as a diffusive temporal evolution governed by the Fokker--Planck equation. Extensive experiments on 16 benchmarks demonstrate that AAM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods by an average of 6\% across various scenarios, while achieving approximately a 4$\times$ speedup in video inference. Overall, these results demonstrate that AAM provides a principled foundation for future research on attention and saliency-related tasks. The dataset and code will be available at https://github.com/wz-zhao/Attend-to-Anything.

</details>


### [77] [\textsc{CR-Seg}: Attention-Guided and CoT-Enhanced Coarse-to-Refined Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03564)
*Yifan Cao,Xiaocui Yang,Faxian Wan,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CR-Seg，一种两阶段粗到细的推理分割框架，通过注意力图提取（EAP模块）和全局到局部思维链（GLCoT）来改进复杂语言驱动的目标分割，解决了跨模态对齐困难和推理-答案不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割方法要么依赖语义token导致跨模态对齐困难，要么依赖显式空间提示（如边界框）丢失整体响应语义。作者旨在解决这些局限性，实现更好的视觉-文本联合推理分割。

Method: 提出两阶段粗到细框架CR-Seg：(1) 设计EAP模块，提取注意力图进行粗目标定位并选择信息点；(2) 引入GLCoT（全局到局部思维链），引导模型从全局场景上下文逐步推理到局部目标细节；(3) 将EAP输出的注意力图和点输入SAM进行掩码精炼。

Result: 在推理分割基准上的大量实验证明了CR-Seg的有效性。

Conclusion: CR-Seg通过粗到细的两阶段设计，结合注意力引导和思维链增强，有效提升了复杂语言指令下的推理分割性能。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to segment target objects described by complex language through joint visual-textual reasoning. Existing methods typically rely on either learned semantic tokens to bridge Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and segmentation models, suffering from difficult cross-modal alignment, or explicit spatial prompts such as bounding boxes, which may lose holistic response semantics. To address these limitations, we propose Attention-Guided and CoT-Enhanced Coarse-to-Refined Reasoning Segmentation, termed CR-Seg, a two-stage framework for coarse-to-refined reasoning segmentation. Specifically, we design an Extract Attention Maps and Points (EAP) module to extract attention maps for coarse target localization and select informative points, both of which are fed into SAM for mask refinement. To alleviate reasoning--answer inconsistency, we further introduce Global-to-Local Chain-of-Thought (GLCoT), which guides the model to reason progressively from global scene context to local target details. Extensive experiments on reasoning segmentation benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of CR-Seg.

</details>


### [78] [Efficient Transformer-Based Localized Patch Sampling for Choroid Plexus Segmentation in Multiple Sclerosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03566)
*Po-Jui Lu,Alessandro Cagol,Mario Ocampo-Pineda,Federico Spagnolo,Marina Mastantuono,Andreea-Alexandra Aldea,Jannis Müller,Özgür Yaldizli,Matthias Weigel,Lester Melie-Garcia,Roberta Magliozzi,Maria Pia Sormani,Ludwig Kappos,Jens Kuhle,Cristina Granziera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SwinUNETR的侧脑室脉络丛自动分割方法，通过32x32x32体素局部补丁采样，在多发性硬化患者MRI数据上达到DSC 0.868，计算量降低99%，优于UXNET模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧脑室脉络丛(LVCP)是MS的重要影像生物标志物，但手动分割极为繁琐，限制了其在临床试验和纵向评估中的广泛应用，亟需自动化分割方法。

Method: 采用SwinUNETR架构，对32x32x32体素大小的脑室局部补丁进行训练，利用多模态MRI（MPRAGE+FLAIR）输入，与3D UXNET模型对比，评估指标包括DSC、HD95和GFLOPs计算量。

Result: 在扩展测试集上，SwinUNETR平均DSC为0.868，显著高于UXNET的0.858（p<0.0001）；仅用FLAIR时DSC达0.863（HD95:1.86mm vs UXNET的3.00mm）；计算量降低99%（91.8 vs 22,080 GFLOPs）。

Conclusion: 局部补丁采样结合SwinUNETR架构提供了精准、鲁棒且统计上优于当前主流模型的方法，极大降低计算成本，适合临床和科研环境广泛部署。

Abstract: Background: The lateral ventricle choroid plexus (LVCP) is gaining recognition as a key imaging biomarker for multiple sclerosis (MS) related to physical disability and neuroinflammation. Yet, manual segmentation of the LVCP is highly tedious, restricting its use in broad clinical trials and longitudinal assessments. This research aims to develop a SwinUNETR-driven pipeline that leverages targeted intra- and peri-ventricular small patch sampling to automatically segment the LVCP in MS from both standalone and multi-modal MRI inputs. Methods: We retrospectively assessed 3T MRI scans across three sets of data stemming from two separate MS-dominant cohorts (Dataset 1: n=177; Dataset 2: n=177; expanded test set: n=388). Our method employed a SwinUNETR architecture trained on 32x32x32 voxel patches, benchmarking it against the 3D UXNET model. The primary metric for evaluation was the Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC), supplemented by computational demand (GFLOPs) and the 95th percentile Hausdorff Distance (HD95). Results: On the extended test set, the SwinUNETR model secured a mean DSC of 0.868 (95% CI: 0.863-0.872) with MPRAGE and FLAIR combined, showing a statistically significant gain over UXNET (DSC: 0.858 [95% CI: 0.853-0.862], p<0.0001). When restricted to standalone FLAIR inputs, the transformer-based approach sustained a high DSC of 0.863, while the spatial localization of UXNET worsened considerably (HD95: 1.86 vs. 3.00 mm). Importantly, the proposed framework lowered computational load by 99% (91.8 vs. 22,080 GFLOPs). By integrating localized patch sampling with a SwinUNETR architecture, this methodology offers an accurate, robust, and statistically superior alternative to current leading models for LVCP segmentation. Its vast reduction in computational cost makes it ideal for widespread implementation in clinical and research environments.

</details>


### [79] [When Attention Collapses: Stage-Aware Visual Token Pruning from Structure to Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03569)
*Jiahui Wang,Kai Zhang,Mai Han,Huanghe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Structure-to-Semantics (STS)两阶段视觉token剪枝框架，先通过排斥采样最大化空间多样性，再通过指令感知交叉注意力过滤与提示无关的token，解决了基于注意力剪枝导致特征多样性丧失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法仅依赖初始注意力分数，存在"高注意力分数在语义相似区域坍塌"的关键缺陷，导致特征多样性严重下降，丢失重要的上下文细节。

Method: 提出STS两阶段框架：阶段一通过排斥采样机制最大化空间和结构多样性；阶段二利用指令感知的交叉注意力精确过滤与提示无关的token。两个阶段协同工作，先保证几何覆盖，再按语义相关性精炼保留的token。

Result: 广泛评估表明STS有效缓解了基于注意力选择的冗余问题，在结构多样性和细粒度任务对齐方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过解耦剪枝过程，将"结构多样性保持"和"语义相关性过滤"分开处理，是提升VLM推理效率同时保留关键信息的有效策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities but suffer from significant computational overhead during inference. While visual token pruning offers a promising solution, existing methods predominantly rely on initial attention scores. This single-metric paradigm presents a critical flaw: high attention scores inherently collapse onto semantically similar regions, thereby severely reducing feature diversity and discarding vital contextual details. To address this, we introduce Structure-to-Semantics (STS), a novel two-stage visual token pruning framework that explicitly decouples the pruning process. The first stage employs a repulsion-based sampling mechanism to maximize spatial and structural diversity. The second stage leverages instruction-aware cross-attention to precisely filter out prompt-irrelevant tokens. This two-stage synergy constitutes the core of STS, first ensuring geometric coverage and then refining the retained tokens according to semantic relevance. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that STS mitigates the redundancy caused by attention-based selection, improving both structural diversity and fine-grained task alignment of the preserved visual tokens.

</details>


### [80] [Eliciting Complex Spatial Reasoning in MLLMs through Wide-Baseline Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03577)
*Hao Zhong,Muzhi Zhu,Shenyan Zeng,Anzhou Li,Cong Chen,Hua Geng,Duochao Shi,Wentao Ye,Tao Lin,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonMatch-Bench基准测试，用于评估多模态大模型在宽基线匹配(WBM)中的空间推理能力，并设计了DCRL强化学习方法，通过自动数据生成和动态对应强化学习显著提升WBM性能（最佳基线F1=37.2 vs 人类84.0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在宽基线匹配（涉及几何理解、视角变化、细粒度感知和遮挡推理）方面缺乏系统的评估和训练框架，亟需建立标准化基准和有效训练方法。

Method: 1) 构建ReasonMatch-Bench基准测试，按视点位移和匹配粒度分层（室内/室外/物体中心场景）；2) 开发可扩展的数据生成管道，从RGB-D视频和SfM重建中自动提取宽基线视图对；3) 提出动态对应强化学习(DCRL)，结合图像级视点渐进和点级对应课程学习，通过可验证奖励训练WBM。

Result: 在困难90样本子集上，人类F1=84.0，最佳基线仅37.2；DCRL显著提升ReasonMatch-Bench性能，并迁移至相关空间基准，同时保持或小幅提升通用视觉理解能力。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在细粒度宽基线匹配上远不及人类，但DCRL方法有效缩小了这一差距，证明了基于可验证奖励的强化学习在不依赖显式思维链监督情况下训练空间推理能力的可行性。

Abstract: Wide-baseline matching (WBM) requires integrating geometric understanding, viewpoint changes, fine-grained perception, and occlusion reasoning, making it a challenging testbed for spatial reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) deployed in physical environments. However, current MLLMs lack systematic evaluation and training frameworks for these capabilities. We introduce ReasonMatch-Bench, a benchmark stratified by viewpoint displacement and matching granularity across indoor, outdoor, and object-centric scenarios, and show that current MLLMs still struggle with fine-grained wide-baseline correspondence: on a difficult 90-sample subset, human annotators achieve 84.0 F1, while the best existing baseline reaches 37.2. To bridge this gap, we build a scalable data-generation pipeline that automatically extracts wide-baseline view pairs from large-scale video-3D corpora, including RGB-D videos and SfM reconstructions, yielding diverse and verifiable supervision. We further propose Dynamic Correspondence Reinforcement Learning (DCRL), which combines Image-Level Viewpoint Progression and Point-Level Correspondence Curriculum to improve WBM training through verifiable rewards without explicit CoT supervision. Extensive experiments show that DCRL substantially improves ReasonMatch-Bench and transfers to related spatial benchmarks, while maintaining general visual understanding performance with modest gains on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [81] [Diffusing in the Right Space: A Systematic Study of Latent Diffusability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03578)
*Tianxiong Zhong,Xingye Tian,Xuebo Wang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了潜在扩散模型中的“潜在可扩散性”问题，发现传统视觉分词器的重建质量并不能直接决定生成质量。通过训练大量不同配置的分词器并进行下游扩散模型评估，作者提出了一种新的度量——Velocity Irreducible Variance (VIV)，用于测量由轨迹交叉引起的速度模糊性，实验表明VIV是生成质量最稳定的预测指标之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现潜在表示的重建质量与生成质量之间存在不一致性，且已有研究提出了多种关于“扩散友好”潜在空间属性的解释，但这些属性仅在有限的tokenizer和实验设置下得到验证。作者希望系统性地探究究竟哪些属性最能够预测下游生成质量，并验证这些结论的泛化能力。

Method: 训练了大量具有不同正则化策略、架构和潜在配置的视觉分词器，使用多个下游扩散骨干网络进行评估；引入新指标 Velocity Irreducible Variance (VIV)，用于量化潜在空间中轨迹交叉导致的速度模糊性。

Result: 发现若干潜在属性与生成质量存在一致相关性，且在不同实验设置下表现出强泛化能力；VIV成为生成质量最稳定的预测指标之一。

Conclusion: 潜在空间的可扩散性应作为评估指标的重要维度，VIV是衡量该性质的有效且稳定的度量工具。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models leverage visual tokenizers to compress images into latent spaces for efficient generative modeling. However, better reconstruction quality of a tokenizer does not necessarily translate into better generation quality, suggesting that latent representations should be evaluated not only by fidelity but also by their diffusability. Recent studies have proposed diverse explanations for diffusion-friendly latent spaces, including semantic separability, affine equivariance, distribution uniformity, spatial structure, spectral smoothness, and manifold continuity. Yet these properties are often validated on a limited set of tokenizers, leaving it unclear which factors are most predictive of downstream generation quality and whether such conclusions hold beyond the specific settings in which they are introduced. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of latent diffusability by training a large collection of tokenizers with diverse regularization strategies, architectures, and latent configurations, and evaluating them with multiple downstream diffusion backbones. Our analysis identifies several latent properties that consistently correlate with generation quality and exhibit strong generalization across experimental settings. Beyond existing metrics, we introduce Velocity Irreducible Variance (VIV), a measure of velocity ambiguity induced by trajectory crossings. Extensive experiments show that VIV is one of the most stable predictors of generation quality.

</details>


### [82] [World Models Meet Language Models: On the Complementarity of Concrete and Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03603)
*Yucheng Zhou,Wei Tao,Yiwen Guo,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PF-OPSD方法，通过特权未来自蒸馏学习何时调用、验证和整合视觉未来模拟，在多模态大语言模型和世界模型之间实现受控具体推理，在VRQABench和OpenWorldQA基准上分别提升10.6%和10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型生成的视觉 rollout 随机且可能视觉合理但任务错误，需要确定何时视觉模拟有用、 rollout 是否可信以及如何影响最终答案，因此需让模型学习受控的具体推理。

Method: 提出Privileged-Future On-Policy Self-Distillation (PF-OPSD)：训练时利用真实未来视频和答案作为特权上下文，评估on-policy的具体推理轨迹；测试时学生模型不依赖真实未来。

Result: 在VRQABench和OpenWorldQA上分别超越基线10.6%和10.9%，同时增强对噪声或冲突rollout的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PF-OPSD有效实现了对视觉未来模拟的受控调用与验证，明确了具体推理与抽象推理的互补整合框架。

Abstract: World models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide complementary capabilities for predicting future outcomes from static visual observations. World models can generate concrete visual rollouts of possible futures, while MLLMs can reason abstractly over questions, goals, and rules. However, generated rollouts are stochastic and may be visually plausible but task-incorrect, making it necessary to determine when visual simulation is useful, whether a rollout is credible, and how it should influence the final answer. We formulate this problem as controlled concrete reasoning, where a model learns to invoke, verify, and integrate visual future simulation alongside abstract reasoning. To study this setting, we construct two human-verified benchmarks, VRQABench for controllable spatial lookahead and OpenWorldQA for open-domain physical prediction, and propose Privileged-Future On-Policy Self-Distillation (PF-OPSD). During training, PF-OPSD uses ground-truth future videos and answers only as teacher-side privileged context to evaluate on-policy concrete-reasoning trajectories, while the deployable student never observes true futures at test time. Experimental results show that PF-OPSD outperforms baseline by 10.6% and 10.9% on VRQABench and OpenWorldQA, respectively, while increasing robustness to noisy or conflicting rollouts. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/yczhou001/PF-OPSD.

</details>


### [83] [SkelHCC: A Hyperbolic CLIP-Driven Cache Adaptation Framework for Skeleton-based One-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03610)
*Yanan Liu,Anqi Zhu,Jingmin Zhu,Jun Liu,Hossein Rahmani,Mohammed Bennamoun,Farid Boussaid,Dan Xu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SkelHCC框架，利用双曲空间和CLIP模型实现单样本骨架动作识别，通过层级双曲嵌入和免训练的LLM引导缓存机制，在多个基准数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单样本骨架动作识别方法基于欧几里得嵌入和底层运动线索，无法有效建模骨架数据的树状层级结构（关节-部位-身体），导致跨模态对齐困难，泛化到未见动作类别时性能不佳。

Method: 提出SkelHCC框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) EH-HCLIP模块，将骨架序列和动作语言嵌入共享双曲空间，利用双曲几何的负曲率和指数体积增长自然编码关节-部位-身体的层级结构；(2) LMV-Cache模块，免训练的LLM引导多粒度投票缓存机制，实现上下文感知推理。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D 60、NTU RGB+D 120和PKU-MMD三个基准数据集上，SkelHCC一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将双曲几何引入骨架动作识别，结合CLIP跨模态对齐和免训练缓存机制，有效解决了单样本场景下的层级结构建模和泛化问题。

Abstract: Skeleton-based action recognition aims to understand human behaviors from body joint sequences and is especially challenging in the one-shot setting, where only a single labeled exemplar is available for each novel action. A key challenge is learning representations that capture the hierarchical and compositional structure of human motion while aligning effectively with high-level action semantics under extreme data scarcity. Existing approaches, largely based on Euclidean embeddings and low-level motion cues, struggle to model the tree-like organization of skeleton data, limiting cross-modal alignment and generalization to unseen action categories. We propose SkelHCC, a unified skeleton hyperbolic CLIP-driven cache adaptation framework for one-shot skeleton-based action recognition. SkelHCC introduces an Explicitly Hierarchical Hyperbolic CLIP (EH-HCLIP) module that embeds skeleton sequences and action language into a shared hyperbolic space. By leveraging the negative curvature and exponential volume growth of hyperbolic geometry, EH-HCLIP naturally encodes the joint-part-body hierarchy of human anatomy and yields structurally consistent cross-modal representations. To support efficient one-shot adaptation, SkelHCC further integrates a training-free LLM-guided Multi-granularity Voting Cache (LMV-Cache) for context-aware inference. Experiments on NTU RGB+D 60, NTU RGB+D 120, and PKU-MMD demonstrate that SkelHCC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [84] [TurtleAI: Benchmarking Multimodal Models for Visual Programming in Turtle Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03626)
*Chao Wen,Jacqueline Staub,Adish Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了TurtleAI基准测试，评估VLM在教育导向的视觉编程任务上的表现。发现大多数模型成功率低于30%，通过少量种子样本生成合成数据进行微调，可将Qwen2-VL-72B的性能提升约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉编程研究多聚焦于生产力场景，缺乏对教育场景下VLM能力的系统评估。需要填补这一空白，理解当前VLM在教育视觉编程任务中的表现及其限制因素。

Method: (1) 构建TurtleAI基准，包含基于真实教育任务的823个视觉编程样本；(2) 评估20+个VLM（含GPT-5、GPT-4o、Qwen2-VL-72B等）；(3) 提出基于少量种子样本的数据生成技术，生成合成数据用于微调。

Result: 多数VLM成功率低于30%；在Qwen2-VL-72B上使用合成数据微调后，在真实任务上性能提升约20%。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在教育视觉编程任务上存在显著不足，主要受限于空间推理和精确视觉复制能力；微调可有效改善视觉推理与代码实现之间的对齐。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have been explored for visual programming, where they generate code to solve visual tasks. However, most prior work focuses on visual programming for productivity; it remains unclear how well current VLMs perform on education-oriented visual programming and what factors limit their performance. To bridge this gap, we introduce TurtleAI, a benchmark containing 823 tasks curated based on real-world visual programming tasks in the Turtle Graphics domain. Solving these tasks requires models to perceive geometric patterns, reason about spatial relationships, and synthesize Python code that faithfully reproduces geometric patterns. We evaluate 20+ VLMs, including GPT-5, GPT-4o, and Qwen2-VL-72B, and find that they struggle significantly, with most achieving success rates below 30%. To address these limitations, we propose a data generation technique that requires only a small set of seed samples. Fine-tuning Qwen2-VL-72B on the resulting synthetic data yields an improvement of about 20% on real-world tasks. Our failure analysis reveals that GPT-4o struggles with spatial reasoning and precise visual replication, whereas fine-tuning primarily improves the alignment between visual reasoning and code implementation.

</details>


### [85] [VidMsg: A Benchmark for Implicit Message Inference in Short Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03635)
*Issar Tzachor,Michael Green,Rami Ben-Ari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VidMsg基准，用于评估模型对短视频中隐含信息（而非表面视觉内容）的理解能力。基准包含400个YouTube片段、9个主题领域和52个细粒度目标信息，通过“信息优先”流程构建。实验表明现有视频-语言模型在此任务上表现不佳，并提出了基线方法VidVec-Msg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解研究多关注可观测的物体和动作，忽略了视频创作者嵌入的隐含信息或创作意图。作者希望填补这一空白，推动视频模型从表面感知走向深层语义理解。

Method: 构建“信息优先”流水线：先用LLM将目标信息转化为间接搜索场景，再检索候选片段，最后由人工标注保留能传达隐含信息但不过于直白的片段。设计了双向信息-片段检索任务和多项选择QA诊断任务。

Result: 现有视频-语言模型在VidMsg上表现较差，因为任务需要语用推理、上下文线索整合以及语义相近信息间的判别能力。VidVec-Msg基线方法有所改进，但仍留有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 隐含信息理解是当前视频理解模型的薄弱环节，VidMsg为这一方向提供了标准化评估基准，未来需要开发更强的高层级语义推理模型。

Abstract: Understanding short online videos involves more than identifying visible objects and actions; video makers often include an underlying message or purpose in the clip. We introduce VidMsg, a benchmark for evaluating implicit message understanding in short, internet-native video clips. VidMsg contains 400 YouTube-derived clips across 9 practical topic areas and 52 fine-grained target messages, covering domains such as career and finance, education, health and well-being, culture, safety, sustainability, and lifestyle. VidMsg is constructed through a message-first pipeline: an LLM first translates target messages into indirect search scenarios, which are used to retrieve candidate clips. Human annotators then retain clips that convey the intended message without being overly explicit. VidMsg is designed primarily for bidirectional message-clip retrieval for scalable applications such as video search and recommendation, where systems must capture holistic video understanding. In addition to retrieval, VidMsg includes a diagnostic multiple-choice QA benchmark, where models select the intended message of a clip from semantically related alternatives. Experiments with contemporary video-language and retrieval models show that strong models often fail on VidMsg, because the task requires pragmatic inference, integration of contextual cues, and discrimination among semantically close messages. We also introduce VidVec-Msg, a baseline method that improves message-oriented retrieval while leaving substantial headroom for future work.

</details>


### [86] [A Benchmark for Semi-supervised Multi-modal Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03646)
*Haoliang Meng,Xiaopeng Hong,Yabin Wang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个半监督多模态人群计数基准，包括任务形式化定义、标准化协议、多样化基线方法及全面评估，代码和数据集划分已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏半监督多模态人群计数的标准化基准，难以公平比较和推动该领域发展。

Method: 1) 形式化定义半监督多模态人群计数任务；2) 设计标准化协议，规定不同标注比例下的有标记/无标记数据划分；3) 精心适配多种基线方法（包括全监督多模态方法和半监督单模态方法）。

Result: 在提出的基准上全面评估了各类基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: 为半监督多模态人群计数提供了首个标准化评估平台，为该领域后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper constructs the first benchmark on semi-supervised multi-modal crowd counting. To lay the foundation for this unexplored task, we first formulate the semi-supervised multi-modal setting and a standardized protocol that specifies the labeled-unlabeled data partition across different labeled ratios. Next, to establish solid reference points, we carefully tailor a diverse set of representative baselines, including existing fully supervised multi-modal methods and semi-supervised single-modal methods. Then, we carefully evaluate their performance under our proposed benchmark. Codes and the data partition will be released on https://github.com/HenryCilence/Semi-supervised-Multimodal-Crowd-Counting.

</details>


### [87] [Graph Regularized Non-negative Reduced Biquaternion Matrix Factorization for Color Image Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03654)
*Hailang Wu,Yonghe Liu,Bingxuan Yu,Chaoqian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对NRBMF忽略数据局部几何结构的问题，提出图正则化非负简化四元数矩阵分解（GNRBMF）模型，通过引入图拉普拉斯正则化项保持原始空间近邻样本在特征空间中表示相似，在简化四元数域保留非负性，并设计了分量式交替投影梯度算法，实验证明识别性能优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NRBMF主要关注重建精度，未利用图像数据的局部几何结构，限制了所学低维特征的判别能力。

Method: 在NRBMF基础上，于简化四元数系数矩阵中引入图拉普拉斯正则化项，使原始空间近邻样本在特征空间中获得相似表示；采用分量式交替投影梯度算法求解优化问题。

Result: 在部分测试设置下取得了具有竞争力或更优的识别性能。

Conclusion: GNRBMF通过图正则化有效提升了彩色图像识别的判别能力。

Abstract: Non-negative reduced biquaternion matrix factorization (NRBMF) uses the product of reduced biquaternion (RB) matrices to incorporate the non-negativity constraints of color image pixels into the factorization process. However, NRBMF mainly focuses on reconstruction accuracy and does not exploit the local geometric structure of image data, which may limit the discriminative ability of the learned low-dimensional features. To address this issue, we propose a graph regularized non-negative reduced biquaternion matrix factorization (GNRBMF) model for color image recognition. The proposed model incorporates a graph Laplacian regularizer into the reduced biquaternion coefficient matrix, encouraging nearby samples in the original space to have similar representations in the learned feature space. Meanwhile, GNRBMF retains the non-negativity-preserving property of NRBMF in the reduced biquaternion domain. To solve the optimization problem, a component-wise alternating projected gradient algorithm is derived, and its convergence properties are analyzed. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed GNRBMF model achieves competitive or superior recognition performance in some tested settings.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Single Solution: Multi-Hypothesis Collaborative Deep Unfolding Network for Image Compressive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03666)
*Wenxue Cui,Hualin Li,Yuhang Qin,Yifu Xu,Xiaopeng Fan,Debin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多假设协作深度展开压缩感知网络（MHC-DUN），通过联合优化多个解空间来处理压缩感知中的病态问题，在梯度下降中使用AlphaNet预测自适应步长，在近端映射中利用假设内和假设间相关性先验，最终在重构性能上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩感知深度展开网络仅局限在单一解空间进行推理，忽略了CS问题的病态本质——理论上存在多个可行的候选假设。

Method: 基于近端梯度下降算法，在多个假设空间上联合执行梯度下降和近端映射：1）梯度下降阶段设计AlphaNet动态预测空间变化步长；2）近端映射阶段设计多假设协作模块，利用假设内和假设间相关性先验联合优化；3）设计复合损失函数平衡测量保真度、假设多样性和重构精度。

Result: 实验结果表明所提出的CS方法优于现有的CS网络。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模和利用多个假设空间，可以更好地处理CS问题的病态性，提升重构性能。

Abstract: Recent deep unfolding networks (DUNs) have advanced Compressive Sensing (CS) by effectively integrating iterative optimization with deep learning architectures. However, most CS approaches predominantly confine their inference to a single solution space, neglecting the inherent ill-posedness of CS problems that intrinsically permits multiple plausible candidate hypotheses. In this paper, a novel Multi-Hypothesis Collaborative Deep Unfolding CS Network (MHC-DUN) is proposed, which explicitly models and leverages multiple hypotheses by jointly optimizing across diverse solution spaces. Specifically, following the Proximal Gradient Descent algorithm, MHC-DUN jointly performs gradient descent and proximal mapping within this multi-hypothesis paradigm. i) For gradient descent, a well-designed AlphaNet is introduced to dynamically predict spatially varying step sizes for all hypotheses, enabling collaborative gradient updates across multiple solutions. ii) For proximal operator, a sophisticated multi-hypothesis collaborative proximal mapping module is designed, which leverages both intra-hypothesis and inter-hypothesis correlation priors to jointly refine multiple solutions. To enable end-to-end training, a novel composite loss function is designed, which balances measurement fidelity, hypothesis diversity, and reconstruction accuracy, encouraging exploration of complementary solutions while maintaining reconstruction fidelity. Experimental results reveal that the proposed CS method outperforms existing CS networks.

</details>


### [89] [A Fast Methane Detection Pipeline on Board Satellites Based on Mag1c-SAS and LinkNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03675)
*Jonáš Herec,Vít Růžička,Rado Pitoňák,Jan Sedmidubsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mag1c-SAS算法，将甲烷检测速度提升约80倍，并结合轻量级LinkNet模型降低噪声，在EMIT-MSeg数据集上AUPRC提升超过30个百分点，STARCOP数据集上F1提升约4个百分点，验证了星上实时检测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷是强效温室气体，通过高光谱卫星图像早期检测泄漏有助于缓解气候变化。但现有高光谱任务需人工指定区域，可能遗漏重要事件；星载实时检测受限于下行链路速率和计算资源，传统算法计算负担过重。

Method: 1) 测试了ACE和CEM快速目标检测方法（此前未用于甲烷检测）；2) 提出Mag1c-SAS，比当前最优Mag1c算法快约80倍；3) 集成U-Net和LinkNet机器学习模型；4) 引入EMIT-MSeg新数据集及高质量标注策略；5) 评估两种新型波段选择策略。

Result: Mag1c-SAS速度提升约80倍；结合LinkNet在EMIT-MSeg上AUPRC提升超30个百分点，STARCOP上F1提升约4个百分点；硬件分析显示功耗和CPU/RAM利用率均在星载可行范围内。

Conclusion: 提出的Mag1c-SAS结合LinkNet的轻量级方案在保证检测效果的同时大幅提升速度，验证了星上实时甲烷检测的可行性，并开源了全部代码、模型和数据。

Abstract: Methane is a potent greenhouse gas, and detecting leaks early via hyperspectral satellite imagery can help climate change mitigation efforts. Meanwhile, many existing hyperspectral missions only capture areas manually targeted by operators, thus missing potential events of interest. To overcome slow downlink rates cost-effectively, onboard detection is a viable solution. However, traditional methane detection methods are too computationally demanding for resource-limited onboard hardware. This work accelerates methane detection by focusing on efficient, low-power algorithms. In particular, we test fast target detection ACE and CEM methods that have not been previously used for methane detection and propose Mag1c-SAS -- a significantly faster variant of the current state-of-the-art Mag1c algorithm. To explore their detection potential, we integrate them with a machine learning model based on U-Net and LinkNet. We evaluate our methods on the STARCOP dataset and a novel EMIT-MSeg dataset, which we introduce and open-source alongside a high-quality annotation strategy. The proposed Mag1c-SAS approach proves highly effective by operating ~80x faster than the original Mag1c approach, providing a visually similar, but noisier result. When additionally paired with the lightweight LinkNet approach, it effectively reduces noise, achieving AUPRC score improvements of over 30 pp on EMIT-MSeg compared to the baseline Mag1c approach, and an F1 score on STARCOP ~4 pp higher. We evaluate two novel band selection strategies and confirm the system's onboard viability through hardware profiling, demonstrating marginal power consumption and efficient CPU/RAM utilization. We release the final system in a user-friendly and lightweight PyPI library at: https://pypi.org/project/onboard-methane-detection/, alongside all experimental code, models, and data at: https://github.com/zaitra/methane-filters-benchmark.

</details>


### [90] [Investigating Adversarial Robustness of Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03713)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大语言模型的对抗鲁棒性，发现大规模多模态对抗预训练是关键因素；通过端到端多模态训练整合鲁棒视觉编码器，在强攻击下显著提升性能；同时提出测试时随机变换可作为非鲁棒模型的有效黑盒防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs虽在视觉-语言任务上表现优异，但引入视觉编码器（如CLIP）扩大了攻击面，使其易受视觉对抗扰动攻击。现有防御方法因强制保持与CLIP嵌入空间对齐而限制了鲁棒性提升，亟需系统性研究如何有效提升MLLMs的对抗鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 提出诊断性CLIP对齐协议，预判哪些鲁棒视觉编码器能有效迁移至多模态场景；2) 将大规模多模态对抗预训练的编码器通过端到端多模态训练整合到MLLMs中；3) 对非鲁棒MLLM采用轻量级测试时视觉随机变换作为黑盒防御。

Result: 端到端训练相比基线在强攻击下，图像描述CIDEr提升28分，VQA准确率提升11.7%；从鲁棒骨干进行端到端对抗训练额外提升1.9 CIDEr和4.3% VQA准确率；测试时随机变换使非鲁棒模型从近乎零性能提升至与鲁棒模型相当的水平；鲁棒模型显著降低白盒越狱攻击下的有毒生成。

Conclusion: 多模态对抗预训练的规模而非单模态规模是鲁棒性迁移的关键；鲁棒视觉表征是实现MLLM对抗鲁棒性的严格先决条件；测试时随机变换为非鲁棒模型提供了有效的轻量级防御方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on vision-language tasks, but incorporating visual inputs through a vision encoder (e.g., CLIP) substantially expands the attack surface, making these models vulnerable to visual adversarial perturbations. Prior defenses typically preserve compatibility with pretrained MLLMs by enforcing strict alignment to CLIP's original embedding space during adversarial fine-tuning; while practical, this constraint fundamentally limits achievable robustness. We present a systematic investigation of adversarial robustness in MLLMs. We first introduce a diagnostic CLIP-alignment protocol that predicts, prior to full MLLM training, which robust vision encoders will transfer effectively to the multimodal setting, revealing that large-scale multimodal adversarial pretraining, rather than unimodal scale alone, is the critical factor for strong robustness transfer. Integrating such encoders into MLLMs via end-to-end multimodal training yields average gains of 28 CIDEr points on captioning and 11.7% VQA accuracy under strong adversarial attacks compared to constrained plug-and-play baselines. We further show that adversarial training applied directly to a standard non-robust MLLM degrades both clean and adversarial performance, establishing robust visual representations as a strict prerequisite, while end-to-end adversarial training from a robust backbone delivers additional gains of 1.9 CIDEr points and 4.3% VQA accuracy. Beyond training-time defenses, lightweight test-time visual stochastic transformations serve as an effective black-box defense for non-robust MLLMs, elevating adversarial performance from near-zero to levels comparable with robust models. Finally, we show that our robust models substantially reduce toxic generation under white-box visual jailbreak attacks. Code and pretrained weights will be released publicly.

</details>


### [91] [Text-to-Image Models Need Less from Text Encoders Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03715)
*Nurit Spingarn,Noa Cohen,Tamar Rott Shaham,Tomer Michaeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到图像扩散Transformer模型在生成图像时，实际只依赖文本表示中两个简单的方面：相邻token合并为词表示、以及词序（由位置编码决定），而非完整的上下文语义信息。作者通过构造仅含单个词义和词序的"词袋+位置标签"表示，发现其生成的图像质量与完整文本嵌入引导的结果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究文本到图像模型中，文本嵌入的哪些方面对图像生成是真正必要的。尽管文本编码器编码了丰富的上下文信息（如组合性、属性绑定），但模型是否实际利用了这些信息尚不清楚。

Method: 构造一种仅编码单个词义和词序、但不包含任何上下文信息的"带位置标签的词袋"（bag of position-tagged words）文本嵌入，与完整文本嵌入进行图像生成效果的对比实验。

Result: 该简化表示生成的图像在视觉质量和文本忠实度上与完整文本嵌入引导的结果相当，表明模型并未利用文本嵌入中的丰富上下文信息。

Conclusion: 文本到图像模型通常不使用文本嵌入中超出单个词义和词序的丰富信息；复杂的语言结构解析实际上是由图像模型自身完成的。

Abstract: Text-to-image models rely on text prompts as their primary interface to human intent. Prompts are encoded by a text encoder into embeddings that condition the image generation process. Beyond individual token meanings, text embeddings encode contextual information across the full prompt, such as compositionality and attribute binding. However, whether image models actually exploit this richer information remains underexplored. Here, we address the question: Which aspects of text representation are essential for image generation? We show that text-to-image diffusion transformer-based models commonly rely only on two relatively straightforward aspects of text representations: (i) the merging of adjacent tokens into a word representation, for words spanning multiple tokens, and (ii) word order, which is imprinted by the positional embedding of the text-encoder. To show this, we construct a new text embedding that encodes only individual word meanings and order but lacks any contextual information about the full prompt. We find that this bag of position-tagged words representation is sufficient to successfully guide image generation, achieving visual quality and text fidelity that are on par with full text embedding-guided generation. This demonstrates that, contrary to common belief, text-to-image models often do not use the rich information encoded in the text embedding beyond individual word meanings and word order. Instead, the decoding of complex linguistic structures is performed by the image model itself. Project webpage: https://nsping13.github.io/contextless-TTI/

</details>


### [92] [Beyond False Stability: High-Noise Drift Gating for Test-Time Adversarial Defenses in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03730)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现CLIP模型在对抗样本的表示空间中存在一个噪声强度转变点：弱噪声下对抗样本异常稳定（虚假稳定），强噪声下对抗样本比干净样本更不稳定。基于此设计了一个无需训练的漂移门控机制，选择性触发测试时防御，显著改善了干净准确率与鲁棒性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时防御（如随机噪声聚合、噪声锚点插值等）虽能提升鲁棒性，但往往以牺牲干净准确率为代价，导致干净的-鲁棒的权衡不佳。作者希望找到一种方法，在不影响干净样本性能的前提下，仅在必要时触发防御机制。

Method: 1) 分析CLIP表示空间中的噪声强度转变现象：弱噪声下对抗样本表现出虚假稳定性，强噪声下对抗样本特征漂移显著大于干净样本。2) 基于此提出漂移门控机制（Drift-Gated），以高噪声下的特征漂移量作为轻量级门控信号，仅在检测到类似对抗的不稳定性时才触发现有测试时防御（如Counterattack或Noise-Anchoring）。

Result: 在13个数据集上一致改善了干净-鲁棒权衡。8个细粒度数据集：Counterattack防御下干净+对抗平均准确率从65.7%提升到71.4%；Noise-Anchoring从68.4%提升到73.2%。ImageNet及4个分布偏移变体：从56.1%提升到66.2%（Counterattack），从62.1%提升到67.6%（Noise-Anchoring）。

Conclusion: CLIP的对抗样本在表示空间中存在可被利用的噪声强度转变特性，基于此设计的漂移门控是一种训练免费、即插即用的高效方案，能显著改善测试时防御的干净-鲁棒权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP show strong zero-shot generalization but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Adversarial training improves robustness but is computationally expensive, motivating test-time defenses. Recent approaches exploit how CLIP's visual representations respond to stochastic perturbations: aggregating predictions across noisy views, constructing Gaussian noise-averaged anchors and interpolating features toward them, or applying counter-perturbations. These strategies improve robustness but often degrade clean accuracy, yielding an unfavorable clean-robust trade-off. We revisit stochastic test-time defenses and identify an underexplored noise-regime transition in CLIP's representation space. Prior work explored perturbations mainly in the weak-noise regime, where adversarial examples can appear unusually stable (false stability). Our analysis shows this reverses as perturbation strength grows: beyond the weak-noise regime, adversarial representations become markedly more unstable than clean ones, giving a clearer separation signal. The transition is consistent across uniform and Gaussian noise, photometric and geometric transforms, datasets, and diverse attacks. It largely disappears in adversarially trained models, suggesting it is tied to the fragile local-basin geometry of adversarial representations in non-robust CLIP. We propose a training-free, plug-in drift-gated mechanism that uses high-noise feature drift as a lightweight gating signal to trigger existing test-time defenses only when adversarial-like instability is detected. Across 13 datasets it consistently improves the clean-robust trade-off. On eight fine-grained datasets, mean clean+adversarial accuracy rises from 65.7% to 71.4% for counterattack defenses and 68.4% to 73.2% for noise-anchoring; on ImageNet and four shifted variants, from 56.1% to 66.2% and 62.1% to 67.6%.

</details>


### [93] [Qwen-Image-Flash: Beyond Objective Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03746)
*Tianhe Wu,Kun Yan,Zikai Zhou,Lihan Jiang,Jiahao Li,Jie Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Ningyuan Tang,Shengming Yin,Xiaoyue Chen,Xiao Xu,Yilei Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yan Shu,Yixian Xu,Yanran Zhang,Zihao Liu,Zhendong Wang,Zekai Zhang,Deqing Li,Liang Peng,Yi Wang,Jingren Zhou,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了少步蒸馏（few-step distillation）在视觉生成模型加速中的训练策略，以Qwen-Image-2.0为例，系统研究了数据构成、教师指导和任务混合三个因素，提出了Qwen-Image-Flash模型，表明有效的少步蒸馏不仅需要精心设计的损失函数，还需要对整体训练流程进行原则性组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作主要集中在蒸馏目标（loss objectives）的设计上，但忽视了训练流程（training recipe）对模型性能的关键塑造作用。本文旨在从这一互补视角出发，探索数据、教师信号和任务组合对少步蒸馏效果的影响。

Method: 以Qwen-Image-2.0为代表性案例，系统研究了三个因素：（1）数据组成（data composition）；（2）教师指导（teacher guidance）；（3）任务混合（task mixture）。涵盖统一文本到图像生成和指令引导的图像编辑蒸馏任务。

Result: 发现了一些非直观的行为（non-obvious behaviors），并基于这些发现开发了Qwen-Image-Flash模型。实验表明，有效少步蒸馏需要兼顾设计良好的目标和原则性组织的训练流程。

Conclusion: 少步蒸馏的成功不仅依赖于蒸馏目标的设计，更依赖于对整个训练pipeline的合理组织。训练策略（数据、教师信号、任务混合）在少步蒸馏中扮演着关键角色。

Abstract: Few-step distillation has become an effective strategy for accelerating advanced visual generative models, yet prior work has largely focused on distillation objectives. In this work, we revisit few-step distillation from a complementary perspective, focusing on the training recipe that critically shapes student performance. Using Qwen-Image-2.0 as a representative case, we systematically investigate three factors in unified text-to-image generation and instruction-guided image editing distillation: data composition, teacher guidance, and task mixture. Our empirical analysis reveals several non-obvious behaviors, which motivate the development of Qwen-Image-Flash. Overall, our results suggest that effective few-step distillation requires not only carefully designed objectives, but also principled organization of the broader training pipeline.

</details>


### [94] [Ultralytics YOLO26: Unified Real-Time End-to-End Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03748)
*Glenn Jocher,Jing Qiu,Mengyu Liu,Shuai Lyu,Fatih Cagatay Akyon,Muhammet Esat Kalfaoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YOLO26是一个统一的实时视觉模型家族，通过无NMS的端到端推理、移除DFL的轻量检测头、MuSGD优化器、渐进损失和STAL标签分配等创新，在COCO数据集上实现40.9-57.5 mAP，速度1.7-11.8 ms，覆盖检测/分割/姿态/分类/旋转检测等多个任务，并扩展了开放词汇版本YOLOE-26。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有YOLO检测器存在三大痛点：推理时依赖NMS、因Distribution Focal Loss导致检测头过重、小目标可能缺乏正标签分配。作者旨在设计一个统一、高效、无需长训练周期且硬件部署友好的实时视觉模型。

Method: 1) 双头设计实现无NMS端到端推理；2) 完全移除DFL，使用轻量头并允许无约束回归范围；3) 训练管线引入MuSGD（混合Muon-SGD优化器）、渐进损失（逐渐向推理头转移监督）、STAL（小目标正标签分配策略）；4) 支持检测/分割/姿态/分类/旋转检测的任务特定头和损失设计；5) 开放词汇扩展YOLOE-26支持文本/视觉/无提示推理。

Result: YOLO26在COCO上达到40.9-57.5 mAP，T4 TensorRT延迟1.7-11.8 ms，超越此前实时检测器的精度-延迟Pareto前沿；YOLOE-26x在LVIS minival上文本提示达到40.6 AP。

Conclusion: YOLO26通过架构与训练协同优化，在不牺牲速度的前提下显著提升了精度和多任务泛化能力，为实时视觉模型设立了新的标杆。

Abstract: Real-time vision demands models that are accurate, efficient, and simple to deploy across diverse hardware. The YOLO family has become widely deployed for this reason, yet most YOLO detectors still rely on non-maximum suppression at inference, carry heavy detection heads due to Distribution Focal Loss, require long training schedules, and can leave the smallest objects without positive label assignments. We present Ultralytics YOLO26, a unified real-time vision model family that addresses these limitations through coordinated architecture and training advances. YOLO26 uses a dual-head design for native NMS-free end-to-end inference and removes DFL entirely, yielding a lighter head with unconstrained regression range. Its training pipeline combines MuSGD, a hybrid Muon-SGD optimizer adapted from large language model training; Progressive Loss, which shifts supervision toward the inference-time head; and STAL, a label assignment strategy that guarantees positive coverage for small objects. Beyond detection, YOLO26 introduces task-specific head and loss designs for instance segmentation, pose estimation, and oriented detection, producing consistent gains across tasks and scales. The family spans five scales (n/s/m/l/x) and supports detection, instance segmentation, pose estimation, classification, and oriented detection in a single pipeline, with an open-vocabulary extension, YOLOE-26, for text-, visual-, and prompt-free inference. Across all scales, YOLO26 achieves 40.9-57.5 mAP on COCO at 1.7-11.8 ms T4 TensorRT latency, advancing the accuracy-latency Pareto front over prior real-time detectors, while YOLOE-26x reaches 40.6 AP on LVIS minival under text prompting. Code and models are available at https://github.com/ultralytics/ultralytics.

</details>


### [95] [AmbientEye: A Dataset for Pupil Segmentation under Natural Ambient Infrared Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03774)
*Mingyu Han,Hyunyoung Han,Nitheekulawatn Thommakoon,Gangtae Park,Jieun Han,Xucong Zhang,Ian Oakley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AmbientEye数据集，研究在纯户外自然光（无主动红外光源）条件下的瞳孔检测可行性。数据集包含来自35位参与者的260万张眼图，实验显示现有算法在该场景下性能从0.928大幅下降至0.767，揭示了无源红外眼动追踪的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼动追踪系统依赖主动红外照明，导致全天候户外使用功耗高、实用性受限。作者希望探索仅利用环境阳光作为唯一光源的被动红外相机能否实现可靠的瞳孔检测，以解决户外使用的能耗瓶颈。

Method: 构建AmbientEye大规模数据集：在户外自然光下采集35名参与者（来自19个国家）的260万张眼图，采用两种离轴相机配置和两种太阳朝向条件。使用SAM2自动分割加人工精修进行高质量标注，并评测现有最优瞳孔分割算法。

Result: 现有SOTA算法在AmbientEye上的瞳孔分割性能仅为0.767，相比在受控红外数据集上的0.928出现显著下降，证实了环境光设置下的巨大挑战。

Conclusion: AmbientEye作为首个面向无源户外眼动追踪的基准数据集，揭示了纯环境光场景与受控红外场景之间的性能鸿沟，为未来户外全天候眼动追踪研究提供了重要参考和挑战基准。

Abstract: Eye tracking is essential for smart glasses, as it provides insight into user attention for ambient intelligence applications. However, most existing eye-tracking systems rely on active infrared (IR) illumination, creating practical barriers to all-day outdoor use due to power consumption. In this paper, we investigate whether passive IR cameras alone, without any active IR light source, can enable reliable pupil detection in unconstrained outdoor environments, where ambient sunlight serves as the sole illumination source. To support this investigation, we introduce AmbientEye, a large-scale dataset of 2,606,225 eye images collected from 35 participants from 19 countries. It is captured outdoors under natural sunlight with two off-axis camera configurations and two sun-orientation conditions. We provide high-quality pupil annotation through SAM2 automatic segmentation, followed by refinement by human annotators. We benchmark a state-of-the-art pupil segmentation algorithm on our dataset and compare its performance with that on existing datasets under controlled IR illumination. Results reveal a substantial drop in pupil segmentation performance from 0.928 on controlled IR datasets to 0.767 on AmbientEye. This performance gap highlights the challenge of the ambient-light setting. This positions AmbientEye as a first benchmark for an unexplored and highly practical eye-tracking scenario.

</details>


### [96] [SLU-2K: A Question-Based Benchmark for Semantic Evaluation of Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03788)
*Zeno Testa,Antonino Furnari,Lorenzo Baraldi,Natalia Díaz-Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SLU-2K 数据集，通过问答形式评估手语理解系统的语义正确性，发现现有翻译系统（BLEU/ROUGE 高分）在实际语义理解上表现较差（56.7%-75.2%），MLLM 仅接近随机水平，表明当前评估指标高估了真实理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语翻译（SLT）评估指标（如 BLEU、ROUGE）仅关注词汇重叠，无法衡量语义是否真正被理解，这与辅助技术的应用目标不符。因此，研究者希望将焦点从手语翻译转向手语理解（SLU），强调语义层面的评估。

Method: 1. 提出 SLU-2K 数据集，包含 2,350 个基于 PHOENIX-2014T 和 CSL-Daily 的视频问答对；2. 设计自动化数据生成流水线，覆盖动作、位置、数字、物体、人物、时间、天气七类问题；3. 使用 MLLM 和两个 SOTA 系统（MMSTL、SpaMo）进行评测。

Result: MLLM 性能接近随机；SOTA 翻译系统（微调后）准确率仅 56.7%-75.2%，存在显著的语义鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前 SLT 评估协议高估了真实理解能力，未来进展不应仅以流畅度和 n-gram 重叠衡量，还应包括语义正确性。

Abstract: Sign Language Translation (SLT) is typically evaluated with surface-form metrics such as BLEU and ROUGE, which reward lexical overlap but do not directly measure whether a translation preserves the meaning of the source sign sequence. This is in contrast with the final objective of integrating SLT in assistive technology. In this work, we shift the focus from Sign Language Translation (SLT) to Sign Language Understanding (SLU), with particular emphasis on semantic understanding. Specifically, we evaluate systems based on their ability to correctly recover, from the input video, key semantic aspects of the original sentence, such as actions taking place and facts about people and objects. To enable this evaluation systematically, we propose SLU-2K, a dataset of 2,350 closed-ended video question-answer pairs based on the popular PHOENIX-2014T and CSL-Daily datasets. To obtain SLU-2K, we propose and extensively evaluate an automated data generation pipeline which produces questions across 7 categories, namely actions, locations, numbers, objects, people, time, and weather conditions. We show the potential of SLU-2K by evaluating popular Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and two representative state-of-the-art systems, MMSTL and SpaMo. Our results show that MLLMs reach near-random performance, highlighting the need for a more systematic integration of SLU in current AI systems. Furthermore, state-of-the-art translation systems carefully fine-tuned on in-domain data still exhibit a substantial semantic gap, with results ranging from 56.7% to 75.2%. These findings suggest that current SLT evaluation protocols overestimate true understanding and that future progress should be measured not only by fluency and n-gram overlap, but also by semantic correctness. Code, prompts, and benchmark files are available at https://github.com/ZenoTsT/SLU-2K

</details>


### [97] [Training-Free Multi-Concept LoRA Composition with Prompt-Aware Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03792)
*Georgios Tsoumplekas,Stella Bounareli,Vasileios Argyriou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于提示感知的权重分配策略（W-Switch和W-Composite），通过根据提示词中触发词的语义影响力动态加权多个LoRA模块的输出，有效解决了多概念文本到图像生成中LoRA组合的干扰问题，并在ComposLoRA测试集上实现了视觉质量、身份保留和组合性的持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法在单概念个性化生成上表现优异，但多概念组合时直接合并多个LoRA权重或输出会导致概念间相互干扰，降低图像质量和参考图像保真度。亟需一种简单有效的多概念定制方法。

Method: 提出提示感知的重要性加权策略：利用各概念对应提示词token的语义影响力，设计两种方法——W-Switch（开关式加权）和W-Composite（复合式加权），在生成过程中动态调整每个LoRA模块的权重。同时提出基于图像的相似度评估框架，通过自动分割生成图像中的概念区域并与真实参考图像对比，衡量身份保留和保真度。

Result: 在ComposLoRA测试集上，该方法在视觉质量、身份保留和组合性三个维度上持续优于现有最先进方法。LLM评估和用户研究进一步验证了方法有效性，并与新提出的定量指标一致。

Conclusion: 提示感知的权重加权策略能有效缓解多LoRA组合时的概念干扰问题，简单而高效。新提出的图像级评估框架为多概念生成提供了更可靠的定量评价方式。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) successfully enables personalization in text-to-image generation by adapting pre-trained diffusion models to specific visual concepts and styles. However, extending such models to multi-concept customization remains challenging. Naively combining multiple LoRA weights or their outputs often leads to interference among concepts, resulting in degraded visual quality and reduced fidelity to the reference images of individual concepts. This paper proposes a simple yet effective approach for multi-concept customization by optimally combining the outputs of multiple LoRA modules. We leverage the relative importance of each concept during generation, as inferred from its corresponding prompt tokens and introduce two methods, W-Switch and W-Composite, that employ a prompt-aware importance weighting strategy in which each LoRA is weighted according to the semantic influence of its trigger words in the target prompt. In addition, we extend existing quantitative evaluation metrics by proposing a new image-based similarity evaluation framework that assesses image fidelity and identity preservation through comparisons between real-world reference images and automatically segmented concept regions from generated images. We evaluate our approach on the ComposLoRA testbed and demonstrate consistent improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods in terms of visual quality, identity preservation and compositionality. Qualitative evaluations, including a Large Language Model (LLM) based assessment and a user study, further validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods and align with the newly introduced quantitative image-based metrics. Our code is available at https://github.com/GeorgeTsoumplekas/Prompt-Aware-Multi-LoRA-Composition.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Compression: Quantifying Spectral Accessibility in Vision Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03795)
*Akayou A. Kitessa,Yijun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过残差频谱损失（RSL）分析视觉-语言模型中投影层对视觉信息结构的频谱可访问性变化，发现频谱变化呈非单调轨迹，中间层峰值后下降，CLIP的投影频谱中性，DINOv2的[CLS]池化引入结构化频谱损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型通过投影层将视觉特征映射到共享嵌入空间，但这些变换如何改变视觉信息的结构尚不清楚。作者希望系统性地理解投影操作对视觉表示频谱特性的影响。

Method: 提出残差频谱损失（RSL），通过比较模型表示与维度匹配的随机投影基线之间的频谱能量线性可恢复性，隔离降维效应。使用预训练冻结模型，在ImageNet和MS-COCO数据集上分析CLIP和DINOv2。

Result: 频谱可访问性沿深度呈非单调轨迹，中间层峰值后下降。CLIP的投影变换频谱中性，变化可被压缩解释；DINOv2的[CLS]池化引入跨频谱的结构化损失。

Conclusion: 中间层和池化机制是现代视觉编码器中频谱变换的主要驱动因素，不同架构（CLIP vs DINOv2）在最终投影变换上存在根本差异。

Abstract: Vision-language models map visual features into a shared embedding space through learned projection layers, yet it remains unclear how these transformations alter the structure of visual information. This study examines changes in representation through spatial-frequency accessibility, measured by the linear recoverability of band-limited Fourier energy from model representations. To isolate effects beyond dimensionality reduction, we introduce Residual Spectral Loss (RSL), which evaluates changes relative to a dimension-matched random projection baseline. To reduce confounding effects from optimization, the analysis uses pretrained models with all parameters frozen. The experimental results show consistent frequency-dependent changes in accessibility across CLIP and DINOv2 on ImageNet and MS-COCO datasets. Spectral accessibility follows a non-monotonic trajectory across depth, peaking at intermediate layers before decreasing toward the output representation. The final transformation differs across architectures: CLIP's learned projection is spectrally neutral, with changes explained by compression, whereas DINOv2's [CLS] pooling induces a structured loss across the spectrum. These findings identify intermediate layers and pooling mechanisms as primary drivers of spectral transformation in modern vision encoders.

</details>


### [99] [Template Collapse and Information-Theoretic Limits in Camera rPPG Pulse Morphology Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03802)
*Achraf Ben Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 消费级摄像头rPPG无法从单周期波形中恢复个体动脉硬化生物标志物形态，所有16种架构均未能提取个体特异性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究消费级摄像头人脸视频中的rPPG信号能否编码个体动脉硬化生物标志物的波形形态特征。

Method: 在153名受试者的3个数据集上评估6大类16种架构，引入跨受试者皮尔逊r区分个体特异性恢复与模板坍塌。

Result: 无架构恢复个体特异性形态（cross-subject r范围0.773-0.9999，真实天花板0.601）；SupCon收敛到log N=4.844；VAE解码器恢复群体级谐波内容（H2/H1：输出0.310 vs输入0.275），零样本泛化到UBFC（r=+0.708），方向性幻觉间隙p=0.150。

Conclusion: 消费级摄像头无法编码个体动脉形态；cross-subject r是波形重建基准的必要崩溃诊断指标。

Abstract: Objective: Consumer face camera remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables passive cardiovascular monitoring, but whether single-cycle waveform morphology encoding arterial stiffness biomarkers is recoverable from this measurement has not been characterised.
  Methods: We evaluated 16 architectures spanning six families on 153 subjects across three datasets, introducing cross-subject Pearson r to distinguish subject-specific recovery from template collapse.
  Results: No architecture recovered subject-specific morphology (cross-subject r range 0.773--0.9999; ground-truth ceiling 0.601). Supervised Contrastive (SupCon) converged to log N = 4.844, constituting the strongest available empirical evidence that no discriminative morphological structure is extractable from single-cycle rPPG by the encoder families tested. The VAE decoder restores population-level harmonic content absent from the rPPG input (H2/H1: 0.310 output vs. 0.275 input), generalising zero-shot to UBFC (r = +0.708); a directional hallucination gap (p = 0.150) suggests partial signal reading. Anti-collapse objectives fail when input carries no discriminative structure.
  Significance: Consumer cameras cannot encode individual arterial morphology; cross-subject r is a necessary collapse diagnostic for waveform reconstruction benchmarks.

</details>


### [100] [TeX-1500: A Paired Real-World LWIR Hyperspectral Dataset and Benchmark for Temperature-Emissivity-Texture Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03806)
*Cheng Dai,Jiale Lin,Hongyi Xu,Bingxuan Song,Ziyang Xie,Fanglin Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了TeX-1500数据集，包含1,522对真实场景的长波红外高光谱成像与温度-发射率-纹理（TeX）分解配对数据，并提供了TeX-UNet基线方法，为数据驱动的热物理属性感知建立了可量化的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TeX分解方法主要依赖场景特定的逆求解器，缺乏配对的长波红外高光谱成像-TeX监督数据，限制了基于学习的分解方法发展。

Method: 构建了TeX-1500数据集（1,522对校准真实场景数据），覆盖多个地点、季节、时间和传感器；提出TeX-UNet波长感知基线模型，将校准HSI波段与波长位置映射到TeX场。

Result: 在DARPA IH推扫场景及零样本/少样本迁移到FTIR场景上的实验表明，TeX-1500提供了可用的配对监督和可测量的基准。

Conclusion: TeX-1500填补了学习型TeX分解缺乏配对数据的空白，为数据驱动的物理属性热感知提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Temperature-emissivity-texture (TeX) decomposition seeks to recover object heat state, material spectral response, and visible-like geometric texture from long-wave infrared hyperspectral imaging (LWIR HSI). Existing TeX pipelines are mainly scene-specific inverse solvers, and the lack of paired LWIR HSI-TeX supervision has limited learning-based decomposition. To address this gap, we introduce TeX-1500, a large-scale paired LWIR HSI-TeX dataset and benchmark for supervised HSI-to-TeX decomposition. TeX-1500 contains 1,522 calibrated real-scene pairs from DARPA Invisible Headlights (DARPA IH) pushbroom imagery and our FTIR acquisitions, covering five locations, four seasons, diverse acquisition times, heterogeneous wavelength layouts, and two sensor families. Each sample stores a calibrated valid-band radiance cube, calibrated wavelength positions, and aligned temperature, emissivity, and texture supervision constructed through a consistent restoration and TeX-construction protocol. We further provide TeX-UNet, a simple wavelength-aware baseline that maps calibrated HSI bands and wavelength positions to TeX fields. Experiments on the held-out DARPA IH pushbroom scenes and zero-/few-shot transfer to FTIR scenes show that TeX-1500 provides usable paired supervision and a measurable benchmark for data-driven physical-property-centered thermal perception.

</details>


### [101] [Conditional Latent Diffusion Model with Fourier-based Motion Modelling for Virtual Population Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03827)
*Shaokun Lan,Haoran Dou,Jinghan Huang,Arezoo Zakeri,Fengming Lin,Zherui Zhou,Jinming Duan,Alejandro F. Frangi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了4D F-MeshLDM，一种用于生成3D+t心脏网格序列的条件生成框架，结合卷积网格VAE、傅里叶级数参数化运动与扩散先验，在UK Biobank数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网格生成器多关注静态解剖，而序列模型缺乏显式周期性，无法满足心血管虚拟试验对动态4D解剖数据的需求。

Method: 1) 卷积网格VAE编码网格；2) 用截断傅里叶级数参数化运动的结构潜空间；3) 扩散先验学习傅里叶系数令牌的潜分布；4) 通过仿射调制以临床协变量条件化扩散过程。

Result: 在5,000例UK Biobank数据上，解剖逼真度优于SOTA基线，周期闭合误差接近零，生成的队列能准确保留临床功能指标。

Conclusion: 该框架为可靠的心血管虚拟试验提供了可控、周期一致的4D解剖生成能力，具有重要的应用潜力。

Abstract: In-silico trials of medical devices require the generation of virtual populations of anatomies. In cardiovascular applications, virtual anatomy is typically represented as a 3D+t mesh sampled from a generative model. However, most existing mesh generators focus on static anatomy, while sequence models often lack explicit periodicity. To this end, we propose 4D F-MeshLDM, a conditional generative framework comprising a convolutional mesh VAE to encode meshes, a structural latent space that parameterises motion using a truncated Fourier series, and a diffusion prior that learns the latent distribution over Fourier coefficient tokens. By conditioning the diffusion process on clinical covariates via affine modulation, we enable controllable synthesis. Sampling tokens and performing inverse Fourier synthesis yield cycle-consistent latent trajectories, which can be decoded into 3D+t cardiac mesh sequences. Experiments on 5,000 UK Biobank subjects demonstrate that 4D F-MeshLDM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in anatomical fidelity and achieves near-zero cycle closure error. Furthermore, the generated cohorts accurately preserve clinical functional indices, highlighting the potential of our framework for reliable in-silico cardiac trials.

</details>


### [102] [Where Do We (Not) Need Temporal Context in Low-Resource Video Task Adaptation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03837)
*Luc P. J. Sträter,Hazel Doughty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视频理解中参数高效微调(PEFT)和探针方法的适配策略，重点分析了时间上下文在骨干网络、PEFT和探针之间的分配问题，并在有限数据场景下提供了新的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然PEFT在视频理解中因标注和计算成本高而具有吸引力，但视频PETF主要集中在适配图像预训练模型，而标准PEFT也可用于视频表示。这两种设置很少被比较，且都将时间推理限制在模型的单一组件中，忽视了时间上下文应如何分布在骨干网络、PEFT和探针之间的问题。

Method: 系统研究了模型适配策略，在关注外观、关注运动和空间密集三种设置下进行评估，特别关注数据有限场景。

Result: 提供了PEFT和探针在不同设置下的新见解，证明了时间上下文分配对于有效视频适配的重要性。

Conclusion: 时间上下文在骨干网络、PEFT和探针之间的合理分配对视频理解中的有效适配至关重要，不同设置下需要不同的分配策略。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and probing enable adaptation of foundation models using only a small number of trainable parameters, making it attractive for video understanding where annotation and computation are expensive. However, video PEFT has focused on adapting image-pretrained models, while standard PEFT methods can also be applied to video representations. These settings are rarely compared and both confine temporal reasoning to a single component of the model, leaving open how temporal context should be distributed across backbone, PEFT and probe. In this work we provide a systematic study of model adaptation strategies for video understanding. We evaluate methods across appearance-focused, motion-focused and spatially dense settings, with a particular focus on scenarios with limited data where parameter-efficiency is most beneficial. Our results provide new insights into PEFT and probing across settings and demonstrate the importance of temporal context allocation for effective video adaptation

</details>


### [103] [Unified Video-Action Joint Denoising for Dexterous Action and Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03868)
*Dingrui Wang,YuAn Wang,Jinkun Liu,Yue Zhang,Mattia Piccinini,Yu Sun,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Donk提出了一种统一的视频-动作去噪模型，通过保持更宽的分布（联合建模交互视频与手部轨迹），在多种条件下生成灵巧手操作策略，同时提升轨迹精度、视频保真度和文本条件动作生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型将视频基础模型的对齐先验窄化为仅对未来动作的条件策略分布，限制了分布的多样性。作者希望从分布视角重新审视对齐问题，保持更宽的分布以更好地利用视觉-动力学先验。

Method: 提出Donk，一个统一的视频-动作去噪模型。在语言、初始图像和初始手部状态条件下，采样未来视频和双手MANO轨迹作为动作策略；无图像条件时，同一架构从文本条件分布生成配对视频-动作数据，将对齐的视频先验转化为数据引擎。

Result: 在动作、视频和文本生成三项评估中，Donk提升了灵巧手轨迹精度，保持了强视频保真度，并在同一统一训练方案下生成了流畅的文本条件动作轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过保持更宽的分布建模（联合视频-动作空间）并在多种条件下统一训练，Donk有效改善了灵巧手操作任务中动作生成的准确性和视频生成的一致性，同时具备数据生成能力。

Abstract: Recent world action models leverage video foundation models by aligning broad visual-dynamics priors with executable robot actions. We revisit this alignment from a distributional perspective. Existing formulations typically narrow the aligned prior into an observation-conditioned policy distribution over future actions. In contrast, we keep the distribution broader by modeling the joint space of interaction videos and executable hand trajectories under multiple conditioning regimes. We propose Donk, a unified video-action denoising model for dexterous hands. With language, an initial image, and the initial hand state, Donk samples future videos and bimanual MANO trajectories as an action policy. Without the image condition, the same denoising architecture samples paired video-action rollouts from a text-conditioned distribution, turning the aligned video prior into a data engine. Across action, video, and text-only generation evaluations, Donk improves dexterous trajectory accuracy, preserves strong video fidelity, and produces smooth text-conditioned action rollouts under the same unified training recipe.

</details>


### [104] [Visual Instruction Tuning Aligns Modalities through Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03871)
*Luis Palacios,Lorenzo Basile,Diego Doimo,Alberto Cazzaniga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过探测分析和因果干预，发现视觉指令微调主要将视觉特征嵌入到LLM的中间语义层，而非早期单模态层，并且这种局部化对齐是多模态集成的关键机制，仅微调中间层即可保持视觉任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉指令微调虽能有效将LLM扩展到多模态任务，但视觉特征如何嵌入到LLM的分层抽象结构中尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示这一机制。

Method: 采用探测分析（probing analyses）和因果干预（causal interventions）方法，对比视觉与文本语义表征的几何结构，并设计仅微调中间层的消融实验。

Result: 1) 指令微调将视觉特征直接桥接到LLM中间语义层，绕过早期单模态层；2) 中间层是多模态处理的语义核心；3) 微调扩展并增强了现有的抽象阶段，使视觉特征与已有文本特征对齐；4) 仅微调中间层即可达到与全微调相当的视觉任务性能。

Conclusion: 多模态集成是LLM内部抽象引擎局部化复用（repurposing）的结果，而非全局性的参数调整。

Abstract: Visual instruction tuning effectively adapts a pre-trained Large Language Model (LLM) to process image information alongside text. Yet, it remains unclear how visual features are embedded into the layer-wise hierarchy of abstractions of the LLM backbone. Across a diverse set of vision-language architectures, we show that instruction tuning primarily serves as a bridge, embedding visual features directly into the intermediate semantic layers of the LLM, bypassing the early layers devoted to unimodal processing. With probing analyses and causal interventions, we show that these intermediate layers are the semantic core of vision-language processing and play a critical role in the performance on a broad set of multimodal benchmarks. In addition, by comparing the geometry of semantically equivalent visual and textual representations, we find that fine-tuning extends and strengthens the existing abstraction phase, aligning visual features with pre-existing textual ones. Finally, we confirm the functional role of this localized alignment by restricting fine-tuning to intermediate layers alone: this strategy preserves the performance of full fine-tuning on vision-centric benchmarks while reducing training time. Our results suggest that multimodal integration is a localized phenomenon driven by the repurposing of the internal abstraction engine of the LLM.

</details>


### [105] [Seg2Track++: Probabilistic Track Validation and Data Association for Multi-Object Tracking and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03875)
*Diogo Mendonça,Tiago Barros,Cristiano Premebida,Urbano J. Nunes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Seg2Track++将SAM2分割模型与新颖的跟踪管理模块结合，通过掩码质心距离、置信感知成本调制和概率跟踪验证等技术，实现了零样本的多目标跟踪与分割，在KITTI MOTS数据集上表现出更好的身份保持能力和更低的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统需要鲁棒的多目标跟踪与分割能力来应对动态环境，而SAM2等基础模型虽然分割能力强，但在直接应用于MOTS时存在跟踪关联不可靠和误报传播的问题。

Method: Seg2Track++框架集成SAM2实例分割与新型跟踪管理模块，使用掩码质心距离(MCD)和置信感知成本调制(CCM)进行跟踪关联，并通过概率跟踪验证(PTV)采用伯努利滤波器验证轨迹存在性、抑制幽灵轨迹。

Result: 在KITTI MOTS数据集上，该方法无需微调即可实现更优的身份保持、更少的误报传播和鲁棒的跟踪管理。

Conclusion: Seg2Track++通过零样本方式有效解决了SAM2直接应用在MOTS中的跟踪关联和误报问题，为动态环境下的自主系统提供了一种无需微调的鲁棒解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems require robust Multi-Object Tracking and Segmentation (MOTS) to operate reliably in dynamic environments, ensuring consistent object identities and precise mask-level delineation. Foundation models such as SAM2 have shown strong zero-shot generalization for segmentation, but their direct application to MOTS is limited by unreliable track association and false-positive propagation. This work introduces Seg2Track++, a framework that integrates instance segmentation with SAM2 and a novel track management module to perform zero-shot MOTS with enhanced temporal consistency. Tracks are associated using Mask Centroid Distance (MCD) and Confidence-Aware Cost Modulation (CCM), while Probabilistic Track Validation (PTV) employs a Bernoulli filter to validate track existence and suppress ghost tracks. Experimental results on KITTI MOTS demonstrate improved identity preservation, reduced false-positive propagation, and robust track management without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [106] [MLP Splatting: Object-Centric Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03877)
*Shinjeong Kim,Yuzhou Cheng,Xin Kong,Paul H. J. Kelly,Andrew J. Davison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MLP-Splatting提出用多个紧凑的MLP作为独立光场基元（而非高斯基元或全局辐射场），在实现照片级新视角合成的同时，天然支持场景分解和物体级编辑，无需额外分割，且内存仅1/15、渲染速度提升3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D表示（如3DGS、NeRF）虽能实现高质量新视角合成，但难以自然分解场景元素为少量基元，进行物体级操作时需要额外的分割或聚类步骤。

Method: 将场景表示为多个独立的紧凑MLP，每个MLP具有局部空间支持，独立预测辐射度和不透明度；渲染时通过稀疏体素合成（ray-primitive interactions）高效完成；仅需RGB监督即可训练，可选语义特征蒸馏支持开放词汇交互。

Result: 相比SOTA语义3DGS方法，内存占用降至1/15，渲染速度提升3倍；无需分割掩码即可实现物体级编辑；支持开放集实例分割和开放词汇场景交互。

Conclusion: MLP-Splatting以局部神经基元替代高斯基元或全局辐射场，兼顾表达能力和局部性，在保真度、编辑能力和效率间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: 3D representations are fundamental to scene rendering, understanding, and interaction. Recent approaches, such as 3D Gaussian Splatting and Neural Radiance Fields, achieve impressive photorealistic novel-view synthesis, but lack the ability to easily decompose scene elements into a few primitives, requiring additional segmentation or grouping for object-level manipulation. We present MLP-Splatting, a method that enables scene decomposition via a few expressive light-field primitives while providing photorealistic novel-view synthesis.
  MLP-Splatting models each primitive as an independent compact MLP with localized spatial support that predicts radiance and opacity. In contrast to low-level Gaussian primitives or a single global radiance field, our neural primitives provide greater expressive capacity while remaining spatially localized. Rendering is performed through efficient sparse volumetric compositing over ray-primitive interactions.
  Our primitives are supervised using RGB supervision alone, which yields primitives that represent local scene regions often corresponding to objects or object parts, enabling interactive object-level editing without segmentation masks by selecting a handful of primitives. Our method, augmented with optional semantic feature distillation, enables open-vocabulary scene interaction and open-set instant segmentation. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, we achieve substantially lower memory usage (1/15$\times$) and faster rendering (3$\times$), as we show in our experiments compared to semantic 3DGS methods. Project Page: https://shinjeongkim.com/mlp-splatting

</details>


### [107] [Beyond Encoder Accumulation: Measuring Encoder Roles in Multi-Encoder VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03879)
*Wei Ding,Yudong Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Xingwu Sun,Jiansheng Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过在统一流水线下重新训练和评估31种视觉编码器子集（约20k GPU小时），揭示了多编码器LVLM设计的三个关键发现：1）从零重新训练与固定检查点掩码得到的编码器排名不同；2）提出容量-必要性两个正交轴，发现高容量锚点+自适应互补编码器配对可匹配全五编码器性能；3）预投影器有效秩可解释残差分数变化，最优配对需锚点秩保持+互补秩扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型趋向融合异构视觉流，但当前缺乏理解多编码器联合训练中交互机制的工具，且训练前难以识别参数高效的编码器配置。

Method: 在Cambrian-1基准套件（16个基准）上，对五种常见视觉编码器的所有31个非空子集进行统一流水线的重新训练和评估（约20k GPU小时），提出容量-必要性分解和预投影器有效秩分析。

Result: 1）重新训练的排名与固定检查点掩码结果不同；2）容量和必要性是两正交轴，高容量锚点+自适应互补配对可匹配全模型，额外编码器仅带来边际收益；3）预投影器有效秩可解释残差分数变化，最优配对需锚点秩保持+互补秩扩张。

Conclusion: 容量-必要性分解和预投影器秩分析揭示了多编码器LVLM设计的方法论差距，并为弥合这一差距提供了具体原语。

Abstract: As foundation models scale toward fusing more heterogeneous visual streams, understanding how diverse encoders interact under joint training becomes a prerequisite for principled design. Yet large vision-language models (LVLMs) currently lack the tools to do so, and parameter-efficient encoder configurations remain hard to identify before training. To re-examine encoder roles under joint training, on the 16-benchmark Cambrian-1 suite we retrain and evaluate all 31 non-empty subsets of five common vision encoders under a unified pipeline (~20k GPU-hours total), and report three findings. First, retraining each subset from scratch reveals encoder rankings that differ from those obtained by masking encoders on a fixed checkpoint, including which encoder ranks first overall. Second, we decompose each encoder's contribution into two axes, Capacity, the score an encoder reaches on its own, and Necessity, the drop when it is removed from the full pool. The two axes are not interchangeable. Pairing the two highest-Capacity encoders is suboptimal, while pairing a high-Capacity anchor with an adaptive complement matches the full five-encoder model. Adding further encoders beyond this pair yields only marginal gains. Third, at fixed parameter count, per-encoder pre-projector effective rank explains the residual score variation. The strongest pairs combine an anchor whose rank survives joint training with a complement whose rank expands under it, suggesting that higher-rank, less-collapsed projector inputs correspond to a more favorable optimization regime at the encoder-projector interface. Together, the Capacity-Necessity decomposition and the pre-projector rank analysis, along with comprehensive evaluation through retraining, expose a methodological gap in multi-encoder LVLM design, and offer concrete primitives for closing it.

</details>


### [108] [CoralBay: A Self-Supervised CT Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03888)
*Ioannis Gatopoulos,Nicolas Känzig,Sebastian Otálora,Fei Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoralBay是一个面向3D医学影像的自监督学习框架，通过层级化3D Swin骨干网络和自蒸馏机制，学习富含空间信息的体素表征，并在多个下游放射学任务中表现优异。同时贡献了开源3D放射学排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D自监督预训练无法有效建模3D医学影像（如CT）的空间连续性、器官解剖结构和基于强度的组织特性（如亨氏单位），需要专门针对3D体素数据的表征学习方法。

Method: 基于DINO自蒸馏框架，采用层级化3D Swin Transformer作为骨干网络，对拼接的多尺度特征进行自蒸馏，实现数据高效的3D自监督学习。

Result: 在多种下游放射学任务中表现出强且一致的性能，有效迁移到不同解剖目标。

Conclusion: CoralBay成功弥合了2D预训练与3D医学影像之间的差距，为体素表征学习提供了有效方案，并建立了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has enabled large-scale pre-training on 2D natural images, producing general-purpose visual representations that transfer effectively across tasks. However, many medical imaging modalities, such as CT scans, are inherently three-dimensional and differ fundamentally from natural images in both structure and semantics. Volumetric modalities capture spatial continuity, organ anatomy, and intensity-based tissue properties (e.g., Hounsfield Units), which are not adequately modeled by 2D pre-training. To bridge this gap, we introduce CoralBay, a self-distillation framework that extends DINO by using a hierarchical 3D Swin backbone and applying self-distillation to concatenated multi-scale features, enabling data-efficient self-supervised learning of rich spatial representations that encode both global semantics and fine-grained local structure. As a result, CoralBay transfers effectively to a wide range of downstream radiological tasks, demonstrating strong and consistent performance across diverse anatomical targets. In addition, we contribute to the open-source \eva framework by introducing a public, reproducible 3D radiology leaderboard that unifies multiple datasets and establishes a standardized benchmark for evaluating volumetric representation learning methods.

</details>


### [109] [OVO-S-Bench: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Streaming Spatial Intelligence in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03890)
*Yifei Li,Pengyiang Liu,Yuhang Zang,Zhongyue Shi,Qi Fu,Hongye Hao,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVO-S-Bench是一个全新的人工标注流式空间智能基准测试，包含1,680个问题和348个源视频。该基准测试评估多模态大语言模型在流式空间推理能力上的表现。结果表明，最先进的模型Gemini-3.1-Pro落后人类专家27个百分点（59.2 vs 86.6），异中心映射是主要瓶颈，且流式空间微调的MLLMs表现不如其基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试要么在完整视频上进行离线评估，要么针对事件而非空间结构进行评测。然而，机器人、增强现实和自动驾驶领域中的多模态智能体需要从连续的自我中心流式输入中推理空间位置和布局，且常常需要利用当前视野之外的证据。缺乏针对这种流式空间智能的专门评测基准。
