Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillPyramid框架，通过层次化技能拓扑和自进化机制，将技能从静态资源池转变为动态演化系统，在多个环境上平均奖励提升38.0%，执行步骤减少27.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI agent缺乏系统的技能构建、积累与迁移机制，导致不同任务间重复构建相似能力、经验难以转化为可复用资产、任务特定技能难以泛化到新场景。

Method: 提出SkillPyramid框架，基于层次化技能拓扑结构，引入自进化机制，使agent在任务执行过程中能够组合、验证并融入新技能。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld三个环境及四种骨干模型上，平均奖励提升38.0%，执行步骤减少27.7%。

Conclusion: SkillPyramid将技能集合从静态资源池转变为动态演化系统，有效提升了agent的任务泛化能力和执行效率。

Abstract: Recent AI agents can flexibly invoke skills to solve complex tasks, but their long-term improvement is fundamentally constrained by a lack of systematic skill construction, accumulation, and transfer. In particular, without a unified framework for skill consolidation, agents tend to redundantly construct similar capabilities across different tasks, are unable to effectively transform experience into reusable assets, and struggle to generalize task-specific skills to novel scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose SkillPyramid, a skill consolidation framework that reuses existing skill experience for broader task generalization. Operating on a hierarchical skill topology, SkillPyramid further introduces a self-evolution mechanism that enables agents to compose, validate, and incorporate new skills during task execution. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld across four backbone models show that SkillPyramid substantially increases the average reward by 38.0% and reduces execution steps by 27.7%. Overall, our method transforms a skill collection from a static resource pool into a dynamic evolution system.

</details>


### [183] [Dynamic Objective Selection with Safeguards and LLM Oversight for Financial Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03704)
*Keigo Sakurai,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama,Anjyu Anan,Kei Nakagawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DOSS框架，通过分类方法动态选择投资目标函数，结合置信度门控和LLM监管机制，避免频繁切换和误选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定优化目标在市场状态变化时表现次优；隐状态区制切换方法存在噪声、延迟和高换手率问题。

Method: 将目标选择建模为分类问题，滚动窗口更新；引入置信度门控（低置信度退回保守默认）和切换频率约束；LLM仅作为监督组件（接受或否决为安全默认），不生成新目标。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体数值结果，但声称能实现前瞻性选择且无时序泄露。

Conclusion: DOSS在不引入隐状态变量的前提下实现动态目标选择，并通过双层安全机制（置信度门控+LLM监督）提升可靠性和可治理性。

Abstract: Financial decision-making tasks such as stock recommendation and portfolio allocation typically estimate future return and risk and then select trades or allocations for an investor, and the chosen optimization objective often determines realized performance. However, because market conditions evolve over time, a fixed objective can be suboptimal across regimes, while regime-switching pipelines that rely on latent regime estimates can be noisy or delayed and frequent switching can increase turnover and operational instability. In this paper, we propose DOSS (Dynamic Objective Selection with Safeguards), a learning-based selector that directly chooses the decision-relevant objective function at each time point from interpretable statistical summaries of recent returns, selecting among a small set of candidates (e.g., return-seeking, loss-averse, and risk-adjusted) without introducing intermediate regime variables. DOSS formulates objective selection as a classification problem over objectives and performs sequential updates with a rolling window to make forward-looking selections without temporal leakage, while also outputting a confidence score for each proposal. To mitigate misselection and excessive switching in deployment, DOSS applies confidence-aware gating with a fail-safe that overrides low-confidence proposals to a conservative default and enforces explicit controls tied to switching frequency. We further integrate governance by positioning a Large Language Model (LLM) as an oversight component rather than a generator of new objectives: the LLM is restricted to accept a proposed objective or override it to a predefined safe default, with deterministic rule-based constraints triggering overrides when needed.

</details>


### [184] [Code-on-Graph: Iterative Programmatic Reasoning via Large Language Models on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03705)
*Weiwei Ding,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Zhuo Chen,Kun Su,Fei Wang,Xiaolong Jin,Jin Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Code-on-Graph (CoG)框架，通过将KG模式表示为Python类并生成可执行代码进行推理，解决了LLM与KG集成中的灵活性和可扩展性两大瓶颈，在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-KG集成框架依赖预定义操作符检索事实知识并注入prompt，存在两大瓶颈：1) 预定义操作符范围有限，缺乏组合表达能力，无法充分捕获KG问题的复杂语义；2) 直接将大规模事实知识注入prompt限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出CoG框架：1) 在每个推理步骤检索事实知识后，识别对应的KG模式并将其表示为Python类，作为检索事实的抽象接口；2) 基于这些类生成可执行的代码，执行时将检索到的事实实例化为对应类的对象。这种设计实现了灵活的基于代码的推理，同时避免将大规模事实知识直接注入prompt。

Result: 在WebQSP、CWQ和GrailQA三个基准数据集上，CoG相比先前最先进模型性能提升高达10.5%。

Conclusion: CoG通过程序化推理框架有效解决了LLM-KG集成中的灵活性和可扩展性问题，代码生成的方式既增强了复杂语义的捕获能力，又避免了大规模事实知识的prompt注入瓶颈，显著提升了推理性能。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are widely used to mitigate the limitations of Large Language Models (LLMs), such as outdated knowledge and hallucinations. Existing LLM-KG integration frameworks typically rely on predefined operators to retrieve factual knowledge from KGs and inject it into prompts for answer generation. This paradigm faces two critical bottlenecks: 1) Inflexibility: The predefined operators are limited in scope and thus lack sufficient compositional expressiveness to fully capture the complex semantics required by KG questions. 2) Unscalability: Direct injection of factual knowledge into prompts limits scalability in handling large-scale factual knowledge. To address these two bottlenecks, we propose Code-on-Graph (CoG), a programmatic reasoning framework for LLM-KG integration. Specifically, given the factual knowledge retrieved at each reasoning step, CoG first identifies the corresponding KG schemas and represents these schemas as Python classes, which serve as abstract interfaces to the retrieved facts. It then generates executable code grounded in these classes, with the retrieved facts instantiated as objects of the corresponding classes during execution. This design enables flexible code-based reasoning while avoiding the direct injection of large-scale factual knowledge into prompts. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and GrailQA demonstrate that CoG outperforms prior state-of-the-art models by up to 10.5%.

</details>


### [185] [Unveiling the Structure of Do-Calculus Reasoning via Derivation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03719)
*Clément Yvernes,Emilie Devijver,Marianne Clausel,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出推导图来表示do-calculus规则的应用与组合，刻画了所有等价于do-calculus的观测和干预概率空间，并证明最多只需四次规则应用即可完成转化，进而利用等价因果查询生成多个有效估计量，提高估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: do-calculus规则的应用和组合顺序复杂，难以系统性地探索等价干预表达式的空间，缺乏高效的结构化方法来表征和简化推理过程。

Method: 引入推导图（derivation graphs）来表示do-calculus规则的组合与顺序应用；利用图的结构特性证明最多四次规则应用可实现等价转化；将等价因果查询应用于识别算法以生成多个有效估计量。

Result: 推导图清晰刻画了do-calculus下的等价概率空间；最多四次规则应用即可完成等价转化；利用等价因果查询可获得多个有效估计量，进而提升估计效率。

Conclusion: 推导图为do-calculus的推理提供了一种简洁的结构化框架，不仅简化了规则组合的过程，还能通过等价查询生成更高效的因果估计量。

Abstract: The do-calculus defines a general system of inference for interventional queries, allowing causal quantities to be transformed through successive applications of its rules. This process induces a rich space of equivalent interventional expressions, but combining and ordering these rules remains challenging. In this work, we introduce derivation graphs, which represent how do-calculus rules are applied and combined, and characterize the full space of observational and interventional probabilities which are equivalent under the do-calculus. The structure of these graphs yields a simple procedure that uses at most four applications of do-calculus rules. Finally, we show how applying identification algorithms to equivalent causal queries produces multiple valid estimands for the same causal quantity, eventually yielding more efficient estimators.

</details>


### [186] [When to Re-Plan: Subgoal Persistence in Hierarchical Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03741)
*Ayushi Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究潜在推理系统中的稳定性-适应性权衡，提出层级推理模型(HRM)的封建式管理-工人接口。在ARC和ConceptARC任务上发现，子目标持久性周期P=3-6时表现最优，适度周期的内部对齐比过频或过长的规划更有效，揭示了组合规划中"中等范围意图"的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理需要在稳定性和适应性之间取得平衡：重新规划太频繁则计算无法形成多步结构，承诺太久则计划过时。现有潜在推理方法缺乏对这种权衡的系统研究。

Method: 扩展层级推理模型(HRM)，引入封建式管理-工人接口：慢速高层模块周期性发射归一化方向性子目标，持续P个低层步骤，通过内在余弦对齐损失偏置工人的隐藏状态更新。

Result: ARC和ConceptARC上：适度周期P=3-6持续优于极短(P=1)和极长周期，P=3时LM损失最低(1.544 vs 1.674 at P=1, 1.640基线)；5次实验均值1.595，标准差0.045。内在对齐权重λ最优值约0.05。消融实验表明干扰来自学习到的方向性结构，而非架构容量或辅助损失本身。

Conclusion: 潜在推理系统的组合规划设计原则：中等范围意图必须在足够多的计算步骤上保持连贯，以形成组合结构。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires a system to commit to medium-horizon intent without becoming rigid: re-plan too often and computation never coheres into multi-step structure; commit too long and the plan goes stale. We study this stability-adaptivity tradeoff in the latent reasoning setting, where multi-step computation occurs inside hidden state rather than externalized token traces. We extend the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) with a feudal-style manager-worker interface: a slow high-level module periodically emits a normalized directional subgoal that persists for P low-level steps, biasing the worker's hidden-state updates and supplying an intrinsic cosine alignment loss. On ARC and ConceptARC, we find that subgoal persistence -- not subgoal injection alone -- is the central knob: moderate periods P in [3, 6] consistently outperform both very frequent (P=1) and very long horizons, with a clear minimum LM loss at P=3 (1.544 vs. 1.674 at P=1, 1.640 baseline; replicated over 5 seeds at mean 1.595, std 0.045). The intrinsic alignment weight lambda shows a complementary narrow optimum (lambda approximately 0.05). A controlled ablation at past-sweet-spot lambda isolates learned directional structure -- not architectural capacity or auxiliary loss alone -- as the source of interference when the alignment signal exceeds its optimum. Together these findings implicate a design principle for compositional planning in latent reasoning systems: medium-horizon intent must be coherent across enough computational steps for compositional structure to form.

</details>


### [187] [Proof-Refactor: Refactoring Generated Formal Proofs into Modular Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03743)
*Yiming Fu,Peixuan Liu,Zichen Wang,Kun yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Proof-Refactor框架，通过四阶段流程（提取候选证明片段、设计辅助声明、形式化证明、修复原证明）来重构LLM生成的形式化证明，使其更可读、模块化、可维护和可重用，在PutnamBench和Putnam2025数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的正式证明在可读性、模块化、可维护性和可重用性方面远不如成熟数学库中的证明。现有方法多依赖可计算的优化目标（如证明长度），但更高级的代码质量难以量化为自动指标。

Method: 提出Proof-Refactor框架，将证明重构分解为四个阶段：（1）提取候选证明片段（2）设计辅助声明（3）形式化验证提取和设计的组件（4）使用已验证的组件修复原证明。

Result: 在PutnamBench和Putnam2025数据集上，Proof-Refactor在基于rubric的重构评分上显著优于Claude Code基线方法，在签名质量和人类可读性方面提升最大。

Conclusion: 过程引导的重构可以在不将证明长度作为主要目标的情况下改善证明结构，为提升LLM生成证明的质量提供了新的方向。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in generating formal proofs, their outputs often remain less readable, modular, maintainable, and reusable than proofs in mature formal mathematics libraries. We argue that this gap stems in part from the compile-first objective implicit in most proof-generation pipelines, which encourages monolithic or ad hoc proof scripts rather than library-quality artifacts. Existing approaches to proof-quality improvement often rely on explicit, computable optimization objectives. In practice, however, the most tractable and experimentally validated objectives are largely length-based, while higher-level qualities such as readability, modularity, maintainability, and reusability are difficult to reduce to reliable automatic metrics. Instead of optimizing proof improvement against a single proxy metric, we take a process-guided approach inspired by human proof-refactoring workflows. We propose an agentic framework $\textbf{Proof-Refactor}$ that decomposes proof refactoring into four phases: extracting candidate proof fragments, designing helper declarations, formally proving the extracted and designed components, and repairing the original proof using the verified components. On generated Lean proofs from PutnamBench and Putnam2025, Proof-Refactor improves rubric-based refactoring scores over a strong Claude Code refactoring baseline, with the largest gains in signature quality and human readability. These results suggest that process-guided refactoring can improve proof structure without treating proof length as the primary objective.

</details>


### [188] [LAP: An Agent-to-Instrument Protocol for Autonomous Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03755)
*Linwu Zhu,Liqiang Gao,Yan Chen,Dan Zhu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Lab Agent Protocol (LAP)，一个填补大语言模型智能体与物理仪器之间通信空白的协议，补充了现有MCP和A2A协议中缺失的agent-to-instrument边缘，支持有状态、安全关键、独占使用的物理实验操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主科学实验系统从零构建推理智能体与物理仪器之间的连接，因供应商SDK碎片化且标准为确定性软件设计，而现有智能体互操作协议（MCP和A2A）只覆盖了agent-to-tool和agent-to-agent边缘，未建模agent-to-instrument边缘，后者涉及有状态、安全关键、独占、物理有体、含单位/校准/不确定度的测量操作。

Method: LAP保留A2A的对等、发现优先、任务生命周期结构，新增四个物理世界原语：(i) InstrumentCard（带签名的能力与物理限制描述），(ii) 独占仪器与样品锁定的保留机制，(iii) 安全围栏握手协议（操作确认令牌加密绑定到特定任务及参数），(iv) MeasurementResult模式（物理类型化、校准锚定、含不确定度、可复现）。协议指定了角色、六层架构、JSON-RPC方法集、任务与安全状态机、错误模型及跨实验室联邦。

Result: LAP与A2A/MCP生态传输兼容，封装而非替换SiLA 2和OPC-UA等现有设备标准，并通过端到端闭环自主实验流程进行了验证。

Conclusion: LAP填补了自主科学中智能体-仪器通信的关键空白，通过四个物理世界原语将状态性、安全性和物理可复现性融入协议设计，为构建可互操作的自主实验基础设施提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: Autonomous science is moving from demonstration to infrastructure. Large language model agents now plan experiments, and self-driving laboratories execute them. Yet every such system rebuilds the link between the reasoning agent and the physical instrument from scratch, against fragmented vendor SDKs and standards built for deterministic software clients rather than probabilistic, goal-directed agents. Recent agent-interoperability protocols clarify two of the three edges of an agentic ecosystem (Anthropic's Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes the agent-to-tool edge, and Google's Agent2Agent (A2A) the agent-to-agent edge), but neither models the agent-to-instrument edge, where operations are stateful, safety-critical, exclusively owned, physically embodied, and produce measurements with units, calibration, and uncertainty. We present the Lab Agent Protocol (LAP), a protocol design that fills this gap. LAP retains A2A's peer-to-peer, discovery-first, task-lifecycle structure and adds four physical-world primitives: (i) the InstrumentCard, a signed capability and physical-limit description; (ii) first-class reservation for exclusive instrument and sample locking; (iii) a safety-fence handshake with operator-confirmation tokens cryptographically bound to a specific task and its parameters, gating hazardous and irreversible operations; and (iv) a MeasurementResult schema that makes every result physically typed (QUDT/UCUM), calibration-anchored, uncertainty-bearing, and reproducible by construction. We specify roles, a six-layer architecture, the JSON-RPC method set, the task and safety state machines, the error model, and cross-laboratory federation, and walk a closed-loop autonomous campaign through the protocol end-to-end. LAP is transport-compatible with the A2A/MCP ecosystem and encapsulates rather than replaces existing device standards such as SiLA 2 and OPC-UA.

</details>


### [189] [From Control Boundary to Insurance Claim: Reconstructing AI-Mediated Losses Through the CER Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03777)
*Alex Leung,Rex Zhang,Kentaroh Toyoda,SiewMei Loh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CER框架，用于解决生成式和代理式AI系统导致损失后的状态重建问题，区分于传统的事件重建，并评估保险索赔的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统（尤其是生成式和代理式AI）在推理、检索、调用工具和行为过程中状态不断变化，传统的事件重建不足以追溯损失原因，需要状态重建。同时，保险市场缺乏针对AI损失的标准化诊断方法。

Method: 提出CER诊断框架：C（控制边界）——系统是否有可执行的运行边界；E（证据重建）——能否从保留的工件重建系统状态与因果链；R（保险响应）——重建损失是否在保险覆盖范围内，以及是否具备理赔所需的证据。

Result: 论文定义了AI特有的重建问题，通过CER框架将其操作化，并明确了理赔级证据要求。使用PocketOS、Replit代理数据库删除事件和Moffatt诉Air Canada案作为公开案例。

Conclusion: AI残余风险转移需要CER这样的用例级诊断工具来衔接技术损失与保险响应，为AI保险的核保和理赔提供结构化方法。

Abstract: AI losses that arise through an insured organization's generative or agentic AI system require state reconstruction, not merely event reconstruction, because the relevant state changes as the system reasons, retrieves, calls tools, and acts. The relevant question is not only what loss occurred, but what the system was allowed to do, what it actually did, and whether that reconstructed loss can support insurance claim recovery. This paper addresses losses in which the insured's AI system is in the causal chain, including externally triggered failures such as prompt injection, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) poisoning, malicious tool output, credential misuse, and data poisoning.
  Specifically, this paper introduces CER, a use-case-level diagnostic for AI residual risk transfer. C (control boundary) asks whether the system had an enforceable operating envelope. E (evidence reconstruction) asks whether the system state and causal chain can be reconstructed from retained artifacts. R (insurance response) asks whether the reconstructed loss is insured: whether insurance coverage is available in the market and placed for the insured, together with the proof needed to support insurance claim recovery.
  The paper makes three contributions: it defines the AI-specific reconstruction problem, operationalizes that problem through CER, and specifies claim-grade evidence for AI reconstruction. Public examples include the reported PocketOS and Replit agentic database-deletion incidents and Moffatt v. Air Canada as an adjudicated output/reliance case.
  Keywords: AI systems; CER framework; residual risk transfer; agentic AI; generative AI; AI insurance; evidence reconstruction.

</details>


### [190] [Enhancing Operational Safety via Agentic Dialogue Hazard Identification Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03812)
*Sanjay Das,Ran Elgedawy,Ethan Seefried,Ryan Burchfield,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HAZDIAL框架，通过结构化多智能体对话（对抗性辩论与建设性讨论）改进基于NLP的危害识别质量，相比单次推理基线在工业安全领域表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域（如工业过程控制、自主系统）的运营安全需要可靠的危害识别，但现有LLM的单次推理缺乏自纠正、深思熟虑和上下文精炼能力，无法模拟安全工程师的迭代分析过程。

Method: 提出HAZDIAL框架，采用多智能体多轮对话机制，系统比较对抗性辩论与建设性讨论两种对话模式，并引入基于算法的智能体交互优化方法。

Result: 在精标黄金数据集上使用标准分类指标（准确率、精确率、召回率、F1）和新颖对话指标进行评估，与单次推理基线进行对比。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对话系统、多智能体推理与AI安全的交叉研究，为对话驱动的危害分析提供了实证证据。

Abstract: Operational safety in high-stakes domains such as industrial process control, autonomous, and safety-critical systems, demand reliable hazard identification. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating safety analysis tasks, single-turn, monolithic inference is brittle: it lacks the self-correction, deliberation, and contextual refinement that safety engineers apply iteratively. In this paper, we introduce HAZDIAL, a framework that investigates whether structured agentic dialogue-multi-agent, multi-turn interactions improves the quality of NLP- based hazard identification over single-pass baselines. We systematically compare two dialogue modalities: adversarial debate and constructive discussion, and propose an algorithm-based agentic interaction optimization. We evaluate all configurations against a curated golden dataset using standard classification metrics (accuracy, precision, recall, F1) and novel dialogue metrics. This work advances the intersection of dialogue systems, multi-agent reasoning, and AI safety, providing an empirical evidence for dialogue-driven hazard analysis.

</details>


### [191] [Leveraging BART to Assess CS1 C++ Programming Assignments using Rubric-based Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03814)
*Kelsey Rainey,Jesse Roberts*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多任务微调BART模型的C++编程作业自动评分方法，通过结合评分标准（rubric）、边界软标签和分布匹配项，使模型预测更接近教师评分行为，在MAE和分数分布一致性上优于单任务、硬标签或仅代码基线的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在自动评分中往往只追求准确率，忽略了评分分布与教师评分行为的一致性。作者希望设计一种校准感知（calibration-aware）、融入评分标准的多任务微调方法，使模型预测更贴近教师的真实评分风格。

Method: 使用BART编码器-解码器架构，结合LoRA适配器进行多任务微调，同时预测数值分数和字母等级桶；引入边界软标签（boundary-based soft labels）替代硬标签；添加分布匹配项使预测分数分布与经验分布对齐；对比单任务/多任务、有无评分标准、不同标签类型等条件，并扩展T5和成对预训练变体。

Result: 多任务BART结合边界软标签和评分标准取得了最低的平均绝对误差（MAE）和最优的分数分布对齐效果；完全微调的T5进一步提升了分布保真度；成对预训练降低了数值误差但牺牲了对少数类的敏感性。

Conclusion: 校准感知、评分标准引导的多任务训练比单纯优化准确率的方法更能产生接近教师行为的评分结果，为自动编程作业评分提供了更实用的范式。

Abstract: This paper investigates rubric-aware, multitask fine-tuning of transformer models for automated grading of introductory C++ programming assignments, with the goal of producing grade predictions that better reflect instructor grading behavior than general-purpose LLMs. Using multi-semester CS1 data, student submissions are paired with numeric scores, letter-grade buckets, and assignment rubrics, then preprocessed into unified sequences for transformer input. A BART encoder-decoder with LoRA adaptation is trained to jointly predict numeric grades and grade buckets, augmented with a distribution-matching term to align predicted and empirical grade distributions, an evaluation dimension often overlooked in prior work. Experiments compare single-task and multitask training, hard one-hot versus fuzzy and boundary-based soft labels, and rubric versus no-rubric conditions, with additional T5 and pairwise-pretrained variants. Results show that multitask BART with boundary-based soft labels and rubric context achieves lower mean absolute error and stronger grade-distribution alignment than single-task, hard-label, or code-only baselines. Fully fine-tuned T5 further improves distributional fidelity, while pairwise pretraining reduces numeric error at the cost of minority-class sensitivity. Collectively, the findings suggest that calibration-aware, rubric-guided training produces more instructor-like grading behavior than accuracy-optimized alternatives.

</details>


### [192] [Calibrating Urban Traffic Simulation from Sparse Road Observations via Genetic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03823)
*Hunter Sawyer,Jesse Roberts,Simon Matei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遗传算法的框架，利用稀疏的道路观测数据校准城市交通模拟，无需详细的就业位置数据。在格林斯博罗市的SUMO平台上验证，该方法生成的模拟交通与真实测量数据高度相关，并能泛化到未训练的道路段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通模拟面临两个数据限制：大多数城市只有少量路段的真实交通测量数据，且建模通勤交通所需的就业分布数据极少有足够分辨率。作者旨在解决这些限制，降低交通模型部署的门槛。

Method: 采用遗传算法优化就业分布和路口交通参数，使基于SUMO平台的模拟交通与少量已知交通流量的路段数据对齐，无需直接使用就业数据作为训练目标。

Result: 模拟交通与真实测量数据相关性良好；能泛化到未参与训练的路段；生成的就业分布与人口普查就业数据在定性上一致。

Conclusion: 证明了从极少的真实观测数据即可实现逼真的城市交通模拟，提供了一种可扩展、数据需求低的校准方法，有助于在各城市广泛部署交通模型。

Abstract: Urban traffic simulation is a critical tool for infrastructure planning, including the placement of electric vehicle charging stations. However, realistic traffic simulation across many cities is hindered by two fundamental data limitations: detailed real-world traffic measurements are available for only a small fraction of road segments in most cities, and employment distribution data critical for modeling commuter traffic is rarely available at the resolution needed for simulation. This paper presents a genetic algorithm-based framework that directly addresses both limitations, calibrating urban traffic simulations from sparse road observations without requiring detailed job location data. Using the SUMO traffic simulation platform for Greensboro, North Carolina, our approach optimizes job distributions and gate-traffic parameters to align simulated traffic with a small sample of roads with known traffic-flow rates. We demonstrate that this approach produces simulated traffic that correlates well with real-world measurements, generalizes to road segments withheld from training, and produces job distributions that show promising qualitative agreement with census employment data despite never directly training on that employment data. This work demonstrates that realistic urban traffic simulation can be achieved from minimal real-world observations, offering a scalable and data-light approach to simulation calibration that reduces the barrier to deploying traffic models across diverse cities.

</details>


### [193] [BigFinanceBench: A Workflow-Grounded Benchmark for Financial-Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03829)
*Alex Wang,Georg Meinhardt,Jacob Katz,Joseph H. Kim,Pratyush K. Chaudhary,Chase Blagden,Eric Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BigFinanceBench，一个包含928个金融研究任务的基准，通过点权评分表评估完整推导过程而非仅最终答案。测试10个前沿模型，最佳系统仅达58.8%评分，表明金融推理评估仍有很大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准主要评估孤立子技能或最终答案，缺乏对可审计推导过程（包括数据源、时期、会计定义、假设和计算步骤）的系统性评估。

Method: 构建BigFinanceBench，包含928个专家撰写的开放式金融研究任务，每个任务配有标准答案和点权重评分表，将推导过程分解为独立可检查步骤；总计36,241个评分点，支持部分评分和错误定位。

Result: 最佳系统（前沿模型）仅达58.8%的评分表分数；最终答案准确率能部分反映推导质量但存在信息损失；模型能力在不同金融工作流中表现不均匀。

Conclusion: 金融研究答案的决策相关性依赖于可审计的推导过程，当前AI系统在完整金融工作流推理方面仍有显著不足，BigFinanceBench为这一评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Financial-research answers are decision-relevant only when another analyst can audit how they were produced: which source was chosen, which period and accounting definition were used, which assumptions were made, and how the calculation was performed. Existing finance benchmarks largely evaluate isolated subskills or final answers, leaving the auditable derivation itself under-measured. We introduce BigFinanceBench, a 928-item expert-authored benchmark of open-ended financial-research tasks in which each item pairs a ground-truth reference answer with a point-weighted rubric that decomposes the derivation into independently checkable steps. BigFinanceBench is workflow-grounded in that it evaluates the full derivation rather than only the final output. Across 36,241 rubric points, the benchmark supports partial-credit evaluation and localization of failures across the analyst workflow. Evaluating ten current frontier and open-weight agents, we find substantial headroom: the best system reaches only 58.8% rubric score, final-answer accuracy is a useful but lossy proxy for derivation quality, and model capability varies non-uniformly across financial workflows.

</details>


### [194] [EvoDS: Self-Evolving Autonomous Data Science Agent with Skill Learning and Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03841)
*Zherui Yang,Fan Liu,Yansong Ning,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDS是一个通过强化学习实现自我进化的数据科学智能体，具备自主技能获取和自适应上下文压缩能力，在多个基准测试上平均领先现有方法28.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在自动化数据科学中存在两个根本限制：静态动作集（无法积累可复用经验）和缺乏原则性的长程上下文管理（难以可靠执行多阶段、迭代式数据科学流程）。

Method: 提出EvoDS框架，包含两大策略：(1) 自主技能获取（ASA）机制，使智能体能够合成、验证和复用可执行技能；(2) 自适应上下文压缩（ACC）策略，将上下文管理视为学习控制问题而非被动截断。采用两阶段多智能体训练方案进行整合。

Result: 在四个不同的基准测试上，EvoDS平均领先最先进的开源数据科学智能体28.9%，并消除了令牌溢出（out-of-token）故障。

Conclusion: 从理论上证明了EvoDS的层次化设计能减少工具选择错误，其优化目标符合信息瓶颈原理，确保了上下文的高效利用；实验验证了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent progress in Large Language Model (LLM) agents has enabled promising advances in automated data science. However, existing approaches remain fundamentally limited by their static action sets and lack of principled long-horizon context management, hindering their ability to accumulate reusable experience across tasks and operate reliably in multi-stage, iterative data science pipelines. To address these challenges, we introduce EvoDS, a self-evolving autonomous data science agent that learns to expand its skills and adaptively managing long-term context through agentic reinforcement learning. Specifically, EvoDS introduces two key strategies: (1) Autonomous Skill Acquisition (ASA) mechanism, which enables agents to synthesize, validate, and reuse executable skills; and (2) Adaptive Context Compression (ACC) strategy, which treats context management as a learned control problem rather than passive truncation. These strategies are orchestrated within a two-stage multi-agent training scheme, enabling EvoDS to autonomously improve over time. Theoretically, we prove that EvoDS's hierarchical design reduces tool-selection error, and its optimization objective aligns with an information bottleneck principle, ensuring efficient context use. Empirically, EvoDS outperforms state-of-the-art open-source data science agents by an average of 28.9% across four diverse benchmarks while eliminating out-of-token failures. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/EvoDS.

</details>


### [195] [PyraMathBench: Evaluating and Improving Mathematical Capability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03858)
*Zetian Ouyang,Linlin Wang,Gerard de Melo,Liang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PyraMathBench基准测试（32,505道数学题）和SOLVE+IRPO方法，通过优化工具调用提升大模型的数值推理能力，在Qwen-2.5上获得5分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试很少同时评估大模型的数值处理和数学推理能力，导致数学任务失败原因难以解释。

Method: 构建PyraMathBench（含32,505道题，覆盖4个认知维度、14个子类、2种模态）；提出SOLVE模块（智能优化与学习通用模块）和IRPO（交互式相对策略优化），通过模糊匹配和低质量调用拒绝来优化工具调用。

Result: Qwen-2.5在使用SOLVE和IRPO训练后提升5分；LLM在数值计算和抽象数值问题上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 数值计算能力不足和抽象数值问题处理薄弱是LLM数学推理的主要瓶颈，SOLVE+IRPO能有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Despite the pivotal role of numerical reasoning as the cornerstone of mathematical capabilities in large language models (LLMs) across applications, few benchmarks evaluate LLMs by integrating numerical processing and mathematical reasoning, hindering the interpretability of failures in math tasks. We introduce PyraMathBench, a comprehensive hierarchical benchmark with 32,505 questions derived from 7,404 math word problems, spanning 4 key cognitive aspects, 14 subcategories, and 2 modalities. Experiments reveal that LLMs' performance is severely compromised by inadequate numerical computation and weak handling of abstract numerical questions. To address this, we propose the Smart Optimization & Learning-based VErsatile module (SOLVE) and Interactive Relative Policy Optimization (IRPO), which enhance LLMs' numerical-mathematical synergy via efficient tool calls (fuzzy matching and low-quality call rejection). Comparative experiments show Qwen-2.5 achieves a 5.0 score improvement with SOLVE and IRPO training.

</details>


### [196] [Reasoning Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03883)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Fabian Farestam,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将大推理模型的推理过程转化为可验证的推理图，基于拓扑结构定义效率指标，揭示传统指标无法区分的推理行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指标（准确率、token数）无法区分本质上不同的推理结构，需要更细粒度的度量方法。

Method: 构建逻辑谜题基准；设计将非结构化推理迹转化为可验证的推理图（包含声明和依赖关系）；基于推理图拓扑定义效率指标。

Result: 结构度量能区分token数和准确率无法区分的推理行为；可用于诊断失败模式并比较推理随难度扩展的规律。

Conclusion: 推理图拓扑分析是评估LRM的有效工具，弥补了传统指标的局限性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are often evaluated using metrics such as final-answer accuracy or token count. However, identical scores on these metrics can hide fundamentally different reasoning structures. To address this limitation, we introduce a scalable LRM benchmark of logic puzzles and a pipeline that converts unstructured traces into verifiable reasoning graphs of claims and dependencies. This turns reasoning into a structured, measurable object whose topology can be quantitatively analyzed. Building on this, we define a reasoning efficiency metric that quantifies how concentrated the model's logical flow is. Our analysis on open-source reasoning models shows that structural measurements separate behaviors that token count and accuracy conflate, providing a practical tool for diagnosing failure modes and comparing how reasoning scales with puzzle difficulty.

</details>


### [197] [scTranslation: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Single-Cell Multi-Omics Modality Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03906)
*Jiabei Cheng,Jingbo Zhou,Jun Xia,Changkai Li,Zhen Lei,Chang Yu,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了scTranslation，一个用于单细胞多组学模态翻译的系统性基准测试平台，整合了多种数据集、先进模型和评估指标，并系统研究了特征选择、特征质量和小样本设置等影响因素对模型性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前单细胞多组学模态翻译缺乏系统性的基准评估，尤其是在数据集、评估指标和影响因素方面尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建scTranslation基准，包含多样化翻译数据集、整合最先进模型、提供综合评估指标，并在特征选择、特征质量和小样本等不同场景下评估模型性能。

Result: 通过大规模研究当前方法，报告了许多有洞见的发现，揭示了特征选择、特征质量和小样本设置等因素对模型性能的显著影响。

Conclusion: 该基准为单细胞多组学模态翻译的未来发展开辟了新可能性，代码已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Simultaneous measurement of multiple omics modalities in single cells enables researchers to gain a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states and regulatory mechanisms. However, due to high experimental costs, significant noise, and incomplete modality coverage, a variety of computational methods for modality translation have emerged in recent years. Despite the development of translation models, there is still a lack of systematic benchmark evaluation in terms of datasets, evaluation metrics, and influencing factors. To address this, we present scTranslation, a comprehensive benchmark for single-cell multi-omics modality translation tasks. It includes diverse translation datasets, integrates state-of-the-art models, and provides a comprehensive evaluation metrics. In addition, we assess model performance under different scenarios, such as feature selection, feature quality, and few-shot settings. These factors significantly affect model performance but have rarely been systematically studied before. Leveraging this benchmark, we conduct a large-scale study of current methods, report many insightful findings that open up new possibilities for future development. The benchmark is open-sourced to facilitate future research. The code is anonymously released at https://github.com/Bunnybeibei/scTranslation.

</details>


### [198] [Hedge-Bench: Benchmarking Agents on Hard, Realistic Tasks Pertaining to Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03918)
*Eric Cho,Shawn Huang,Alice Lu,Andy Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Hedge-Bench 1.0基准，包含102个基于对冲基金分析师真实工作任务的测试案例，采用确定性评分方法评估AI模型在金融分析推理方面的能力，前沿模型得分低于16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融分析基准无法捕捉开放性问题推理这一核心挑战，且依赖模型评判的评估方式存在噪声和循环论证问题。

Method: 收集102个对冲基金分析师的实际工作任务，基于专业分析师的显式推理轨迹构建基准，实现对专家验证步骤的确定性评分。

Result: 前沿模型和智能体在该基准上的得分低于16%。

Conclusion: Hedge-Bench 1.0为评估AI在真实金融分析场景中的开放性问题推理能力提供了更可靠、更客观的基准，当前模型表现远未达到专业水平。

Abstract: AI agents can increasingly handle the mechanical tasks of financial analysis: retrieving documents, calculating formulas, updating spreadsheets. The harder, more valuable challenge is reasoning through the open-ended questions that define expert Analyst work. Existing benchmarks do not capture this class of problem, and those that attempt to evaluate open-ended reasoning rely on model-judged outputs that introduce noise and circularity. We present Hedge-Bench 1.0: a benchmark of 102 actual, on-the-job tasks grounded in the explicit reasoning traces of professional hedge fund analysts working with relevant information sources. This approach enables deterministic grading against verified expert steps. Frontier models and agents score below 16\% on the benchmark. We publish the dataset and evaluation harness at github.com/Trata-Inc/trata-hedge-bench.

</details>


### [199] [Entropy Is Not Enough: Unlocking Effective Reinforcement Learning for Visual Reasoning via Vision-Anchored Token Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03937)
*Senjie Jin,Peixin Wang,Boyang Liu,Xiaoran Fan,Shuo Li,Zhiheng Xi,Jiazheng Zhang,Yuhao Zhou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在视觉推理任务中，仅基于词元熵的强化学习信用分配机制会失效，因为视觉敏感词元天然具有低熵。为此提出VEPO框架，通过视觉敏感度与词元熵的耦合选择机制，在7B和3B规模上分别超越基线2.28和3.15个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本RLVR中，token-level熵被广泛用于信用分配，但作者发现这一机制在视觉推理中失效——视觉敏感词元天然具有低熵，导致其被错误忽略。现有多模态RL方法要么缺乏系统的视觉度量，要么忽视了熵主要驱动语义探索的本质，无法满足精确感知与语义推理交织的需求。

Method: 提出VEPO框架，核心是视觉敏感度与词元熵的**乘法耦合**机制：选择同时满足"视觉上可定位"且"信息量大"的词元，将梯度信用导向这些词元，从而在感知和语义两个维度同时进行有效探索。

Result: 在7B规模上超越仅使用熵的基线2.28个点，在3B规模上超越3.15个点。消融实验进一步验证了方法的合理性。

Conclusion: VEPO通过将视觉感知信号与词元熵进行结构化结合，有效解决了视觉推理中信用分配失效的问题，为多模态强化学习提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: While token-level entropy is commonly recognized as effective for credit assignment in text-only reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), it remains unclear whether this mechanism still holds in visual reasoning. Our controlled study shows that this mechanism collapses in visual reasoning due to the omission of vision-sensitive tokens with naturally low entropy. Although existing multimodal RL methods increasingly acknowledge the importance of visual perception, they struggle to satisfy the inherent demand for interleaving precise perceptual grounding with semantic reasoning, either lacking systematic visual measurements or overlooking that token entropy primarily drives semantic exploration. To address this, we introduce VEPO (Vision-Entropy token-selection for Policy Optimization), an effective RL framework explicitly integrating visual sensitivity with token entropy via a principled multiplicative coupling, where VEPO redirects gradient credit toward tokens which are simultaneously visually grounded and highly informative. Extensive experiments demonstrate VEPO's leading performance, significantly outperforming the entropy-only baseline by 2.28 points at 7B-scale and 3.15 points at 3B-scale. Ablations further substantiate the soundness of our method.

</details>


### [200] [Imaginative Perception Tokens Enhance Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03988)
*Mahtab Bigverdi,Lindsey Li,Weikai Huang,Yiming Liu,Jaemin Cho,Jieyu Zhang,Tuhin Kundu,Chris Dangjoo Kim,Zelun Luo,Linda Shapiro,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT)，一种中间感知表征，帮助视觉语言模型在空间推理中处理不可见信息，在Perspective Taking、Path Tracing、Multiview Counting三项任务上优于文本思维链方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在空间推理中当关键信息不可直接观察时表现不佳，而传统文本思维链存在模态不匹配问题，因此需要一种能外化"想象感知"的中间表征来提升模型的空间推理能力。

Method: 设计Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT)作为中间感知表征，让VLM在替代空间配置下想象感知内容；构建约20K样本的三个空间推理任务（PET、PT、MVC）数据集；以BAGEL为骨干模型，在训练中引入IPT监督信号。

Result: IPT监督一致提升空间推理能力，MVC任务上准确率提升3.4%，PT任务上与强闭源模型竞争；IPT与标签监督结合效果更优，而文本思维链反而损害性能。

Conclusion: IPT提供了一种原则性的监督信号，用于推理未观察到的空间结构，能提升泛化能力并产生可解释的中间表征，且避免了文本思维链在空间计算中的模态不匹配问题。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) excel at many tasks but still struggle with spatial reasoning when critical information is not directly observable. Many such problems require imaginative perception: inferring what would be seen from an unseen viewpoint, tracing paths through occluded spaces, or integrating partial observations into a coherent spatial representation. We introduce Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT), intermediate perceptual representations that externalize what a VLM would perceive under alternative spatial configurations while remaining consistent with the observed input.
  To study this capability, we formulate three tasks, Perspective Taking (PET), Path Tracing (PT), and Multiview Counting (MVC), and construct datasets of approximately 20K examples with ground truth imaginations, answers, and evaluation benchmarks. Using the unified VLM BAGEL as the backbone, IPT supervision consistently improves spatial reasoning and often outperforms textual chain of thought training, even without generating images at inference time. On MVC, IPT improves accuracy by 3.4% and achieves competitive performance with strong closed-source models on PT. We further find that combining IPT and label-only supervision yields additional gains, whereas textual chain of thought can substantially degrade performance, suggesting a modality mismatch when spatial computation is forced through language. Overall, IPT provides a principled supervision signal for reasoning about unobserved spatial structure, improving generalization while producing interpretable intermediate representations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Too Much of a Good Thing: When sim2real Efforts Impede Policy Learning (And What to Do About It)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02636)
*Kyle Morgenstein,Bharath Masetty,Stephen Welch,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文批判了sim2real过度约束导致策略学习受限的问题，并提出了一种基于运动学约束的sim2sim2real新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sim2real方法对策略学习施加了过多真实世界的约束，导致模拟器锁定和策略探索不足，作者希望揭示这一问题的根源并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出sim2sim2real范式，仅以机器人运动学作为唯一的设计约束，减少不必要的真实世界约束对策略学习的干扰。

Result: 未在摘要中提及具体实验结果，主要贡献在于问题诊断和范式提出。

Conclusion: 过度sim2real会产生负面效果，sim2sim2real有望通过放松约束来改善策略学习和迁移效果。

Abstract: While sim2real efforts are necessary for effective policy transfer to hardware, there is such a thing as too much of a good thing. We argue that sim2real efforts have led to misaligned incentives with policy learning, resulting in simulator lock in and poor policy exploration due to the unreasonable constraints imposed by the real world. We offer a diagnosis and explanation of the current status of the problem, and propose a potential solution via a sim2sim2real paradigm that leverages the robot's kinematics as the sole design constraint.

</details>


### [202] [CARVE: Certified Affordable Repair of Vetoed Maneuvers via Envelopes for Interactive Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02641)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CARVE，一种无预测的交互修复认证层，用于解决自动驾驶中规则硬边界导致的误否决问题，在589个真实场景中接受98.64%被否决的机动，同时完全尊重路权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶栈中，规则硬边界会误否决那些只需非优先级代理做出微小合法让步即可恢复的可行机动，而现有方法（规则书、预测规划器）无法提供交互修复的运行时证明对象。

Method: 提出CARVE，一种基于有限格（lattice）的无预测证书层，定义自我代理和他人代理的战术算子；引入合作包络线 B_j(s) 区分运动学可达性与规范性优先级，生成包含绑定规则、修复类别、责任加权成本分割和回退方案的证书。

Result: 在589个INTERACTION数据集回放片段上，CARVE-Greedy接受98.64%初始被否决的机动，恢复370/378个人类判定的误否决，零优先级代理误报，400/400压力场景正确否决，完全保持路权尊重。

Conclusion: CARVE证明了交互修复的可靠、可归因、规范可接受性，无需预测他人行为即可提供形式化安全保障，填补了规则硬否决与预测规划之间的空白。

Abstract: Interactive driving exposes a failure mode that is easy to miss in rule-aware autonomous-driving stacks: a hard-rule margin can be negative for an ego candidate even though a small lawful accommodation by a non-priority agent would restore feasibility. Existing rulebooks, shields, and reachability filters are strong at vetoing unsafe actions, while prediction-based planners model likely responses. Neither returns a runtime proof object that states which bounded multi-agent edit repairs the maneuver, who owns the edit, whether the request is right-of-way affordable, and what ego fallback remains if the request is not observed. We formulate this missing object as *interactive repair certification* and introduce *CARVE*, a prediction-free certificate layer over a finite lattice of ego-owned and agent-owned tactical operators. Agent-owned requests are admissible only inside \(B_j(s) = β(π_j)α_j^{\max}(s)\), a cooperation envelope that separates kinematic reachability from normative priority. The resulting certificate records the binding rule, repair category, repair set, responsibility-weighted cost split, and fallback. On 589 Lanelet2-geometry-grounded INTERACTION replay episodes, CARVE-Greedy accepts 98.64% of initially vetoed maneuvers and recovers 370/378 human-resolved false vetoes, while preserving 589/589 right-of-way respect, zero priority-agent false positives, and 400/400 negative-stress vetoes. We prove certificate soundness, structural right-of-way respect, exact finite-lattice minimality, fallback contingency, and blame-consistency conditions. CARVE does not predict or require another driver's compliance; it certifies whether a proposed interaction is bounded, attributable, and normatively admissible under declared assumptions.

</details>


### [203] [Fixed-Time Dynamic Landing of Quadrotors using Adaptive Unscented Kalman Filtering and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02658)
*Mohammadreza Izadi,Zeinab Shayan,Steven Waslander,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合非线性模型预测控制、最小加加速度轨迹规划器和自适应无迹卡尔曼滤波的框架，实现了多旋翼无人机在移动平台上的动态着陆，具备可重复的着陆性能和更优的速度预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机在移动平台上动态着陆面临两大挑战：着陆时间的一致性和时变传感质量下的鲁棒性。现有方法难以同时满足这两点需求。

Method: 1) 非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）进行控制；2) 实时最小加加速度轨迹规划器，强制规定触地时间；3) 自适应无迹卡尔曼滤波（AUKF），在线更新过程噪声和测量噪声统计量；4) 参考可行性分析，证明最小加加速度参考在标准跟踪假设下产生有界推力和力矩命令。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，该方法能实现可重复着陆，并在平台速度预测精度上优于基于EKF/UKF的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过轨迹规划与自适应估计的结合，有效解决了移动平台着陆中的定时一致性和传感质量时变问题，为实际部署提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces an estimation and control framework for dynamic landing of multi-rotor uncrewed aerial vehicles on moving platforms. The proposed method integrates nonlinear model predictive control with a real-time minimum-jerk trajectory planner that enforces a prescribed touchdown time, enabling consistent timing during the terminal descent. To enhance robustness in the presence of time-varying sensing quality, we utilize an adaptive unscented kalman filter that updates the process and measurement noise statistics online. In addition, we provide a reference feasibility analysis showing that minimum-jerk references induce bounded thrust and torque commands under standard tracking hypotheses. The proposed framework is evaluated in simulation and hardware experiments, and it is shown to achieve repeatable landings and improved platform velocity prediction accuracy relative to EKF/UKF-based methods.

</details>


### [204] [Motion Planning in Dynamic Environments: A Survey from Classical to Modern Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02677)
*Zongyuan Shen,Yaming Ou,Shalabh Gupta,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Gao Wang,Zhongqiang Ren,Junfeng Fan,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2015-2025年间138篇动态环境下运动规划的研究工作，将方法分为五类（采样、图搜索、模型预测控制、学习、经典局部规划），并讨论了动态感知技术及动态环境特有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下的运动规划需要机器人持续适应环境变化，但现有综述多聚焦静态场景，缺乏对动态环境的系统性梳理，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 综述了138篇文献，将运动规划方法分为五类：采样、图搜索、模型预测控制、学习（监督学习与强化学习）、经典局部规划（速度障碍、势场法、动态窗口法）；同时讨论了动态感知技术（相机、LiDAR、事件传感器）。

Result: 分类总结了各类方法的原理、优势与局限，指出了动态环境特有的挑战，包括预测不确定性、人机交互问题和冻结机器人问题。

Conclusion: 为研究人员提供了动态环境下运动规划方法的系统性理解框架。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments requires robots to continuously adapt their paths in response to environmental changes for safe and uninterrupted navigation. While many surveys have reviewed planning in static settings, systematic reviews focused on dynamic environments remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of 138 works, primarily published between 2015 and 2025, spanning both classical and learning-based approaches. The motion planning methods are grouped into five categories based on the concepts of sampling, graph search, model predictive control, learning, and additional classical local planning approaches, including velocity obstacles, potential fields and dynamic windows. The learning techniques include supervised learning and reinforcement learning. We also discuss the role of dynamic perception in motion planning, covering techniques for detecting and modeling moving obstacles using cameras, LiDAR, and event-based sensors. The survey analyzes the principles, strengths, and limitations of each method, with particular attention to challenges unique to dynamic environments, such as prediction uncertainty, human-robot interaction, and the freezing robot problem. The survey provides researchers with a structured understanding of motion planning methods in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [205] [See Less, Specify More: Visual Evidence Budgets for Generalizable VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02735)
*Yueh-Hua Wu,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出S2框架，通过"少看多指定"（See Less, Specify More）改善VLA模型的泛化能力。Specify More用精细化轨迹语言补充指令，See Less通过显式视觉证据预算训练执行器从任务充分证据中行动。在8个真实机器人任务上，平均子任务成功率从54.2%提升至79.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在干扰物、外观变化和语义相似任务下泛化困难，根本原因是模型需要从粗粒度指令中推断局部执行细节，同时决定图像中哪些部分对控制重要，这种双重不确定性导致监督信号混叠。

Method: S2框架包含两个核心组件：1) Specify More：保留原始指令作为高层目标，同时为每条轨迹重新标注精细化的轨迹级和子任务级语言描述，消除执行歧义；2) See Less：无需区域或掩码标注，引入显式视觉证据预算，训练执行器仅从任务充分证据中行动，而非无约束的视觉上下文。

Result: 在8个真实机器人任务上，使用TX-G2（AgiBot G2兼容变体）和HSR机器人，S2将平均子任务成功率从54.2%（pi0.5基线）提升至79.0%。

Conclusion: VLA泛化能力的提升关键在于：训练执行器从信息丰富的局部引导和任务充分的视觉证据中行动，而非从弱监督中同时恢复两者。粗粒度指令会导致可避免的监督混叠，保持目标一致的局部指导优于指令替换，显式证据预算减少了模型对宽泛视觉上下文的依赖。

Abstract: Generalization remains a central bottleneck for vision-language-action (VLA) models: under distractors, appearance shifts, and semantically similar tasks, the policy must often infer local execution details from coarse instructions while also deciding which parts of the image matter for control. We present S2 (See Less, Specify More), a framework for improving VLA generalization by training the executor under a cleaner interface.
  Specify More preserves the original instruction as a stable high-level goal while relabeling each trajectory into refined trajectory- and subtask-level language that disambiguates the current execution mode. Unlike native attention, See Less imposes an explicit visual evidence budget, training the executor to act from task-sufficient evidence rather than unconstrained visual context, without any region or mask annotation.
  This interface lets the executor follow detailed guidance without relying on distracting visual patches or resolving avoidable ambiguity on its own, and it remains compatible with off-the-shelf VLM planners through in-context learning. Across our main evaluation settings, S2 improves overall generalization metrics by changing the executor's learning problem: coarse instructions induce avoidable supervision aliasing, goal-preserving local guidance outperforms instruction replacement in our main ablations, and explicit evidence budgeting reduces dependence on broad visual context beyond efficiency considerations.
  Across eight real-robot tasks on TX-G2 (an AgiBot G2-compatible variant) and HSR, S2 raises mean subtask success from 54.2% to 79.0% over pi0.5. Together, these results suggest that VLA generalization improves when the executor is trained to act from informative local guidance and task-sufficient visual evidence, rather than recovering both from weak supervision.

</details>


### [206] [SeeTraceAct: Visibility-Aware Latent Planning from Cross-Embodiment Demonstration Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02745)
*Jaehyeon Son,Junhyun Kim,Kyle Kam,Jeremiah Coholich,Seok Joon Kim,Jinhoo Kim,Chris Dongjoo Kim,Jaemin Cho,Dieter Fox,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SeeTraceAct，一种通过可见性感知的未来末端执行器轨迹预测来增强空间定位的演示条件型VLA框架，在RoboCasa-DC和真实世界基准上均取得最优成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端方法在执行需要精确定位小目标区域的任务时表现不佳，而收集任务特定的遥操作数据成本高昂。因此，研究如何利用单个演示视频来高效适应新任务。

Method: 提出SeeTraceAct框架，通过可见性感知的未来末端执行器轨迹预测（visibility-aware prediction of future end-effector traces）来鼓励精确的空间定位。同时构建了RoboCasa-DC数据集，包含跨实体演示的配对人形视频。

Result: 在RoboCasa-DC的所有四个设置中取得最佳成功率，并在真实世界基准上将平均成功率提升12.5个百分点。

Conclusion: SeeTraceAct通过增强空间精确定位能力，有效提升了演示条件型VLA在未见任务上的泛化性能，且跨实体（人类演示→机器人执行）的演示条件式学习具有可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are promising general-purpose robot policies, but adapting them to new tasks typically requires costly task-specific teleoperation data. As an alternative, we study one-shot demo-conditioned VLAs, where a robot policy is conditioned on a single demonstration video of an unseen task. We find that existing end-to-end approaches often struggle when successful execution requires precisely localizing small target regions. To address this limitation, we propose SeeTraceAct, a demo-conditioned VLA framework that encourages precise spatial grounding through visibility-aware prediction of future end-effector traces. To enable reproducible evaluation with cross-embodiment demonstrations, we introduce and release RoboCasa-DC, a demo-conditioned extension of RoboCasa with episode-paired humanoid videos. Experiments on RoboCasa-DC and a real-world benchmark, where a Franka Panda arm is conditioned on human demonstrations, show that SeeTraceAct outperforms baselines, achieving the best success rate across all four RoboCasa-DC settings and improving real-world average success by 12.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [207] [Hybrid Adaptive Kalman Filtering for Data-Efficient Joint Tracking and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02767)
*Jiho Lee,Nisar R. Ahmed,Rebecca Russell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自监督混合自适应卡尔曼滤波器，从测量数据中学习系统动态和噪声协方差的修正，保持滤波器概率结构，实现准确的状态估计和模型分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波对模型失配和噪声协方差调参敏感；现有学习方法需要大量标注数据且不能产生一致的不确定性估计。

Method: 提出自监督混合自适应卡尔曼滤波器，从测量数据中学习对系统动态和过程噪声协方差的结构化修正，同时保留滤波器的概率结构，计算创新似然并通过广义贝叶斯推断进行模型分类。

Result: 在真实和模拟数据集上，该方法在估计精度、统计一致性上表现更优，且在低数据和大数据场景下均具有鲁棒的分类性能。

Conclusion: 自监督学习与卡尔曼滤波概率结构的结合能够同时提升估计精度和模型分类能力，克服传统方法和监督学习方法的局限。

Abstract: Kalman filtering performance is highly sensitive to model mismatch and noise covariance tuning. Learning-based approaches address these limitations but typically rely on supervised training with large datasets and do not produce consistent uncertainty estimates. In this paper, we propose a self-supervised Hybrid Adaptive Kalman Filter that learns structured corrections to system dynamics and process noise covariance from measurements alone while preserving the probabilistic structure of the filter. This allows the innovation likelihood to be computed and subsequently used for model classification via generalized Bayesian inference. Experimental results on real-world and simulated datasets demonstrate improved estimation accuracy and statistical consistency as well as robust classification performance across both low-data and large-data scenarios.

</details>


### [208] [A Measurement-Driven Digital Twin Architecture for Plant-Level Biomass Estimation and Growth Forecasting in Hydroponic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02796)
*Morgan Mayborne,Abhisesh Silwal,George Kantor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种结合数字孪生模型、定制传感器硬件和RGB-D神经网络的 hydroponics 系统，能连续跟踪单个生菜植株的生长，未来1-4天产量预测误差约2克，质量估计误差约1.5克。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决城市密集中心的食品分配问题，开发水培等替代土壤园艺方法，并需要精确跟踪植物生长以优化产量预测。

Method: 构建数字孪生模型整合传感器数据流，使用RGB-D图像作为输入的定制神经网络（在1300张图像数据集上训练）连续测量产量，并将系统集成到运行中的水培温室。

Result: 神经网络质量估计误差在1.5克以内，数字孪生模型对未来1-4天的产量预测误差约2克。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生系统能够有效预测水培生菜的未来产量，为精准农业提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Alternatives to soil-based horticulture, such as hydroponics, have been developed to respond to food distribution concerns for dense urban centers. A new system was developed to track an individual lettuce plant's growth in a hydroponic environment, utilizing streams of measured information and available models to continuously update the growth trajectory estimates for a plant. These "digital twin" models were integrated into an operating hydroponic greenhouse, with custom horticultural and sensor hardware to grow and measure relevant information. To aid in updating model parameters, plant yield was continuously measured with a custom neural network, using RGB-D images of the plants as an input. The network, trained on a collected dataset of 1300 images, was able to estimate mass within 1.5 g of the ground-truth value. After integration into the custom system, digital twin growth projections could approximate future yield between one and four days in the future, maintaining around a 2 g forecasting error.

</details>


### [209] [Direct Informed Sampling on Riemannian Manifolds via Loewner Order Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02879)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种矩阵值可容许启发式方法，利用Loewner序计算度量张量的最紧常数下界，通过Cholesky分解将黎曼流形上的有信息集映射为欧氏空间中的扁球体，实现无拒绝采样，显著加速多种渐进最优规划器在流形约束下的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于欧氏距离的启发式在有黎曼度量的配置相关空间中不再可容许，而标量特征值下界虽恢复可容许性但丢弃了度量的方向结构，导致有信息集过于保守，采样效率低。

Method: 利用Loewner序对称正定矩阵计算度量张量的最紧常数下界（矩阵值），通过Cholesky分解定义到各向同性欧氏空间的线性映射，将黎曼有信息集转化为标准扁球体，实现直接无拒绝采样。

Result: 在6-DoF UR5、7-DoF Franka和14-DoF PR2三种机械臂上，三种不同黎曼度量下，该方法产生的有信息集均比欧氏和标量特征值下界更紧凑，在多款渐进最优规划器中均加速收敛。

Conclusion: 矩阵值可容许启发式保留了度量的方向结构，能产生更紧的有信息集，从而更高效引导采样，提升运动规划器在黎曼流形上的性能。

Abstract: Informed sampling techniques accelerate sampling-based motion planners by focusing the search on promising regions of the state space, yet most existing methods rely on Euclidean heuristics that become inadmissible under configuration-dependent Riemannian metrics. While scalar eigenvalue bounds restore admissibility by uniformly scaling the Euclidean distance, they discard the directional structure of the metric, producing overly conservative informed sets. We propose a matrix-valued admissible heuristic that exploits the Loewner order on symmetric positive definite matrices to compute the tightest constant lower bound on the metric tensor while preserving its full directional structure. The Cholesky factorization of this bound defines a linear map to an isotropic Euclidean space in which the Riemannian informed set reduces to a standard prolate hyperspheroid, enabling direct, rejection-free sampling using existing algorithms. Experiments on manipulation tasks with a 6-DoF UR5, 7-DoF Franka, and 14-DoF PR2 under three distinct Riemannian metrics show that our heuristic produces consistently tighter informed sets than both the Euclidean and scalar eigenvalue bounds, accelerating convergence across multiple state-of-the-art asymptotically optimal planners.

</details>


### [210] [Impact of a Soft Wearable Back-Support Device on Postural Stability during Trip-Like Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02888)
*Yuanhao Chen,Rohan Khatavkar,Soubhagya Nayak,Jiefeng Sun,Hyunglae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探究了软性可穿戴背部支撑装置在不同刚度条件下对受试者在站立和行走中受到扰动时姿势稳定性的影响，发现装置能显著改善稳定性，高刚度效果在站立时更突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够提升人体在外部扰动下姿势稳定性的可穿戴设备，以预防跌倒。

Method: 在站立和行走两种实验范式中施加类绊倒扰动，对比无设备、低刚度、高刚度三种背部支撑条件下的最小稳定裕度（MOS）。

Result: 使用装置时MOS增加，姿势稳定性提升；站立时MOS随刚度增加显著提高，行走时两种装置条件均优于无装置，但彼此无显著差异。

Conclusion: 软性可调刚度背部支撑装置有助于改善对外部扰动的反应性平衡控制，未来应探索个性化刚度优化并在高风险人群中评估效果。

Abstract: The effectiveness of a soft wearable back-support device in enhancing postural stability was investigated under trip-like perturbations using two experimental paradigms: perturbed standing and perturbed walking. Healthy subjects completed trials under three different back-support conditions: no device, device worn with low stiffness, and device activated with high stiffness. Whole-body stability was quantified using the minimum Margin of Stability (MOS) at the point of maximal instability. Results demonstrated increased MOS during device use, indicating enhanced postural stability. In standing, MOS increased significantly with device stiffness, whereas in walking, both device conditions improved MOS relative to no device but did not differ significantly from each other. These findings highlight the potential of soft wearable back-support devices with adjustable stiffness to improve reactive balance control against external perturbations, with important implications for fall prevention. Future research should explore personalized stiffness optimization and evaluate efficacy in populations at elevated risk of falls.

</details>


### [211] [Improved Postural Stability Using a Lightweight Semi-Active Soft Back Support Device Under Standing Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02928)
*Rohan Khatavkar,Jiefeng Sun,Hyunglae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种轻量级半主动软质背部支撑装置，用于帮助老年人在站立扰动后预防跌倒。该装置结合了气动人工肌肉（主动元件）和弹性带（被动元件），能在扰动后快速提供辅助力。实验表明，该装置显著降低了全身角动量并增加了稳定裕度，有效改善平衡恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人站立时受到扰动（如向前失去平衡）后特别容易跌倒。现有被动轻量化装置因躯干屈曲角度小、辅助力有限，效果不理想；而重型背部支撑装置虽能提供足够支撑，但附加质量会不利地改变身体质心位置，反而影响稳定性。因此需要一种轻量且能提供足够辅助力的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种轻量级半主动软质背部支撑装置，将气动人工肌肉（主动元件）与弹性带（被动元件）并联结合。扰动发生后，主动元件快速提供辅助力，克服了被动装置作用力不足的局限。

Result: 在5名健康受试者上的实验表明，该半主动装置显著降低了全身角动量，并增加了稳定裕度，表明平衡恢复性能得到改善。

Conclusion: 半主动软体可穿戴机器人作为轻量级策略，在站立扰动后的跌倒预防方面具有良好前景。

Abstract: Older adults are particularly susceptible to falls following perturbations during standing, such as forward loss of balance. Back support devices that assist trunk extension may help mitigate fall risk by preventing excessive trunk flexion. Previous studies have investigated heavy back support devices; however, these systems often introduced adverse effects on stability due to their added mass, which shifted the body's natural center of mass unfavorably. In contrast, lightweight passive devices have shown limited benefits, as they can generate only modest assistive forces during the relatively small trunk flexion associated with forward balance loss. In this study, we evaluated the effects of a lightweight semi-active soft back support device on postural stability following standing perturbations. Our device combines an active element (a pneumatic artificial muscle) in parallel with a passive elastic band. The active element rapidly provides assistive force following a perturbation, overcoming the limitations of passive devices. Experiments conducted with five healthy individuals demonstrated that the semi-active device significantly reduced whole-body angular momentum and increased the margin of stability, indicating improved balance recovery performance. These results highlight the promise of semi-active soft wearable robots as an effective and lightweight strategy for fall prevention during standing perturbations.

</details>


### [212] [SCOPE: Real-Time Natural Language Camera Agent at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02951)
*Nikolaj Hindsbo,Sina Ehsani,Pragyana Mishra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE，一个模块化智能体，用于自然语言驱动的PTZ摄像头控制和视觉场景理解，可在Blender仿真和物理摄像头上运行。发布536项任务的基准测试，评估19种规划器-感知模型组合，发现更强的小语言模型减少幻觉，混合专家模型在延迟和内存上匹配或超越密集模型，量化在精度损失极小的情况下提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言驱动的机器人代理缺乏反映真实任务需求的评估（自然语言指令+可复现结果），且需要在边缘部署场景下连接语言模型与感知/控制工具，同时评估延迟、准确性和错误模式这些关键指标。

Method: 设计SCOPE模块化智能体，支持Blender仿真环境和物理PTZ摄像头；发布536项任务的基准测试（含QA、单/多步指令、计数、空间推理、描述、OCR）；结合执行轨迹与LM-as-Judge评估延迟、准确性和错误模式；评估19种规划器-感知模型组合（Qwen3 SLM + Moondream/Qwen VLM）。

Result: 更强的SLM显著减少幻觉并改善工具路由；当SLM足够强大时，感知成为性能瓶颈；混合专家模型在延迟和内存占用上与更小网络相当或更优；量化在精度损失极小的情况下提供额外效率提升。

Conclusion: SCOPE验证了一个真实可行的、面向边缘部署的实时语言驱动PTZ控制方案，通过仿真到现实的验证，为实际应用提供了设计参考点。

Abstract: Deploying language-driven agents in robotics requires evaluations that reflect real-world task demands: natural-language instructions with reproducible outcomes. Such agents must connect language models to callable perception and control tools, and be assessed using deployment-critical metrics including latency, accuracy, and error modes. We present SCOPE (Simulation and Camera Operations for Perception and Evaluation), a modular agent for natural-language, open-vocabulary pan-tilt-zoom (PTZ) camera control and visual scene understanding, designed explicitly for edge deployment. SCOPE operates both in a Blender-based simulation environment and on a physical PTZ camera, executing all perception, planning, and control locally at the deployment site using edge-accessible compute.
  We release a 536-task benchmark spanning QA, single- and multi-step commands, counting, spatial reasoning, descriptions, and optical character recognition in a Blender-based simulation environment that exposes realistic PTZ control affordances. Execution traces are combined with an LM-as-Judge to evaluate latency, accuracy, and error modes.
  We evaluate 19 planner-perception model combinations pairing Qwen3 small language models (SLMs) with Moondream and Qwen vision-language models (VLMs). Stronger SLMs substantially reduce hallucinations and improve tool routing, leading to more reliable closed-loop behavior. Once a sufficiently capable SLM is used, perception becomes the dominant performance bottleneck. Mixture-of-Experts models on both the planning and perception side consistently match or exceed dense alternatives at latencies and memory footprints comparable to much smaller networks. Quantization provides additional efficiency gains with minimal accuracy degradation, identifying a practical, sim-to-real validated design point for real-time, edge-feasible language-driven PTZ control.

</details>


### [213] [Hybrid Dynamics Modeling for a Flexible 2-DoF Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02969)
*Maciek Popik,Daniel Yang,Mahdis Bisheban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种建模方法来描述柔性连杆2-DoF机械臂的动力学，发现纯物理参数模型精度最差，而正则化和数据驱动方法更准确，支持半参数残差学习方法的开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体模型无法捕捉柔性连杆机械臂中未被建模的动力学特性（如连杆柔性），需要探索更好的建模方法。

Method: 比较了三种方法：(1) 结合刚体动力学和高斯混合模型的物理信息模型；(2) 基于运动学特征的岭回归纯数据驱动模型；(3) 基于物理参数的普通最小二乘模型。

Result: 基于物理参数的模型精度最差，正则化和最小二乘估计与实测力矩更吻合；残差分析和误差指标表明纯参数模型在柔性连杆系统中存在局限性。

Conclusion: 纯参数化模型不足以描述柔性连杆系统，正则化和数据驱动识别更有价值，支持半参数残差学习方法的发展。

Abstract: This paper examines three approaches for modeling the dynamics of a flexible-link 2-DoF robotic arm to address unmodeled dynamics not captured by rigid-body models. Two physics informed models combine rigid-body dynamics (RBD) formulations with a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) to capture residual model errors and linkage flexibility. A kinematics-based regression model serves as a purely data-driven baseline. Using an open-source dataset, torque predictions are first estimated using Ridge regression on kinematic features, while the physicsbased baseline is constructed from published specifications, and ordinary least-squares regression is subsequently used to estimate the same parameter set directly from data. Results show that the physics-based parameters yield the poorest accuracy, while regularized and least-squares estimators align more closely with measured torques. Residual analysis and error metrics highlight the limitations of purely parametric models for flexible-link systems and underscore the value of regularization and data-driven identification, supporting developments of semi-parametric residual learning methods.

</details>


### [214] [MARIO: Motion-Augmented Real-Time Multi-Sensor Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02996)
*Yiquan Li,Taeyoung Yeon,Chenfeng Gao,Vasco Xu,Xuanyou Liu,Karan Ahuja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MARIO框架，通过将学习型IMU推断的人体姿态先验（人体运动学约束）融入惯性里程计，并融合商用AR眼镜已有传感器（磁力计、气压计、辅助IMU），在Nymeria等大规模数据集上将定位漂移降低36%-42%，为无相机的精确人体追踪设立了新标杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的惯性里程计方法虽通过大规模预训练提升了泛化性，但仍存在漂移和噪声问题，根本原因在于未显式捕获人体运动动力学特征（物理一致性约束）。

Method: 1) 提出学习型IMU推断的姿态先验，将人体运动学约束融入IO架构；2) 设计多传感器融合框架，整合商用AR眼镜已有的磁力计、气压计和辅助IMU信号。

Result: 在Nymeria数据集（比此前大5倍）上，姿态先验减少漂移达36%；融合策略进一步减少42%的漂移。整体提升了鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过统一人体运动学与多模态感知，开创了惯性/轻量级里程计的新范式，为无相机人体追踪设立了新基准。

Abstract: Inertial odometry (IO) using only Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) provides a lightweight solution for human motion tracking in augmented reality (AR) and wearable devices. Recent learning-based IO methods have improved the generalizability of inertial localization through large-scale pretraining on human motion datasets. However, these approaches remain prone to drift and noise because they do not explicitly capture human motion dynamics, especially on daily activity datasets such as Nymeria. In this work, we propose to ground inertial odometry in human kinematics through a learned IMU-inferred pose prior, which promotes physically consistent motion constraints. We integrate this pose prior into existing IO architectures and reduce positional drift by up to 36% on the challenging Nymeria dataset, which is 5x larger than datasets used in prior work. We further improve long-term performance with a sensor-fusion framework that incorporates auxiliary signals from lightweight sensors already available on commercial AR glasses, including magnetometers, barometers, and secondary IMUs. With this fusion strategy, positional drift is reduced by up to 42%, improving robustness and generalization across diverse motion conditions. Together, our results introduce a new paradigm for inertial and lightweight odometry by unifying human motion kinematics with multimodal sensing, setting a new benchmark for accurate and robust camera-less human tracking. Our website is available at https://spice-lab.org/projects/MARIO/.

</details>


### [215] [ModuLoop : Low-Level Code Generation using Modular Synthesizer and Closed-Loop Debugger for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03047)
*Gina Yoon,Sumin Lee,Joo Yong Sim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出闭环模块化代码合成器框架，利用预训练LLM无需微调即可生成并迭代调试机器人控制代码，在RGB-D相机与机械臂标定及抓取任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在代码生成和问题解决方面表现出色，但在机器人控制（尤其是低层任务需要精确操作、实时反馈和环境依赖执行）中的应用仍然受限。

Method: 提出Closed-Loop Modular Code Synthesizer框架，使用预训练LLM进行模块化代码规划与生成，通过闭环结构迭代执行代码并插入调试探针观察行为，进行系统化调试与优化。

Result: 在RGB-D相机与机械臂标定及后续抓取任务中，框架实现了高执行准确性和自主性，验证了校准精度及框架的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了LLM在机器人控制中的实用性和可扩展性，无需任务特定微调即可生成可执行的控制程序。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance across various domains, including code generation and problem solving. However, their application in robotic control, particularly in low-level tasks that require precise manipulation, real-time feedback, and environment-dependent execution, remains limited. To address this challenge, we propose the Closed-Loop Modular Code Synthesizer framework. This framework leverages a pre-trained LLM without any task-specific fine-tuning to perform modular code planning and generation, and iteratively executes the generated code while inserting debugging probes to observe its behavior. This closed-loop structure facilitates systematic debugging and refinement, ultimately producing executable control programs. We apply the proposed framework to the calibration of an RGB-D camera and a robotic arm, validating its effectiveness in real-world settings. Furthermore, through a subsequent pick-and-place task, we demonstrate not only the accuracy of the calibration but also the potential extensibility of the framework. Across both tasks, the framework achieved high execution accuracy and autonomy, illustrating the practicality and scalability of LLM-based robotic control using our framework.

</details>


### [216] [TTT-VLA: Test-Time Latent Prompt Optimization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03127)
*Wenbo Zhang,Jianxiong Li,Shuai Yang,Sijin Chen,Jiajun Liu,Lingqiao Liu,Xiao Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TTT-VLA框架，通过测试时训练优化潜在提示（Latent Prompt），使VLA模型能够在部署阶段从交互数据中自适应调整策略行为，无需修改模型参数，实验证明能有效提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在部署时面临分布偏移问题，而基于提示的策略引导通常依赖外部干预，缺乏自适应能力。作者希望实现VLA模型的测试时训练（TTT），通过在部署阶段自主优化提示来适应新环境。

Method: 提出Latent Prompt Optimization (LPO)框架：训练阶段通过代理任务学习潜在提示作为额外条件信号；测试阶段仅收集环境交互数据，利用代理任务的自监督信号优化潜在提示，策略模型本身保持不变。

Result: 在SimplerEnv上的实验表明，该方法在单体和多体设置中均能持续提升任务成功率。进一步分析发现，性能提升主要源于纠正少量关键决策，而非全局改变策略行为。

Conclusion: LPO为部署阶段提升基础操作策略性能提供了一种有效且实用的路径，通过优化少量关键决策来实现适应，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models trained on large-scale data have made remarkable progress, but they remain vulnerable to distribution shifts at deployment time. Recent VLA models suggest that prompts can serve as an efficient interface for steering policy behavior, but existing prompt-based steering typically relies on external guidance. This raises a natural question: can test-time training (TTT) for VLA be achieved by optimizing a prompt, so that the steering interface itself can be learned and adapted from interaction? We address this question with TTT-VLA, a test-time training framework based on Latent Prompt Optimization (LPO). During training, the latent prompt is learned with an additional proxy task, providing an extra learned conditioning signal for policy learning. At test time, TTT is performed by collecting interaction data from the current environment and optimizing only the latent prompt on those data using the proxy task's self-supervised signal, without modifying the policy itself. Experiments on SimplerEnv demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves task success rates in both single- and multi-embodiment settings. Further analysis shows that the gains arise primarily from correcting a small number of critical decisions rather than globally altering policy behavior. These results suggest that LPO provides an effective and practical pathway for deployment-time improvement of foundation manipulation policies.

</details>


### [217] [How Visible Are Silent Manipulation Failures? An Observability Study of False-Success Detection in Simulated Robot Episodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03134)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人模仿学习中的虚假成功标签问题，发现仅靠本体感觉数据能否纠正这些错误取决于具体任务，但在真实场景中受传感器噪声限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习依赖自身的成功检测器来标注训练数据，但检测器可能产生"虚假成功"（实际失败却被标为成功）的错误，这种错误会严重损害策略学习质量。作者想探究：在已经标记为成功的轨迹中，仅靠本体感觉（关节数据）能否识别出这些虚假成功，还是必须依赖视觉信息。

Method: 在ALOHA双臂操作平台上构建两个模拟任务（方块转移和钉子插入），通过环境扰动诱导真实失败而非编辑标签，利用特权模拟器状态获得真实标签，筛选出机器人标记为成功但实际失败的轨迹。对比仅使用本体感觉（关节数据）的检测器与基于视觉的检测器在识别虚假成功上的表现。

Result: 两个任务表现差异巨大：方块转移任务中，虚假成功几乎完全可通过关节数据恢复；钉子插入任务中，本体感觉只能恢复部分虚假成功，而视觉检测器能弥补大部分差距。此外，本体感觉的可区分性依赖于远低于现实传感器噪声水平的微小速度差异。

Conclusion: 虚假成功的可恢复性高度依赖任务特性；本体感觉的效果应视为无噪声模拟器下的乐观上限，实际部署中传感器噪声会严重削弱其效果；视觉信息在需要精细区分时不可或缺。

Abstract: Imitation-learning policies for robot manipulation inherit the quality of the success labels attached to their training episodes, and those labels are usually produced by the robot's own success check. A particularly damaging error is the false success: an episode the robot logs as a success when the task outcome was actually wrong. We ask a narrow but practical question about these episodes. Once an episode has already been flagged as a success, how much of the information needed to overturn that label is present in proprioception, and how much requires vision? We build a simulated testbed on two bimanual ALOHA tasks, induce failures through environment perturbations rather than label edits, label every episode by privileged simulator state that the detector never sees, and keep only episodes the robot flagged as successful. We then compare detectors restricted to proprioception against a vision-based detector. We find that recoverability spans a wide range: in cube transfer the false successes are almost fully recoverable from joint data alone, while in peg insertion proprioception recovers only part of them and a vision detector closes most of the gap. We also show that the proprioceptive separability we measure rests on velocity differences far below any realistic sensor noise floor, so it is best read as an optimistic upper bound that a noiseless simulator inflates. We release the generation and evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [218] [ConTrack: Constrained Hand Motion Tracking with Adaptive Trade-off Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03177)
*Yutong Liang,Quanquan Peng,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConTrack是一个强化学习框架，通过将物体跟踪视为约束并分配剩余控制权给运动保真度，结合自适应中轨迹重置库，实现从人类演示到真实机器人的灵巧操作迁移，显著提升了成功率与物体位姿精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示提供了机器人操作的强先验，但由于运动学差异，难以直接迁移到真实机器人上。现有方法依赖手工设计的奖励函数，需要针对每条轨迹调整，且在有限交互预算下容易失败。因此需要一种可扩展的、无需繁琐调参的跟踪框架。

Method: ConTrack采用强化学习框架，将物体跟踪建模为约束条件，剩余控制能力分配给运动保真度，通过双变量更新在线自适应任务-风格权衡。同时引入自适应中轨迹重置库，重用策略可达的模拟器状态来稳定长周期学习。

Result: 在仿真跟踪和真实机器人实验中，ConTrack在成功率和物体位姿精度上显著优于先前方法，同时保持关节运动与接触保真度。

Conclusion: ConTrack通过约束式强化学习与自适应重置机制，有效解决了灵巧操作中长周期、高接触序列的跟踪难题，实现了从模拟到真实机器人的高效迁移。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide strong priors for robot manipulation, yet it is non-trivial to transfer them to execute on real robots due to the kinematic gap. In dexterous manipulation, it remains challenging to track long-horizon, contact-rich sequences even in simulators: a reference-tracking policy must keep objects on their target trajectories while preserving demonstrated joint motion and contact timing. Existing approaches often rely on hand-crafted reward tuning that require per-sequence tuning and break under limited interaction budgets. We introduce ConTrack, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that scales with tracking data. ConTrack treats object tracking as a constraint and allocates remaining control authority to motion fidelity, which allows it to adapt task--style trade-offs online using a dual-variable update. In addition, ConTrack also stabilizes long-horizon learning with an adaptive mid-trajectory reset library that reuses policy-reachable simulator states. Our qualitative and quantitative results in simulation tracking and real robot demonstrate that ConTrack improves success and object pose accuracy significantly over prior arts while preserving joint and contact fidelity. Website: https://www.lyt0112.com/projects/ConTrack.

</details>


### [219] [GeoSem-WAM: Geometry- and Semantic-Aware World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03188)
*Fulong Ma,Daojie Peng,Wenjun Yue,Jiahang Cao,Bintao Wang,Qiang Zhang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化世界建模框架，通过几何和语义监督增强潜在表征，在不增加测试时推理开销的情况下显著提升了世界动作模型在具身决策中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型(WAMs)的成功究竟是源于测试时的未来想象还是预测训练带来的表征学习尚不清楚，且现有RGB-based未来预测缺乏结构化空间理解。

Method: 在原有RGB未来预测基础上，引入两个辅助预测分支：未来几何表征和未来语义表征，在统一潜在空间中联合捕捉场景动态、空间几何和语义上下文。

Result: 在多个具身场景中，结构化世界监督一致提升了动作预测精度、场景理解能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结构化世界建模能有效提升WAMs的性能和可扩展性，且不增加测试时推理成本，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Recent World Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in embodied decision-making. However, whether their effectiveness stems from explicit future imagination during inference or representation learning induced by predictive training remains an open question. Emerging evidence suggests the primary advantage lies in learning robust latent representations rather than generating future observations at test time. Nevertheless, existing WAMs mainly rely on RGB-based future prediction, which provides limited structural and spatial understanding of complex environments. To address this, we propose a structured world modeling framework that enhances latent representations through geometric and semantic supervision. Alongside future RGB prediction, our model introduces two auxiliary prediction branches for future geometry and semantic representations, enabling it to jointly capture scene dynamics, spatial geometry, and semantic context within a unified latent space. Crucially, our approach preserves efficient inference by avoiding explicit future rollout or video generation at test time. Extensive experiments show that incorporating structured world supervision consistently improves action prediction accuracy, scene understanding, and robustness under challenging embodied scenarios, highlighting its potential for advancing scalable and efficient WAMs.

</details>


### [220] [Toward Gripper-Integrated Active Electrosense for Pre-Contact Sensing in Underwater Soft Grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03204)
*Ahsan Tanveer,Muhammad Hamza,Waqar Hussain Afridi,Chen Wang,Guangming Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了在浑浊、强光和夹爪遮挡等低能见度水下环境中，将主动电感应作为一种轻量级感知模态，集成到章鱼仿生软体夹爪中，通过测量导电介质中电场扰动，在接触前提供类接近感信号，实验表明多电极电压读数能检测到物体相关的结构化变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下操作常因浊度、眩光和夹爪遮挡导致视觉感知受限，而软体夹爪虽适合柔性交互，但缺乏接触前的接近感知信号来引导抓取动作，因此需要一种替代或补充的预接触感知方式。

Method: 将主动电感应集成到章鱼仿生软体夹爪中，采用离散电极布局和多通道电压记录，通过测量外加电场在导电介质中受物体扰动后的电压变化，实现类接近感信号，并在仿真和盐水箱实验中用悬浮导电球进行验证。

Result: 多电极电压读数相比空水基线呈现出结构化、依赖目标的明显变化，检测效果受激励电压（5-20V）和频率（1mHz-1kHz）影响，证明了电感应信号的可检测性。

Conclusion: 夹爪集成的主动电感应有望作为水下软体操作中视觉感知的补充预接触信号，值得进一步系统研究。

Abstract: Underwater manipulation often occurs under degraded visibility due to turbidity, glare, and gripper occlusion, limiting the reliability of vision-based perception during approach and grasping. In such settings, soft grippers are well suited for compliant interaction, but they typically lack an onboard pre-contact cue that can guide approach and closure when vision is unreliable. This extended abstract explores active electrosense as a lightweight sensing modality that can provide a proximity-like signal prior to contact by measuring perturbations of an applied electric field in conductive media. We instrument an octopus-inspired gripper with a discrete electrode layout and record multi-channel sensing voltages using off-the-shelf hardware. Simulation and tank experiments with a suspended conductive sphere show structured, object-dependent changes in the multi-electrode voltage readout relative to empty-water baselines, with detectability varying across excitation of 5 to 20 V and frequencies from 1 mHz to 1 kHz.
