These findings motivate systematic investigation of gripper-integrated electrosense as a complementary pre-contact cue for underwater soft manipulation.

</details>


### [221] [BotDirector: Robot Storytelling Across the Symmetrical Reality with Multi-modal Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03223)
*Zhe Sun,Meng Wang,Lei Wang,Yuxi Wang,Wanxin Li,Yujia Peng,Zhenliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个结合实体玩具和自然语言交互的机器人讲故事系统，让幼儿能用日常物品和LLM代理创建叙事，并让自导航群体机器人执行动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人讲故事虽然能吸引儿童，但技术层面过于复杂，儿童难以参与创作过程。

Method: 儿童用实体物品布置场景，通过自然语言与LLM代理交互创建叙事，叙事被转化为基于地图和角色的运动序列，由自导航群体机器人执行。

Result: 该系统增强了机器人讲故事的灵活性，使幼儿能用日常物品创作机器人戏剧。

Conclusion: 通过实体交互与LLM的结合，降低了儿童参与机器人故事创作的技术门槛，实现了更自然、灵活的讲故事体验。

Abstract: Robot storytelling offers a unique blend of technological innovation and creative expression that engages children in unprecedented ways. However, the technical aspects are often too complicated for children. We propose an interactive system that facilitates robot storytelling with tangible and natural language interactions. Children arrange the playground with their own stuff and create narratives with an LLM agent. The created narratives are transformed into a motion sequence based on the map and characters, and the motions are executed by self-navigating swarm robots. This system enhances robot storytelling with flexible scenarios, enabling young children to create robot dramas with everyday objects.

</details>


### [222] [GeoAlign: Beyond Semantics with State-Guided Spatial Alignment in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03240)
*Yizhi Chen,Zhanxiang Cao,Xinyi Peng,Yixiao Zheng,Xiaxi Si,Yiheng Li,Liyun Yan,Keqi Zhu,Xueyun Chen,Shengcheng Fu,Tianyue Zhan,Yufei Jia,Jinming Yao,Yan Xie,Kun Wang,Cewu Lu,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoAlign是一个面向VLA策略学习的空间对齐架构，通过RGB几何分支的后训练和机器人本体状态引导的特征查询，在多个基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要优化语义理解，但可执行操作需要几何感知的空间对齐和动态affordance选择，现有方法在这两方面存在不足。

Method: 1) 使用RGB-D数据对RGB几何分支进行后训练，生成GEP特征；2) 机器人本体状态作为查询，从GEP特征网格中提取相位相关的紧凑几何token，用于动作预测。

Result: LIBERO上99.0%，SimplerEnv-Fractal三个任务平均85.3%，真实ALOHA几何关键任务上78.8%。

Conclusion: 几何后训练和本体状态引导的查询机制对提升空间对齐能力至关重要，GeoAlign有效弥合了语义理解与几何操作之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Current Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models often optimize for semantic grounding, whereas executable manipulation requires geometry-aware spatial alignment and dynamic affordance selection. We introduce GeoAlign, a state-guided spatial alignment architecture for VLA policy learning. GeoAlign post-trains an RGB geometry branch with robot-domain RGB-D supervision, yielding RGB-derived Geometry-Enhanced Post-Trained (GEP) features for policy rollout. The robot's proprioceptive state queries the GEP feature grid, producing compact, phase-dependent geometry tokens for action prediction. GeoAlign achieves 99.0% on LIBERO, 85.3% across three SimplerEnv-Fractal tasks, and 78.8% on eight geometry-critical real-world ALOHA tasks, with ablations confirming the value of geometry post-training and proprioceptive-state-guided querying.

</details>


### [223] [AirDreamer: Generalist Drone Navigation with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03252)
*Zian Liu,Andong Yang,Chunkai Yang,Ruidong An,Chao Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习和世界模型的无人机导航框架，在复杂未知环境中实现了更高的导航成功率（比最佳基线高5.3%），并实现了无需调优的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机导航方法严重依赖人工设计的感知流程和预定义规则，环境泛化能力差。受动物导航行为启发，希望设计一个不依赖人工调参、能在未知复杂环境中可靠导航的框架。

Method: 设计了一个导航框架：底层基于世界模型的环境理解，上层使用强化学习策略进行导航；采用无需人工设计的稀疏奖励函数，避免局部最优并鼓励偏航控制行为。

Result: 在仿真和真实无人机上，该方法在复杂未知地图中导航成功率比最佳基线高5.3%，能逃脱其他方法失败的局部最优陷阱，并实现了无需调整的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过将世界模型与强化学习结合，辅以稀疏奖励设计，有效提升了无人机在未知复杂环境中的导航泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Navigating a drone in unseen and cluttered environments requires reliable generalization to unseen scene layouts and understanding of environmental structure relative to the robot's capabilities. Previous methods, which assume the same environment configuration, often rely heavily on human-designed perception pipelines and predefined rules to guide the robot toward the target. This process is environment-dependent and generalizes poorly across environments. Inspired by animal navigation behavior, we design a navigation framework that navigates with a reinforcement-learning-based policy on top of a world-model-based environment understanding to overcome these issues. In addition, a sparse reward function without hand-crafted shaping terms is designed to avoid local minima traps and encourage yaw control behaviors. In simulation and on real drones, our method exhibits emergent capabilities for navigating complex, unseen environments and escaping local optima where other methods fail. In challenging maps, it achieves a 5.3% higher navigation success rate than best baseline. Furthermore, the proposed framework achieves effective sim-to-real transfer without any tuning during deployment. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [224] [Wheel-Mounted/GNSS Fusion with AI-Aided Position Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03265)
*Gal Versano,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合神经惯性导航框架，结合轮式惯性传感器、强制周期性轨迹和轻量神经网络，在误差状态扩展卡尔曼滤波中回归车辆位移，显著提升定位精度，RMSE降低约46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主地面车辆在GNSS信号受限环境下，准确且鲁棒的定位仍是基础性挑战，现有轮式惯性传感器融合方法精度不足。

Method: 提出混合神经惯性导航框架：采用轮式安装的惯性传感器，利用强制周期性轨迹提高惯性信噪比，使用简单高效的神经网络仅凭惯性读数回归车辆位移，并在误差状态扩展卡尔曼滤波中融合GNSS位置更新。

Result: 通过多组轮式惯性传感器的真实世界实验验证，定位均方根误差（RMSE）相比标准轮式惯性传感器与GNSS融合方法降低约46%。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合神经惯性导航框架能有效提升定位精度，周期性轨迹增强策略结合轻量神经网络是实现高精度定位的关键。

Abstract: Accurate and robust localization remains a fundamental challenge for autonomous ground vehicles. In this work, we propose a hybrid neural inertial navigation framework that integrates a wheel-mounted inertial sensors, enforced periodic trajectories, and a simple, efficient neural network capable of regressing vehicle displacement with GNSS position updates in an error-state extended Kalman filter. The periodic trajectories increase the inertial signal-to-noise ratio, allowing the network to use only inertial readings to estimate displacement. The approach is validated through real-world experiments using multiple wheel-mounted inertial sensors. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a significant improvement in positioning accuracy, reducing the position root mean squared error by approximately 46 % compared to standard wheel-mounted inertial sensor fusion with GNSS updates.

</details>


### [225] [EaDex: A Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation Framework from Low-Cost Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03268)
*Qian Zhao,Xin Tong,Chengdong Wu,Yang Yang,Yingtian Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EaDex框架，通过低成本RGB-D摄像头采集人手数据、MANO建模和动态演示退火机制，实现多形态灵巧手的高效操作学习，在9种跨形态任务中相对基线提升55.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习长期受困于数据收集和训练的高成本问题——纯强化学习需要大规模交互探索，模仿学习则依赖昂贵的高质量演示数据。本文旨在解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 数据层面：单RGB-D相机捕捉人手运动，经MANO手部建模、数据归一化和运动重定向构建结构化演示数据。学习层面：提出基于接触奖励的动态演示退火机制，早期由演示引导探索，随接触奖励积累逐步过渡到自主优化。

Result: 在3种灵巧手和3种物体打开任务上共9种跨形态设置中评估，相比无演示退火的基线方法，相对性能提升55.3%。

Conclusion: 验证了所提出的低成本演示管线以及动态演示退火策略在灵巧操作学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation learning has long been hindered by the high costs of data and training, as pure reinforcement learning typically requires large-scale interactive exploration and imitation learning depends on high-quality demonstrations that are expensive to collect. To address this problem, we propose EaDex, a multi-embodiment dexterous manipulation learning framework under low-cost demonstration conditions, which enables rapid generation of demonstration data and consequently reduces training time for efficient dexterous manipulation. At the data level, EaDex captures human hand motions using only a single RGB-D camera and constructs structured demonstration data through MANO-based hand modeling, data normalization, and motion retargeting. At the learning level, we introduce a contact-reward-based dynamic demonstration annealing mechanism, which guides early-stage exploration under demonstration and gradually transitions to autonomous optimization with accumulating contact rewards. Using our custom dataset, we evaluate EaDex on three dexterous hands and three articulated object-opening tasks, covering nine cross-embodiment manipulation settings, achieving a 55.3% relative improvement over the baseline without demonstration annealing. These results validate the effectiveness of the proposed low-cost demonstration pipeline and the dynamic demonstration annealing strategy for dexterous manipulation learning.

</details>


### [226] [Bridging Predictive Uncertainty and Safe Action: Sample-Conditioned Differentiable Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03296)
*Chengzhen Meng,Pei Liu,Zhiyu Huang,Chen Lv,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种样本条件可微规划框架，通过将扩散模型生成的多样化未来轨迹直接融入优化过程，并引入CVaR尾风险约束，弥合不确定性建模与安全运动规划之间的鸿沟，在Waymo和Argoverse数据集上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶系统中，高表达力的不确定性建模与可解释、安全的运动规划之间存在脱节（disconnect），这是性能瓶颈的核心原因。

Method: 1) 使用条件扩散模型生成多样化的未来场景样本；2) 将这些样本直接输入可微规划器；3) 引入经验条件风险价值（CVaR）作为尾风险约束，显式缓解预测不确定性；4) 提出有向图表示场景上下文，提升预测效率与效果。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion和Argoverse 2数据集上的开环与闭环评估中，该方法在安全性、效率和乘坐舒适性上均显著优于SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 通过将扩散生成样本与可微规划结合，并加入CVaR约束，能够有效桥接不确定性建模与安全规划之间的鸿沟，实现更鲁棒、可解释的自动驾驶决策。

Abstract: Complex, dynamic, and interactive driving environments pose significant challenges for autonomous driving, primarily due to the pervasive uncertainty of surrounding traffic. A fundamental bottleneck in current systems is the disconnect between highly expressive uncertainty modeling and interpretable, safe motion planning. In this paper, we propose a novel sample-conditioned differentiable planning framework that bridges this gap by explicitly incorporating diffusion-generated future trajectories into the optimization process. Rather than compressing predictions into a single deterministic future or relying on black-box end-to-end architectures, our approach leverages a conditional diffusion model to generate a diverse set of plausible future scenarios. Crucially, these samples are directly fed into a differentiable planner, which explicitly mitigates predictive uncertainty via an empirical Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) tail-risk constraint. This allows the planner to optimize a physically interpretable trajectory that is robust to rare yet safety-critical interactions. Furthermore, we introduce a directed graph representation for scene context that yields substantial improvements in both predictive effectiveness and computational efficiency. Validated through extensive open-loop and closed-loop evaluations on the Waymo Open Motion and Argoverse 2 datasets, our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in safety, efficiency, and ride comfort.

</details>


### [227] [SplitAdapter: Load-Aware Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Factorized Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03297)
*Jeonguk Kang,Hanbyel Cho,Sanghyun Kang,Donghan Koo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对人形机器人操作中重物负载导致的多因素耦合问题，提出SplitAdapter，通过分解式上下文编码器、分裂世界模型、对抗正则化和分层FiLM，在sim-to-real场景下显著提升重载操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于历史信息的适配器将负载变化和机器人动态失配压缩为单一隐变量，在重载操作时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 冻结预训练策略，引入对象/负载编码器和动态感知编码器；使用分裂世界模型目标训练；采用GRL跨对抗正则化；通过分层FiLM调制特征。

Result: 在sim-to-sim和真实部署中，对2、4、6kg负载及0、30、60cm高度，SplitAdapter成功率全面超越基线，重载条件下提升最大。

Conclusion: 分解式因子适应能有效解耦负载与动态变化，提升人形机器人重载操作在sim-to-real迁移中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires stable whole-body control under varying object masses and pickup/placement heights. This becomes particularly challenging in sim-to-real transfer, where object-induced load variation and robot-side dynamics mismatch interact during physical contact. Existing history-based adapters often compress these factors into a single latent representation, which can weaken robustness under heavy-load manipulation. We propose \textbf{SplitAdapter: Load-Aware Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Factorized Adaptation}, which freezes a pretrained box manipulation policy and extends it with object/load and dynamics-aware context encoders trained with split world-model objectives, GRL-based cross-adversarial regularization, and hierarchical Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM). In sim-to-sim experiments and real-world deployment, SplitAdapter improves Full-task success over the base policy and world-model FiLM baselines across object masses of $2$, $4$, and $6$ kg and pickup/placement heights of $0$, $30$, and $60$ cm, with the largest improvements under heavy-load conditions.

</details>


### [228] [RobotValues: Evaluating Household Robots When Human Values Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03312)
*Jongwook Han,Hyeongjin Kim,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RobotValues基准，在10K个价值冲突的家庭场景中评估机器人规划者的价值偏好。研究发现视觉语言模型存在默认价值偏好（安全、包容），难以在冲突时覆盖默认行为，错误率高达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有家庭机器人评估仅关注任务完成，但真实场景中存在价值冲突（如人类自主性、效率、社交适宜性），缺乏评估机器人价值偏好的基准。

Method: 通过LLM辅助场景生成、利益相关者驱动的价值提取、图像生成和自动质量控制构建RobotValues基准，包含10K个价值冲突场景。

Result: VLM表现出默认价值偏好（安全、包容），忽视隐私优先行为；当指令与默认偏好冲突时，80%情况下选择错误动作。

Conclusion: 家庭机器人评估应不仅衡量任务完成和安全合规，还需评估其在人类价值冲突时选择合理行为的能力。

Abstract: While household robots are often evaluated based on task completion, everyday domestic environments involve value-conflicting situations in which robots are expected to choose actions that prioritize other values than task success, such as human autonomy, efficiency, or social appropriateness. Yet, there are no benchmarks for evaluating robots' value preferences in such scenarios. We introduce RobotValues, a benchmark to evaluate household robot planners in 10K value-conflict scenarios. Each instance consists of a realistic household image with multiple plausible robot actions that prioritize different human values. We construct RobotValues through LLM-assisted scenario generation, stakeholder-grounded value extraction, image generation and automatic quality control. Using RobotValues we evaluate VLMs used in robotics and find that models exhibit default value preferences, including safety and accommodation, while underselecting privacy-prioritizing actions. When the models are instructed to prioritize specific values that conflict with their own preferences, they often fail to override their default actions, choosing incorrect actions for 80% of the time. These findings suggest that household robot evaluation should measure not only task completion or safety compliance, but also whether robots can choose among plausible actions when human values conflict.

</details>


### [229] [GPU-Parallel Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Demonstration Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03335)
*Rui Zhang,Qiwei Wu,Zhengyu Zhang,Tao Li,Yunrong Guo,Junjie Lai,Renjing Xu,Weihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将结构化操作任务族转化为GPU并行多任务强化学习基准的构建方法（MT-Libero），并设计了基于演示引导的在线策略方法DGPO，结合重要性加权PPO与自适应行为克隆，在稀疏奖励和有限先验数据条件下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU并行强化学习大多针对单一任务训练专用策略，缺乏支持异构多任务套件的并行基准，且稀疏奖励和有限先验数据使得多任务训练在实际中难以落地。

Method: 1) 构建MT-Libero基准：利用LIBERO资产和Isaac Lab中的任务谓词，支持异构任务套件的同时强化学习，具备并行渲染、物理随机化和状态/视觉输入策略。2) 提出DGPO：在在线策略PPO中融入重要性加权PPO与自适应行为克隆，利用匹配的演示动作引导训练，实现可调节的演示任务分布偏好。

Result: DGPO在稀疏奖励和有限先验数据条件下，优于无先验强化学习和现有基于演示的方法，同时保持了在线策略PPO的稳定性和在线改进能力。

Conclusion: 通过MT-Libero基准和DGPO方法，证明了结合演示引导的在线策略在多任务机器人操作学习中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Large scale GPU-parallel reinforcement learning has changed what can be trained in robot simulation, yet most systems still optimize one specialist policy per task. We propose a construction methodology for turning structured manipulation task families into GPU-parallel multi-task RL benchmarks, and instantiate it as MT-Libero using LIBERO assets and task predicates in Isaac Lab. The resulting benchmark supports simultaneous reinforcement learning over heterogeneous task suites with parallel rendering, physics randomization, and state-input or visual-input policies. To make such training practical under sparse success signals and limited prior data, we further propose DGPO, an on-policy demonstration guided method that combines importance weighted PPO with adaptive behavior cloning on matched demonstration actions. DGPO enables a tunable preference toward demonstrated task distributions, outperforming both prior-free RL and existing demonstration-based methods while preserving the stability and online improvement benefits of on-policy PPO.

</details>


### [230] [Autonomous Navigation System for Library Service Robot Based on Unitree Go2 Edu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03340)
*Aoduo Li,Haoran Lv,Bingquan Ou,Jianfeng Li,Yingdong Li,Zimeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于ROS 2的四足机器人导航系统，在真实图书馆场景中实现了高成功率（静态100%、低动态96%、高动态88%）和3.7cm的建图精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图书馆环境需要机器人安静、安全地穿行于狭窄过道，同时避开读者、椅子、包包和手推车等动态障碍。现有轮式平台对地面过渡、临时杂物和部分阻塞通道等实际部署中的不连续性容忍度较低，因此需要更灵活的四足机器人方案。

Method: 使用Unitree Go2 Edu四足机器人，搭载4D LiDAR、前视深度相机和IMU。采用RTAB-Map进行视觉-LiDAR SLAM，AMCL和EKF融合实现定位，Nav2栈配合A*全局规划与DWA局部避障。在真实图书馆中进行静态、低密度动态和高密度动态三种场景的实验。

Result: 在真实图书馆中，静态场景成功率达100%，低密度动态场景96%，高密度动态场景88%。地图验证的均方度量误差为3.7cm。

Conclusion: 该四足机器人导航系统在图书馆复杂动态环境中表现优异，验证了基于ROS 2的导航方案在室内非结构化场景中的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Libraries require autonomous robots to move quietly through narrow aisles while remaining safe around readers, chairs, bags, and carts. This paper presents a ROS 2 navigation system for a Unitree Go2 Edu quadruped equipped with a 4D LiDAR, a front depth camera, and an IMU. Rather than assuming the library is rough terrain, we target the practical mobility discontinuities of real deployments, including floor transitions, temporary clutter, and partially blocked passages where low-clearance wheeled platforms are less tolerant. RTAB-Map is used for visual-LiDAR SLAM, AMCL and EKF-based sensor fusion provide localization, and a Nav2 stack with A* and DWA supports planning and local avoidance. In a real library, the system achieves 100%, 96%, and 88% success rates in static, low-density dynamic, and high-density dynamic scenes, while map validation against surveyed control distances yields a mean metric error of 3.7 cm.

</details>


### [231] [eMEM: A Hybrid Spatio-Temporal Memory System For Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03374)
*A. Haroon Rasheed,Maria Kabtoul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: eMEM是一个面向具身智能体的混合图记忆系统，结合SQLite、hnswlib和R-tree多索引架构，通过分层记忆整合管线模拟海马体-新皮质的记忆巩固过程，在设计的eMEM-Bench v1基准测试（涵盖8项认知心理学范式）上达到80.8加权平均分，且记忆曲线在1小时到1年模拟延迟下保持平稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统（如Generative Agents、MemGPT、A-MEM）将记忆视为文本流或知识图谱，但具身智能体需要同时能按语义、空间和时间维度检索的记忆，现有系统无法满足这一需求。

Method: 提出混合图记忆系统eMEM，采用多索引架构：SQLite用于结构化存储、hnswlib用于近似最近邻语义搜索、R-tree用于空间查询，统一在单一图模型下。设计了分层记忆整合管线，将原始感知观察压缩为摘要，模拟生物系统的海马体-新皮质记忆巩固过程。提供10个面向智能体的召回工具，将概念到位置的解析和跨层召回等操作作为LLM工具调用的头等操作。

Result: 在988个探针上达到80.8加权平均分；纯RAG基线在上下文依赖检索上损失30分，在DRM诱饵拒绝上损失29分，证明了多层存储和记忆巩固各自的贡献。

Conclusion: eMEM在具身记忆的综合检索任务上表现优异，记忆曲线平坦（1小时到1年模拟延迟），且通过消融实验验证了多索引架构和记忆巩固管线的有效性。系统完全嵌入式运行，无需外部服务。

Abstract: We present eMEM (Embodied Memory), a hybrid graph-based memory system for embodied agents operating in physical environments. Current agent memory architectures, such as Generative Agents, MemGPT, and A-MEM, treat memory as text streams or knowledge graphs, but embodied agents require memory that is simultaneously searchable by meaning, space, and time. eMEM fills this gap with a multi-index architecture (SQL ITE for structured storage, hnswlib for approximate nearest neighbour semantic search, and an R-tree for spatial queries) unified behind a single graph model. A tiered consolidation pipeline transforms raw perceptual observations into compressed summaries, mirroring hippocampal-neocortical consolidation in biological systems. Ten agent-facing recall tools expose memory retrieval primitives, including concept-to-location resolution and cross layer recall, as first-class operations for LLM tool calling. The system is fully embedded and runs in-process alongside the agent. In addition we introduce eMEM-Bench v1, a benchmark we construct over ProcTHOR-10K scenes for embodied memory evaluation. The benchmark is organised explicitly around eight cognitive-psychology paradigms (DRM lures, pattern separation, pattern completion, source monitoring, context-dependent retrieval, long-horizon interference, serial position, and a foil augmented retention curve), each chosen so that the result is interpretable against the broader memory-systems literature in humans and prior agent-memory systems; a level of diagnostic that surface-task benchmarks like LoCoMo or OpenEQA cannot provide. eMEM scores 80.8 weighted mean over 988 probes, with a flat retention curve at ceiling from 1 h to 1 yr of simulated delay on room-unique items. We show that a pure RAG baseline (the flat_rag ablation) loses 30 pt on context dependent retrieval and 29 pt on DRM lure rejection, isolating the contribution of multi-layer storage and consolidation respectively. We release both the system and the benchmark code.

</details>


### [232] [Grasp-Then-Plan with Failure Attribution: A Closed Two-Stage Framework for Precise and Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03385)
*Jiahao Xu,Peiyuan Wang,Hanzhuo Zhang,Zihao Yu,Tianyu Fu,Hao Chen,Xuanhao Xiang,Jianbo Yu,Chenchen Fu,Wanyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GTP-FA框架，通过抓取-规划两阶段解耦与故障归因，实现长程机器人操作的诊断驱动优化，显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中抓取与运动规划紧耦合常导致失败根源不明、试错效率低，现有方法难以实现高效的长程操作。

Method: 1) 两阶段抓取-规划框架（GTP-FA）；2) 学习故障归因模型泛化到未见抓取，生成稳定故障模式分布；3) 基于归因结果对抓取模块注入任务先验与风险惩罚，对规划模块针对高风险初始态进行数据收集与微调。

Result: 在仿真和真机实验中，GTP-FA在RL、IL、扩散策略、VLA等多种基线上均显著提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: 解耦抓取与规划并引入故障归因的诊断驱动优化，是提升长程机器人操作效率与成功率的有效范式。

Abstract: In robotic manipulation, the tight coupling between grasping and motion planning often obscures the true source of failure, leading to inefficient trial-and-error. To enable efficient long-horizon manipulation, we propose GTP-FA (Grasp-Then-Plan with Failure Attribution), a task-oriented two-stage grasp-then-plan framework that generates grasp candidates and performs downstream motion planning conditioned on the selected grasp. Given a failed manipulation trajectory, we learn a failure attribution model that generalizes to unseen grasps and produces a stable distribution over failure modes for diagnosis-guided optimization. Based on these attribution results, we then optimize both modules in a diagnosis-driven manner: on the grasping side, we inject task-level priors and risk penalties into grasp candidate scoring and optimization to suppress unstable or task-incompatible grasps; on the planning side, we target high-risk initial states through data collection and fine-tuning to address genuine planning bottlenecks. We evaluate the proposed framework in both simulation and real-robot experiments, and show that GTP-FA improves the corresponding base learners across RL, IL, diffusion-policy, and VLA-based settings, achieving substantially higher overall task success rates.

</details>


### [233] [Extreme Motion Generation via Hybrid Null-Space Control for Straight-Line Path Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03390)
*Xinyi Yuan,Weiwei Wan,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合控制框架，结合强化学习策略和模型控制方法，用于最大化机械臂沿预定轨迹的笛卡尔路径长度（极端运动生成），在7自由度Franka FR3上实现平均27%的路径延长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端运动可以充分利用固定基座机械臂在有限可达范围内的运动学能力，对工业中路径跟踪任务（如涂覆、焊接）至关重要。然而，在安全边界附近执行长时域决策具有挑战性。

Method: 提出步级混合控制器：基于标准化关节极限距离在RL策略和模型控制器之间切换；采用条件扩散采样初始化关节配置，利用学习到的运动先验提高可达路径长度。

Result: 在10,000条直线路径跟踪任务中，平均 rollout 长度比纯模型基线提升27%，部分任务在极端运动方向上表现出显著延伸。

Conclusion: 将长时域决策委托给学习策略，近边界区域由经典模型控制器覆盖，可有效实现极端运动生成。

Abstract: This work studies ``extreme motion generation'', which aims to maximize the Cartesian path length along a pre-defined trajectory within the manipulator's workspace. This objective is important in industry as long as path-following is fundamental to a large variety of tasks such as surface coating and welding. More critically, extreme motion enables a fixed-base manipulator to exploit the kinematic capability under limited reachability. However, such exploitation is challenging in practice, as the manipulator must actively avoid the safety boundary through execution, which is inherently a long-horizon problem. Accordingly, we claim that long-horizon decision-making should be delegated to a learning-based policy to maximize exploitation, while a classical model-based controller covers the near-boundary region, where the learning policy degrades sharply due to sparse data coverage. In detail, our proposed method is a step-level hybrid controller that switches between an RL-based and a model-based controller according to the normalized joint-limit distance. The initial joint configuration is sampled through conditional diffusion-based sampling, which improves the achievable path length based on the learned motion prior. We evaluate the proposed framework on 10,000 straight-line path-following tasks with a 7-DoF Franka FR3, extending the average rollout length by 27\% over the model-based baseline. Notably, certain tasks yield a pronounced extension toward the motion extreme, as reflected in the maximum improvement reported in the statistical results. The project website and related videos of this paper can be found at https://yuan-xinyi.github.io/extreme-motion-generation/.

</details>


### [234] [OpenEAI-Platform: An Open-source Embodied Artificial Intelligence Hardware-Software Unified Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03392)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Luoyi Fan,Leiyu Wang,Yeqiang Wang,Yicheng Zhu,Cewu Lu,Nanyang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenEAI-Platform是一个完全开源的具身AI平台，包含低成本6+1自由度机械臂OpenEAI-Arm和可复现的VLA模型OpenEAI-VLA，在真实操作任务中性能优于商用机械臂，且仅用少量预训练数据就达到与大规模基线pi0相当的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI系统依赖昂贵的商业硬件和闭源模型，缺乏可复现、低成本的开源平台，阻碍了研究的可扩展性和数据收集的可规模化。

Method: 设计低成本6+1自由度开源机械臂（OpenEAI-Arm）并提供符合性控制方法；基于Qwen3-VL-4B构建VLA模型（OpenEAI-VLA），采用Diffusion Transformer动作头，两阶段训练仅使用开源机器人数据和多模态数据集。

Result: OpenEAI-Arm在四项真实操作任务中优于两款同自由度商用机械臂；OpenEAI-VLA在仅有限预训练数据下达到与大规模预训练pi0基线相当的成功率。

Conclusion: 开源硬件和软件结合可以有效降低具身AI研究门槛，在低成本条件下达到甚至超越商业系统的性能。

Abstract: Embodied AI in the real world requires both accurate hardware and robust vision-language-action (VLA) policies. We present OpenEAI-Platform, a fully open-source platform that integrates a low-cost 6+1 degree-of-freedom (dof) robotic arm (OpenEAI-Arm) and a reproducible VLA model (OpenEAI-VLA). OpenEAI-Arm provides open-source mechanical designs for low manufacturing cost and compliant control methods for higher accuracy. OpenEAI-VLA builds on Qwen3-VL-4B and uses a Diffusion Transformer action head, and is trained in two stages with only open-source robot and multimodal datasets. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, OpenEAI-Arm outperforms two commercial 6+1-dof arms under the same policy, and OpenEAI-VLA achieves success rates comparable to the large-scale pretrained pi0 baseline with only limited pretraining data. We will release the full hardware designs, drivers, models, and training/data pipelines to support reproducible research and scalable data collection. Our codes, layouts, and models will be released after the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [235] [Reliability-Guided Depth Fusion for Glare-Resilient Navigation Costmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03421)
*Shang-En Tsai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于显式深度可靠性建模的防眩光代价地图构建方法，通过DRM-Net预测像素级测量可信度，并利用RGF机制在占用更新前过滤被眩光破坏的深度数据，有效减少室内导航中的虚假障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动立体RGB-D传感器在反射地板、玻璃墙面等眩光环境下会产生深度空洞和尖峰，这些错误测量会累计为持久性幻影障碍物，严重威胁室内导航的安全性。

Method: 1) 构建轻量级DRM-Net预测每像素深度可靠性；2) 设计可靠性引导的加权-门控融合(RGF)机制，在占用栅格更新前对不可靠测量进行抑制；3) 采用多视角参考深度构建减少循环监督偏差。

Result: 在搭载RealSense D435和Jetson Orin Nano的移动机器人平台上，该方法在反射地板、玻璃墙和自然光眩光条件下显著减少了虚假障碍物插入，提高了自由空间保持率，并保持实时运算性能。

Conclusion: 应将眩光视为测量可靠性问题而非密集深度补全问题，这对安全关键的室内导航具有重要意义。

Abstract: Specular glare on reflective floors, glass boundaries, and glossy indoor surfaces frequently corrupts active-stereo RGB-D depth measurements, producing holes and spikes that accumulate as persistent phantom obstacles in occupancy-grid costmaps. This paper presents a glare-resilient costmap construction method based on explicit depth-reliability modeling. A lightweight Depth Reliability Map network (DRM-Net) predicts per-pixel measurement trustworthiness under specular interference, and a reliability-guided weighted-and-gated fusion (RGF) mechanism modulates occupancy updates before corrupted measurements are accumulated into the map. To support robust training and evaluation, the method uses pose-aligned multi-view reference-depth construction to reduce circular-supervision bias and is evaluated through fusion-variant ablations, parameter-sensitivity analysis, cross-condition tests, paired navigation comparisons, reliability-map metrics, and embedded runtime profiling. Experiments on a real mobile robotic platform equipped with an Intel RealSense D435 and a Jetson Orin Nano show that the proposed method reduces false obstacle insertion, improves free-space preservation, and maintains real-time throughput under reflective-floor, glass-wall, and natural-light glare conditions. These results support treating glare as a measurement-reliability problem rather than as a dense depth-completion problem for safety-critical indoor navigation.

</details>


### [236] [PerchRL: Vision-Based Agile Perching on Inclined Platforms under Rapid and Irregular Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03441)
*Zihong Lu,Zongzhuo Liu,Huaxu Li,Jinqiang Cui,Jie Mei,Youmin Gong,U Kei Cheang,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PerchRL框架，通过两阶段强化学习（状态预训练+视觉微调）实现四旋翼无人机在快速不规则运动的倾斜平台上进行自主视觉降落，解决视野有限问题，在仿真与真实实验中验证了可行性、稳定性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主视觉降落在移动倾斜平台上的方法受限于视野（FOV）不足，难以应对平台的快速和不规则运动，亟需一种鲁棒且能处理间歇性视觉丢失的解决方案。

Method: 采用两阶段强化学习策略：1）基于状态的预训练；2）基于视觉的微调。通过随机化平台轨迹防止过拟合，使用时序增强提取历史运动模式；在视觉微调阶段引入可见性感知状态增强和主动感知奖励机制，提升间歇性视觉丢失下的鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真和真实实验验证了PerchRL的可行性、稳定性和实时性；在不同四旋翼平台上的成功部署证明了其良好的适应性。

Conclusion: PerchRL有效解决了视野受限下在移动倾斜平台上的敏捷降落问题，具备跨平台泛化能力，开源代码将推动相关领域发展。

Abstract: Autonomous vision-based perching of quadrotors on moving inclined platforms is critical for air-ground collaboration but remains challenging due to the limited field of view (FOV). In this paper, we propose PerchRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for vision-based agile perching on inclined platforms under rapid and irregular motion. Specifically, we employ a two-stage learning strategy consisting of state-based pre-training followed by vision-based fine-tuning. To improve generalization across diverse platform motions, we employ randomized platform trajectories to prevent overfitting and temporal augmentation methods to capture latent motion patterns from historical observations. During vision-based fine-tuning, a hybrid learning framework consisting of visibility-aware state augmentation and active perception rewards is presented to improve robustness under intermittent visual loss. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the feasibility, stability, and real-time performance of PerchRL, while successful deployment across distinct quadrotor platforms further validates its adaptability. The source code will be released to benefit the community.

</details>


### [237] [Human2Humanoid: Physics-Aware Cross-Morphology Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03476)
*Tianchen Huang,Feiyang Yuan,Junchi Gu,Shurui Fang,Xiaohu Zhang,Yu Wang,Wei Gao,Shiwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Human2Humanoid无监督运动重定向框架，利用CycleGAN、骨架感知图卷积网络和形态不变末端执行器一致性损失，将人类运动高保真重定向到人形机器人，在未配对数据下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人形机器人与人类在骨骼拓扑、肢体比例、自由度等方面存在巨大形态差异，且缺乏成对的运动数据，因此无监督地将人类运动重定向到人形机器人具有挑战性。

Method: 基于CycleGAN架构，结合骨架感知图卷积网络捕捉拓扑相关运动特征；引入形态不变末端执行器一致性损失对齐归一化末端轨迹；施加物理感知可行性约束以减少接触伪影。

Result: 成功在无配对数据下将人类运动重定向至Unitree G1人形机器人，在下游可控性和物理可行性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Human2Humanoid框架有效解决了跨形态运动重定向中的域差距和尺度不匹配问题，为人形机器人远程操作和模仿学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Retargeting human motion to humanoid robots is critical for teleoperation, imitation learning and human-robot interaction. However, it remains challenging because of substantial morphological discrepancies between humans and robots, including differences in skeletal topology, limb proportions and degrees of freedom, as well as the scarcity of paired motion data. This paper presents Human2Humanoid, an unsupervised motion retargeting framework that transfers human motions to humanoid robot behaviors with high fidelity. To bridge the domain gap under unpaired data, we adopt a CycleGAN-based architecture equipped with a skeleton-aware graph convolutional network to capture topology-dependent motion features. To address cross-domain scale mismatches, we introduce a morphology-invariant end-effector consistency loss that aligns normalized end-effector trajectories to preserve motion semantics across embodiments. To improve physical plausibility and reduce contact artifacts, we impose explicit physics-aware feasibility constraints to encourage reproduction of the contact patterns in the source motion. Experimental results show that the proposed method successfully retargets human motion to the Unitree G1 humanoid robot without paired data, and outperforms existing methods in both downstream controllability and physical feasibility.

</details>


### [238] [SPADE: Sketch-guided Path Planning Augmented with Diffusion Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03512)
*Charbel Abi Hana,Tatiana Ghantous,Mikael Khalil,Anthony Rizk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对自主移动机器人路径规划中模仿学习泛化性差和鲁棒性低的问题，提出了一种增强框架：基于ROS 2重构了标注工具，并引入扩散增强训练策略。该方法在降低39.1% APE和33.5% FID的同时，参数量减少93.8%，兼具扩散模型的泛化能力和实时边缘部署特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模仿学习的路径规划方法在未见过的环境下泛化能力有限，且专家演示数据收集的鲁棒性较低，亟需一种能同时提升泛化性和鲁棒性的轻量级框架。

Method: 1) 基于ROS 2重新设计了标注工具，提升数据收集效率与质量；2) 提出将扩散模型的数据增强技术融入基线行为克隆模型，增强训练数据的多样性；3) 通过消融实验评估各组件贡献。

Result: 相比SOTA方法，绝对位姿误差（APE）降低39.1%，Fréchet Inception Distance（FID）降低33.5%，可训练参数量减少93.8%。同时实现了扩散级别的泛化能力与实时边缘部署性能。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了通过轻量级增强（改进标注工具+扩散增强）可显著提升模仿学习路径规划器的泛化性和鲁棒性，为AMR在复杂环境中的实用部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Path planning is essential for Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs). Conventional methods for incorporating human preferences into planning typically rely on either complex reward engineering or hardware-intensive solutions. Recent state-of-the-art frameworks leverage imitation learning to train behavior-specific path planning models from expert demonstrations. However, these approaches face two key limitations: limited generalization to unseen environments and low robustness in demonstration collection. To address these challenges, this work introduces an enhanced framework that focuses on two main contributions: an overhauled annotation tool built on ROS 2, and a novel training strategy that integrates diffusion-based augmentation into baseline behavioral cloning models. A dataset of expert demonstrations is provided and evaluated through ablation studies to assess the robustness of the proposed solution. The enhanced approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods with 39.1% lower Absolute Pose Error (APE) and 33.5% lower Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) while having 93.8% less trainable parameters. Moreover it attains diffusion-level generalization while preserving the real-time, on-edge properties of state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [239] [Bionic Human-Motion Style Transfer for Physically Executable Whole-Body Control of Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03536)
*Tianchen Huang,Mingkuan Zhao,Yang Gao,Feiyang Yuan,Junchi Gu,Xiaohu Zhang,Dongdong Zhao,Shi Yan,Yu Wang,Wei Gao,Shiwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仿生生成-控制框架，通过短时人类风格样本和物理感知潜扩散模型，实现人形机器人全身运动风格迁移，并在Unitree G1上达到96.0%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人表达性运动多依赖于固定示教或手动设计脚本，难以将已示教的风格复用至不同的运动内容中。受人类通过步态节律、姿势、摆臂和身体摇摆传递情感与意图线索的启发，本文旨在实现风格与内容的解耦与迁移。

Method: 提出仿生生成-控制框架：1）物理感知多条件潜扩散模型，融合风格、内容与轨迹条件；2）无分类器引导调整风格强度；3）接触一致性与时间平滑性正则化；4）基于聚类-精炼策略的预览式全身跟踪策略。

Result: 仿真与Unitree G1实验表明，该方法能将短时人类风格样本迁移至多种机器人运动内容，减少接触与抖动伪影，125次真实机器人实验成功率达96.0%。

Conclusion: 短时人类运动样本可作为可复用的仿生源，用于生成物理可执行的人形机器人表达性运动，验证了风格迁移在人形机器人上的可行性。

Abstract: Expressive whole-body motion is important for humanoid robots operating in human environments, where robots are expected to move stably while presenting readable and adjustable body behaviors. However, most expressive motions are still obtained from fixed demonstrations or manually designed scripts, making it difficult to reuse a demonstrated style across different motion contents. Inspired by the way human motion styles convey affective and intentional cues through gait rhythm, posture, arm swing and body sway, this paper proposes a bionic generation-to-control framework for exemplar-driven style transfer on humanoid robots. Given a short human style exemplar and a target content motion, the proposed framework generates a stylized whole-body reference that preserves the intended motion content while transferring the demonstrated style. A physics-aware multi-condition latent diffusion model is developed to fuse style, content and trajectory conditions, and classifier-free guidance is used to adjust the style intensity without retraining. To improve hardware executability, contact-consistency and temporal-smoothness regularization are imposed on decoded motions during training. The generated references are then converted into G1-compatible robot references and executed by a preview-based whole-body tracking policy trained with a cluster-and-distill strategy. Simulation and Unitree G1 experiments show that the proposed method can transfer short human style exemplars to diverse robot motion contents, reduce contact and jitter artifacts compared with animation-oriented style-transfer baselines, and achieve a 96.0% success rate over 125 reported real-robot trials. The results demonstrate the feasibility of using short human motion exemplars as reusable bionic sources for physically executable expressive humanoid motion.

</details>


### [240] [NVIDIA Isaac Sim: Enabling Scalable, GPU-Accelerated Simulation for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03551)
*Sicong Gao,Maurice Pagnucco,Tomasz Bednarz,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了NVIDIA Isaac Sim在机器人研究中的应用，重点分析了其GPU加速架构、并行训练能力、合成数据生成流水线，并与主流模拟器进行对比，总结了五大应用领域的使用模式及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述将Isaac Sim视为众多模拟器之一，缺乏对其架构特性、使用模式和局限性的系统分析，因此需要一篇专门的综述来填补这一空白。

Method: 从系统和应用两个视角出发，梳理Isaac Sim的架构，与主流模拟器进行对比，分析五大领域（数据生成、高保真仿真等）的代表性研究，总结通用使用模式。

Result: 归纳了Isaac Sim在数据生成和高保真仿真方面的典型使用模式，识别了其GPU加速和大规模并行训练的核心优势。

Conclusion: 指出了未来方向与挑战，包括物理开放世界学习、以仿真为中心的训练以及实际可用性约束。

Abstract: Simulation has become a core infrastructure for robotics research. Unlike previous simulators, NVIDIA Isaac Sim leverages GPU acceleration to enable large-scale parallel training and physics-accurate modeling. Its synthetic data generation pipeline alleviates the scarcity of high-quality training data, supporting data-driven robot learning and large-scale simulation-centric experimentation. However, existing surveys often treat it as one simulator among many, without a systematic analysis of its architectural characteristics, usage patterns, and limitations. This survey reviews Isaac Sim from system and application perspectives, outlining its architecture and comparing it with widely used simulators. We analyze representative studies across five major domains and summarize common usage patterns, particularly in data generation and high-fidelity simulation. We also outline key future directions and challenges, including physics open-world learning, simulation-centric training and practical usability constraints.

</details>


### [241] [Partially Observable Adversarial Patch Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03556)
*Xiaofei Wang,Mingliang Han,Tianyu Hao,Yi Yang,Yun-Bo Zhao,Keke Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部分可观测威胁模型下的对抗性补丁攻击方法，通过注意力图定位关键区域并优化补丁来同时破坏视觉语义理解和动作轨迹，在仿真和真实机器人环境中实现了长时域的任务失败诱导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA对抗攻击研究假设攻击者能访问完整执行轨迹，这在实际中不现实。本文旨在解决这一局限，提出更贴合实际的部分可观测威胁模型。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 利用注意力图定位与完整指令对应的视觉关键区域；2) 优化补丁以破坏目标对象的语义锚定并增加动作轨迹曲率，同时在感知和控制层面制造复合失败。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，该方法在部分可观测条件下能维持对抗效果，引发长时域干扰并显著降低任务成功率。

Conclusion: 本文证明了即使攻击者只能观察到轨迹前缀，仍能通过精心设计的固定补丁在VLA模型上实现有效的长时域对抗攻击，揭示了该类模型在实用场景下的安全隐患。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are gaining attention in robotics, yet their robustness to adversarial attacks remains largely unexplored. Existing work shows that adversarial patches can mislead VLA-based robots but assumes full access to the entire execution trajectory, an unrealistic requirement in practice. We address this limitation by formulating a partially observable threat model, where the adversary can exploit only a short prefix of the trajectory to generate a fixed patch applied to all subsequent frames. Under this setting, we propose a two-phase framework. First, we localize the patch using the model's attention maps to identify visually critical regions that correspond to the full instruction. Then, we optimize the patch to disrupt the semantic grounding of target objects and increase the curvature of action trajectories, thereby compounding failures in both perception and control. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world robotic environments show that our method sustains adversarial effects under partial observability, inducing long-horizon disruptions and significantly reducing task success rates.

</details>


### [242] [CANMOT: Class-Aware Noise Modeling for Multi-Object Tracking in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03590)
*Timo Osterburg,Stefan Schütte,Torsten Bertram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CANMOT，一种针对自动驾驶3D多目标跟踪的类别感知与目标对齐噪声建模方法，改进了标准卡尔曼滤波中全局噪声假设的缺陷，在nuScenes上减少了身份切换，但发现即使改进后不确定性校准仍存在严重不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准卡尔曼滤波在多目标跟踪中为所有类别使用全局统一的噪声协方差，忽略了不同交通参与者（如行人、车辆）的不确定性特征差异，导致模型过自信和身份切换问题。

Method: 提出CANMOT：为每个目标类别定义独立的对角过程噪声和测量噪声协方差矩阵，并在目标坐标系中表达以保留纵向-横向各向异性。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上，类别感知与目标对齐的噪声建模相比SotA方法显著减少了身份切换；通过ANEES和卡方检验发现标准KF基线存在严重过自信，CANMOT虽改善了校准但仍不一致。

Conclusion: 类别感知的噪声建模能提升跟踪性能和校准质量，但现有框架仍存在根本性的不确定性不一致问题，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Kalman filter (KF)-based multi-object tracking (MOT) remains a strong baseline for autonomous driving due to its strong performance, computational efficiency and interpretability. In most practical systems, the process noise and measurement noise covariances are defined globally and shared across object classes, presuming identical uncertainty characteristics across heterogeneous traffic participants.
  This work revisits this assumption and proposes CANMOT, a class-aware and object-aligned noise modeling framework for KF-based 3D MOT. Class-specific diagonal process and measurement covariance matrices are introduced and optionally expressed in the object coordinate frame to preserve longitudinal-lateral anisotropy.
  Systematic experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that class-aware and object-aligned noise modeling improves tracking performance and substantially reduces identity switches compared to state-of-the-art (SotA). In addition, the consistency of the estimated uncertainty is analyzed using the Average Normalized Estimation Error Squared (ANEES) and $χ^2$-based violation tests. The results reveal severe overconfidence in standard KF-based MOT baselines. While the proposed formulation improves calibration without modifying the underlying filtering framework, it still exhibits substantial inconsistency, highlighting the need for further research in this area.
  Code is available at https://github.com/rst-tu-dortmund/learned-3d-nms.

</details>


### [243] [PHASER: Phase-Aware and Semantic Experience Replay for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03598)
*Ziyang Chen,Shaoguang Wang,Weiyu Guo,Qianyi Cai,He Zhang,Pengteng Li,Yiren Zhao,Yandong Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHASER是一个面向VLA模型的持续学习框架，通过阶段中心容量分配和多模态干扰路由策略，解决机器人在开放环境中持续获取新技能时的灾难性遗忘问题，在LIBERO基准上最高提升31%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在开放环境中持续学习新技能时面临严重的灾难性遗忘问题。简单经验回放（ER）中的均匀采样与操作轨迹的时间特性不匹配，会系统性地欠采样短暂但因果关键的子技能（阶段饥饿），且忽略历史任务不同程度的遗忘。

Method: 提出PHASER框架：1）阶段中心容量分配，为所有子技能提供均等的内存支持；2）多模态干扰路由策略，动态优先处理遗忘风险高的历史阶段；3）Auto-PC管道，结合无监督动作信号变点检测和VLM语义验证，自动提取时间边界。

Result: 在三个VLA骨干网络上的LIBERO持续学习套件中，PHASER相比等预算ER提升高达31%的平均成功率，在LIBERO-Goal CL设置中达到87.8%的最终ASR。

Conclusion: PHASER作为架构无关的持续学习框架，有效解决了VLA模型在语言条件机器人操作中的灾难性遗忘问题，推动了机器人自主终身适应能力的发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved remarkable success in language-conditioned robotic manipulation. However, deploying these models in open-ended environments requires continuously acquiring novel skills, a process that inevitably triggers severe catastrophic forgetting of previously learned behaviors. While experience replay (ER) serves as a standard mitigating strategy, naive uniform sampling fundamentally misaligns with the temporal characteristics of manipulation trajectories. It systematically under-samples brief but causally critical sub-skills, leading to phase starvation, and completely overlooks the varying degrees of forgetting across historical tasks. To overcome these limitations, we introduce PHASER, an architecture-agnostic continual learning framework. PHASER employs a phase-centric capacity allocation to guarantee equal memory support for all sub-skills, coupled with a multi-modal interference routing strategy that dynamically prioritizes historical phases at high risk of forgetting. Furthermore, to enable fully autonomous lifelong adaptation, we integrate Auto-PC, a lightweight pipeline combining unsupervised action-signal change-point detection with VLM-based semantic verification to extract temporal boundaries without intensive manual supervision. Evaluated across three VLA backbones on LIBERO continual learning suites, PHASER yields substantial empirical improvements, increasing Average Success Rate (ASR) by up to 31% over matched-budget ER and achieving an 87.8% final ASR on the LIBERO-Goal CL setting.

</details>


### [244] [A 3D Isovist World Model -- Revealing a City's Unseen Geometry and Its Emergent Cross-City Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03609)
*Xuhui Lin,Stephen Law,Nanjiang Chen,Kunyao Li,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以3D isovist（球形可见性深度图）为预测目标的具身世界模型，用于城市导航智能体。该模型预测负空间（建筑物之间的开放体积）而非外观或平面占用网格，通过深度残差、自滚动计划采样和持久潜变量鸟瞰图实现了跨路径一致性。令人惊讶的是，仅在曼哈顿和巴黎训练的城市盲模型涌现出跨城市空间特征，城市身份可从时间潜变量中线性解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航世界模型要么预测外观（场景外观而非可通行空间），要么预测几何目标如鸟瞰占用网格（将三维环境压平到地面，丢失了多层结构和地面以上空间）。缺乏一个既能捕捉智能体实际穿行的可通行几何、又不纠缠光度信息、也不坍缩第三维的预测目标。

Method: 以3D isovist（球形可见性深度图）作为预测目标，编码每个方向到最近表面的距离。模型从历史isovist序列和运动动作预测下一个isovist；预测公式化为深度残差以继承建筑锐利边缘；采用自滚动计划采样（self-rollout scheduled sampling）训练以保持几何流形上的正确上下文；配备持久潜变量鸟瞰空间地图实现跨路径一致性。

Result: 一个仅在曼哈顿和巴黎训练的城市盲模型，其时间潜变量中涌现出跨城市空间特征，城市身份可从这些潜变量中线性解码，且性能远超单帧基线。该表示轻量、可解释、可复现。

Conclusion: 该方法为具身AI、机器人和城市分析提供了一种几何基质的空间推理表示，并开放了数据集和代码。

Abstract: Embodied agents that navigate cities rely on world models that predict how their surroundings will change as they move. But for navigation, what matters is not what the buildings look like; it is where the agent can go. Most world models nonetheless predict appearance, learning how a scene looks rather than the space an agent can move through. Those that do target geometry, such as bird's-eye-view occupancy grids, flatten the three-dimensional environment onto a ground plane, discarding the above-ground and multi-level structure that shapes real navigation. What is missing is a predictive target that captures the navigable geometry an agent actually traverses, without photometric entanglement and without collapsing the third dimension. Our key idea is to model the open volume between buildings, the negative space, encoded as a 3D isovist: a spherical visibility-depth map recording the distance to the nearest surface in every direction. We introduce an embodied world model that predicts the next isovist from a short history of past isovists and a movement action. The prediction is formulated as a depth residual so the decoder inherits sharp building edges, trained with self-rollout scheduled sampling to keep corrupted context on the geometry manifold, and equipped with a persistent latent bird's-eye-view spatial map for cross-path consistency. Our central finding is emergent and unexpected: a single city-blind model trained on Manhattan and Paris develops a cross-city spatial signature, with city identity linearly decodable from its temporal latents far above single-frame baselines, so the signature lives in the learned dynamics rather than in appearance. The representation is lightweight, interpretable, and reproducible, offering a geometric substrate for spatial reasoning in embodied AI, robotics, and urban analysis, released with an open dataset and pipeline.

</details>


### [245] [GN0: Toward a Unified Paradigm for Generation, Evaluation, and Policy Learning in Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03682)
*Xinhai Li,Xiaotao Zhang,Yuehao Huang,Jiankun Dong,Tianhang Wang,Sunyao Zhou,Yunzi Wu,Chengnuo Sun,Yunfei Ge,Qizhen Weng,Chi Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GN-Matrix框架，包含大规模导航数据集、基于3DGS的高保真仿真平台、首个BEV基准GN-Bench，以及基于强化学习的导航基础模型GN-BAE，在VLN任务上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN系统受限于导航数据的数量和质量，导致泛化能力和长程任务能力不足，亟需大规模、高质量的数据集和统一的仿真-学习框架。

Method: 1) 构建GN-Matrix数据集，通过自动化pipeline从多样化3D场景中生成大规模导航数据；2) 基于3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)引擎搭建高保真仿真平台，支持交互式漫游和碰撞感知导航；3) 提出首个BEV基准GN-Bench，引入动态3DGS化身进行人机交互评估；4) 开发RL驱动的导航基础模型BAE，结合监督学习、DAgger策略和下游RL探索；5) 将3DGS渲染的BEV表示作为紧凑记忆，释放VLM中的潜在空间推理能力。

Result: 在GN-Bench和VLN-CE上的广泛评估表明，GN0方法在性能上超越了现有的SOTA VLN方法。

Conclusion: GN-Matrix提供了涵盖数据、仿真和学习的统一框架，推动了具身导航在研究和工业应用中的发展。

Abstract: Embodied navigation connects intelligent agents with the physical world and is fundamental for general robotic intelligence. Limited availability and quality of navigation data have constrained Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) systems' generalization and long-horizon capabilities. To address this, we curate diverse 3D scenes and develop an automated pipeline for large-scale navigation data, resulting in the GN-Matrix dataset. Building on a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) engine, we introduce a high-fidelity simulation platform supporting interactive roaming and collision-aware navigation. We further propose GN-Bench, the first BEV-based benchmark incorporating dynamic 3DGS avatars for human-robot interaction evaluation. To leverage the simulator, we develop an RL-driven navigation foundation model, Break and Establish (BAE). After supervised learning, DAgger exposes the model to rollout-induced states, breaking narrow expert-centric distributions and enabling downstream RL exploration. This unified VLN paradigm integrates map-based and map-free tasks, including instruction following, human following, and goal navigation. GN-BAE formalizes high-fidelity 3DGS-rendered Bird's Eye View representations as compact memory, unlocking latent spatial reasoning in VLMs. Extensive evaluations on GN-Bench and VLN-CE show that GN0 outperforms state-of-the-art VLN methods. Overall, GN-Matrix offers a unified framework spanning data, simulation, and learning, advancing embodied navigation in research and industrial applications.

</details>


### [246] [Face versus Body Tracking for Human-Robot Interaction: An Egocentric Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03694)
*Jessica Wenninger,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对社交机器人面临的独特自我中心视觉挑战（如人的弹跳、相互遮挡、离帧等导致的身份切换问题），构建了Furhat机器人自采样的自定义标注数据集，系统评估了人脸vs身体追踪、扩展空间记忆与外观重识别（ReID）的影响，发现ReID可改善身体追踪但使人脸IDSW激增，最终通过优化管道将身份切换降低49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型主要为监控或自动驾驶优化，无法处理社交机器人面临的自我中心动态场景（如人跳动、遮挡、离帧），频繁的身份切换导致机器人对话中断，亟需针对社交互动的感知模型验证方法。

Method: 1) 构建Furhat机器人采集的自定义标注自我中心数据集；2) 系统化评估：分离检测错误与跟踪逻辑、对比人脸vs身体跟踪、评估扩展空间记忆与ReID的影响；3) 优化跟踪管道。

Result: 扩展空间记忆可缓解长时间遮挡但无法处理复杂动态事件；集成ReID显著提升身体跟踪稳定性，却因侧脸角度敏感性导致人脸身份切换激增；最终优化管道将IDSW降低49%。

Conclusion: 标准基准缺乏密集近距离遮挡场景，本文强调真实社交动态数据对验证HRI感知模型的必要性，优化后的跟踪方案可有效减少交互中断。

Abstract: To enable meaningful human-robot interaction (HRI), a robot must continuously assess engagement by consistently tracking users over time. State-of-the-art computer vision models, however, are heavily optimized for surveillance or autonomous driving. A social robot faces distinct egocentric challenges, such as humans bouncing, obstructing each other, or leaving the frame. Frequent identity switches (IDSW) cause the robot to lose its footing mid-conversation. To address this, we introduce a novel, custom-annotated egocentric dataset collected via the Furhat robot to capture complex social dynamics. We present a systematic evaluation isolating detection errors from tracking logic, comparing face versus body tracking, and assessing the impact of extended spatial memory and appearance re-identification (ReID). Results indicate that increasing spatial memory mitigates prolonged occlusions but fails on complex dynamic events. Integrating ReID resolves complex switches but exhibits opposing effects: it substantially improves body tracking stability, yet causes facial IDSW to spike due to profile angle sensitivity. Ultimately, our optimized pipeline reduces IDSW by 49\%, mitigating interaction breakdowns. Because standard benchmarks lack dense, close-quarter occlusions, this work highlights the critical need for natively captured social dynamics to truly validate HRI perception models.

</details>


### [247] [Neural Navigation Functions for Zero-Shot Generalizable Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03756)
*Benjamin D. Shaffer,Pei-An Hsieh,Brooks Kinch,Nathaniel Trask,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural Navigation Functions (Neural-NF)，一种能够零样本迁移至未见环境几何结构的可学习反应式导航函数。该方法将数据驱动适应置于结构化椭圆规划器中，通过学习导航目标的同时保持规划器结构。实验表明，Neural-NF在不同几何结构间实现了强零样本迁移，性能优于直接预测价值函数的学习型规划器达5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的导航方法通常在特定环境中训练，无法泛化到未见过的几何结构；而传统的反应式导航函数方法缺乏适应性和数据驱动能力。作者希望结合两者的优势，实现既保持结构化规划器理论保证（无碰撞、单调下降、全局最小值），又能通过数据驱动适应不同环境。

Method: 将内蕴拉普拉斯特征映射到局部PDE系数，通过求解边界值问题在每个目标域上生成全局一致的价值函数。该方法保持规划器结构，使得任意可学习模型都天然具有无碰撞、单调下降和全局最小值在目标点的性质，并可解释为线性可解的最优控制问题。

Result: Neural-NF在多种几何结构间实现了强零样本迁移能力，相比于直接预测价值函数的学习型规划器，性能提升高达5倍。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动适应与结构化椭圆规划器相结合的方法有效且高效，能够在保证理论性质的同时实现出色的泛化能力，为反应式导航提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce Neural Navigation Functions (Neural-NF), a learned reactive navigation function capable of zero-shot transfer across unseen environment geometries. Neural-NF places data-driven adaptation within a structured elliptic planner, where the navigation objective is learned while planner structure is preserved by construction. Specifically, intrinsic Laplacian-derived features are mapped to local PDE coefficients, and solving the resulting boundary value problem produces a globally consistent value function on each target domain. For every admissible learned model, the resulting policy is collision-free, provides monotonic descent and a global minimum at the goal by construction. This admits a linearly-solvable optimal-control interpretation for any parameter setting. Empirically, Neural-NF achieves strong zero-shot transfer across diverse geometries and outperforms learned planners that directly predict the value function by up to a $5\times$ improvement.

</details>


### [248] [Revisiting Embodied Chain-of-Thought for Generalizable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03784)
*Nan Sun,Yuan Zhang,Yongkun Yang,Wentao Zhao,Peiyan Li,Jun Guo,Wenxuan Song,Pengxiang Ding,Runze Suo,Yifei Su,Xin Xiao,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了迄今最大的具身思维链数据集（978K轨迹，226M样本），发现有效的具身CoT应将高层语义理解转化为具体动作指导（如末端执行器描述、图像空间轨迹），而非仅靠高层推理。为解决CoT作为自回归动作前缀时的推理误差累积和不稳定问题，提出ERVLA模型，将CoT作为表示塑造监督而非测试时推理，采用推理丢弃训练策略，在LIBERO-Plus上达86.9%成功率，VLABench上达53.2%，并在真实机器人实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身思维链旨在连接语言推理与机器人控制，但其有效形式（何种CoT最有效）和集成策略（如何将CoT融入VLA模型）尚未得到充分探索。现有工作缺乏大规模系统性研究。

Method: 1) 构建大规模具身CoT数据集（978K轨迹）；2) 系统性实验验证有效CoT形式（需具体动作引导，而非仅高层推理）；3) 揭示显式CoT作为自回归前缀的缩放不可靠性（推理误差累积、推理-动作耦合不稳定）；4) 提出ERVLA模型：采用推理丢弃策略，训练时利用CoT作为表示塑造监督，推理时直接预测动作而不解码CoT。

Result: LIBERO-Plus上86.9%成功率（SOTA），VLABench上53.2%成功率，在语义消歧和长程任务上显著优于基线，展示强分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 有效的具身CoT应作为训练时的表示塑造监督而非测试时推理过程，推理丢弃训练策略能提升模型的可扩展性和推理稳定性。

Abstract: Embodied chain-of-thought (CoT) aims to bridge linguistic reasoning and robotic control, but its effective form and integration strategy remain underexplored. In this paper, we revisit embodied CoT for vision-language-action (VLA) models at large scale. We construct the largest embodied CoT corpus to date, comprising 978,743 trajectories, 226.3M samples, and 2592.5 hours of robot data. Through extensive experiments, we find that effective embodied CoT should ground high-level semantic understanding into concrete action guidance, such as end-effector movement descriptions and image-space trajectories, while high-level reasoning alone brings only marginal gains. We further show that explicit CoT does not scale reliably when used as an autoregressive action prefix, as it suffers from compounding inference errors and unstable reasoning-action coupling. To address these limitations, we propose ERVLA, a VLA model that uses embodied CoT as representation-shaping supervision rather than mandatory test-time reasoning. ERVLA is trained with a reasoning-dropout strategy, enabling the model to absorb rich reasoning traces during training while predicting actions directly without CoT decoding during inference. This design improves scalability with increasing pre-training data and avoids autoregressive instability. ERVLA achieves state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO-Plus with an 86.9% success rate and reaches 53.2% success rate on VLABench, demonstrating strong out-of-distribution generalization. In real-robot experiments, ERVLA further outperforms competitive state-of-the-art baselines, especially on tasks requiring semantic disambiguation and long-horizon execution. Code, data, and model checkpoints will be released.

</details>


### [249] [Worth Remembering: Surprise-Gated Robot Episodic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03787)
*Nicolas Gorlo,Derek K. Wise,Alberto Speranzon,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于贝叶斯惊奇（Bayesian surprise）的门控机制，用于机器人长期记忆的选择性存储。该方法利用V-JEPA-2的潜在空间计算惊奇度，筛选高实用性事件存入记忆。结合4D场景图空间记忆，在机器人问答任务上比现有方法提升≥12%，并在事件分割任务中超越监督和无因果方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行通用任务时，人类指令常引用过去事件（如"带我去昨天化学品泄漏的地方"）。由于存储所有事件不可行，需要选择性记忆机制。但通用型机器人的未来任务不可预知，因此需要一种能自动筛选对未来任务有用的记忆的通用方法。

Method: 提出贝叶斯惊奇作为记忆形成的门控机制，在V-JEPA-2提供的语义丰富且部署无关的潜空间中计算惊奇度。惊奇度高的事件被选择性存储到情节记忆中，并与4D场景图空间记忆相结合。

Result: 在机器人问答基准上，时间、空间和二元问题均提升≥12%，超越现有机器人记忆方法；在事件分割任务中，以无监督因果方法超越监督和非因果方法。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯惊奇可作为通用型机器人长期记忆筛选的有效门控机制，结合语义丰富的潜空间表征，能显著提升机器人对过去事件的问答能力和事件理解能力。

Abstract: Robots solving generalist tasks need to be able to ground instructions in their past experience, since humans may refer to notable past events when giving a task (e.g., ``Take me to where the chemical spill happened yesterday''). Since memory limits make storing all past events infeasible, long-term robot memory must be selective, ideally retaining only those episodes with high utility for future tasks. However, future tasks are not typically given a priori for generalist robots. To select generically useful memories, we propose Bayesian surprise as a gating mechanism for memory formation. We present an approach to compute surprise in a semantically rich deployment-agnostic latent space provided by V-JEPA-2. Using our gated episodic memory to augment 4D scene graph-based spatial memory, we show a consistent improvement over state-of-the-art benchmarks in robot question answering, outperforming prior robot memory methods by $\geq12\%$ for temporal, spatial, and binary questions, and surpassing the performance of supervised and non-causal methods with an unsupervised causal method in event segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [250] [Optimal Design and Analytical Modeling of a Soft Fin-Ray Effect Gripper Finger Using the Finite Rigid Elements Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03798)
*Sara Adeli,Hassan Sayyaadi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并优化了一种基于Fin Ray效应的软体夹爪手指，通过有限刚体元法（FREM）建模，实现了对番茄等易损农产品的高精度轻柔抓取，FREM预测变形误差仅3%，ANSYS模型误差2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体夹爪具有非线性、无限自由度、材料属性多变等固有挑战，且农业中需要既能适应不规则形状又能精确控制抓取力的方案，现有方法难以兼顾柔顺性与力控制精度。

Method: 采用有限刚体元法（FREM）对Fin Ray效应软体手指建模，结合ANSYS有限元仿真与实验验证，以尖端位移、总变形、应力分布和接触力为指标，对手指长度、肋间距、肋角度和肋厚度进行参数优化。

Result: 优化后手指参数：长度30mm、肋间距10mm、7根肋、肋角-15°、肋厚1mm。FREM理论模型预测变形误差3%，ANSYS数值模型误差2%。

Conclusion: 有限刚体元法在保持解析精度的同时为力控制器开发提供了可靠基础，优化后的Fin Ray软体夹爪手指能够实现对番茄等农产品的轻柔、精确抓取。

Abstract: Fin Ray-inspired soft grippers offer a promising solution for gently handling delicate, irregular objects, especially in agriculture. The objective of this research is to design, fabricate, and model a Fin Ray Effect (FRE) soft gripper finger to enable precise force control in future applications. This design aims to gently grasp delicate agricultural products, such as tomatoes, that require both adaptability and accurate force application. To address the inherent challenges of soft robotics, including nonlinear behavior, infinite degrees of freedom, and variable material properties, the Finite Rigid Elements Method (FREM) was employed for modeling. This method preserves analytical accuracy while providing a reliable foundation for the development of a force controller in later stages. A detailed Finite Element Model (FEM) was created using ANSYS, and the analytical results were validated through simulation and experimental testing. The gripper's fingers were optimized based on four key criteria: tip displacement, total deflection, stress distribution, and contact force. The optimal finger configuration includes a length of 30 mm, rib spacing of 10 mm, seven ribs angled at -15 deg, and a rib thickness of 1 mm. Theoretical modeling using the FREM predicted finger deformation with a 3% error, while the ANSYS numerical model achieved 2% error.

</details>


### [251] [Let the Dynamics Flow: Stable Flow Matching Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03834)
*Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Francisco Leiva,Andrea Testa,Leonel Rozo,Javier Ruiz del Solar,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMDS框架，将流匹配生成模型与李雅普诺夫稳定性保证相结合，通过软约束和硬结构约束两种变体，在低维和高维状态空间上实现稳定、可扩展且多模态的机器人运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法虽能建模丰富的多模态运动策略，但缺乏形式化的稳定性保证；而传统基于动力系统的稳定方法又难以捕捉复杂任务中的动作分布多样性。两者之间存在空白。

Method: 将动力学系统参数化为流匹配模型，同时通过两种方式施加稳定性约束：(1) 基于惩罚项的软约束；(2) 嵌入模型架构的硬结构约束。两种变体均扩展至李群。

Result: 在基准数据集、仿真和人形机器人上验证，SFMDS在低维和高维状态空间中均能学习稳定、可扩展且多模态的动力学系统。

Conclusion: SFMDS弥合了高容量生成模型与形式化Lyapunov稳定性保证之间的鸿沟，实现了既安全又富有表现力的机器人运动生成。

Abstract: Flow matching has recently emerged as a powerful approach for imitation learning, enabling scalable, expressive, and multimodal motion policies. However, incorporating formal stability guarantees into these generative models, a prerequisite to ensure safe and generalizable robot behaviors, remains a significant challenge. While modeling robot motions as dynamical systems allows for such stability-based inductive biases, existing frameworks struggle to capture the rich action distributions inherent in complex robotic tasks. This paper introduces Stable Flow Matching Dynamical Systems (SFMDS), a novel framework that bridges the gap between high-capacity generative modeling and formal Lyapunov stability guarantees. SFMDS parametrizes dynamical systems via flow matching while simultaneously constraining the model to a family of stable solutions. We propose two variants: a soft constraint based on a penalty term, and a hard structural constraint embedded directly in the model architecture. We further extend both formulations to Lie groups. Experiments on benchmark datasets, in simulation, and on a humanoid robot show that SFMDS learns stable, scalable, and multimodal dynamical systems in low- and high-dimensional state spaces, enabling safe and expressive robot motion generation.

</details>


### [252] [Denoising Tells When to Replan: Denoising-Variance Adaptive Chunking for Flow-Based Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03847)
*Xiangdong Feng,Yuxuan Cheng,Chen Shi,Boyao Han,Yuxuan Yan,Yitong Hong,Zhuotao Tian,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DVAC方法，通过分析流式机器人策略去噪过程中的方差信号，自适应地决定每个预测动作块中执行多少动作，在保持动作连贯性的同时减少重规划频率，提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分块策略使用固定的执行步长，忽略了自由空间运动和精度关键操作阶段需要不同重规划频率的问题。

Method: 提出DVAC方法，在测试时通过测量去噪最后几步中干净动作估计的方差，执行稳定的低方差前缀，在高方差动作执行前进行重规划，并利用滚动估计校准阈值。

Result: 在LIBERO上成功率从94.75%提升至98.00%，重规划次数减少43.0%；在RoboTwin、CALVIN及真实世界操作中均有提升。

Conclusion: DVAC通过自适应动作分块，有效平衡了动作连贯性与重规划效率，是一种无需重新训练的通用测试时优化方法。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a common inference strategy for flow-based robot policies, improving action coherence by modeling multi-step temporal dependencies in demonstrations. However, the execution horizon is still typically set as an empirical fixed value, overlooking that predictable free-space motions and precision-critical interaction phases often require different replanning frequencies. In this work, we first show that the denoising process of flow-based policies contains an intrinsic signal of task phases: clean-action estimates remain stable during predictable motion phases, but fluctuate more strongly around contact-rich or precision-sensitive operations. Motivated by this observation, we propose DVAC (Denoising-Variance Adaptive Chunking), a test-time method that adaptively determines how many actions to execute from each predicted chunk. DVAC measures the variance of clean-action estimates over the final denoising steps, executes the stable low-variance prefix, and replans before high-variance future actions are committed. To transfer across tasks and rollouts, DVAC further calibrates the threshold with a rolling estimate of the local variance scale. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, CALVIN, and real-world manipulation show that DVAC improves task success while reducing replanning frequency. With a $π_{0.5}$-based policy, DVAC improves LIBERO success from 94.75% to 98.00% and reduces replanning by 43.0%, while also yielding aggregate gains on RoboTwin and CALVIN and improving real-world execution efficiency.

</details>


### [253] [Semantic-weighted ICP for LiDAR Odometry: Class-Aware Residual Reweighting for Robust Scan Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03905)
*Vasco Carvalho,Tiago Barros,Urbano J. Nunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义类别加权ICP方法，根据语义类别的几何稳定性对点云残差加权，而非简单过滤，以提升LiDAR里程计在动态和非结构化环境中的表现。实验在SemanticKITTI和RELLIS-3D上验证，尤其在越野场景中效果显著，且权重策略的有效性高度依赖环境语义组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何ICP在动态/非结构化环境中因运动物体、稀疏几何特征、植被等导致匹配退化；现有引入语义信息的工作多采用严格过滤策略，忽略了部分不稳定但仍有信息量的结构。

Method: 提出语义类别加权ICP：不直接过滤点，而是根据语义类别的预期几何稳定性对残差进行加权，使不稳定但信息丰富的结构（如植被）参与配准，同时抑制动态目标的影响。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和RELLIS-3D数据集上验证，语义加权ICP提升了位姿估计精度，尤其在越野等缺乏刚性特征的环境中改善明显；权重策略的效果高度依赖场景的语义与结构组成。

Conclusion: 语义加权ICP优于传统几何方法及严格过滤策略，但有效性受环境语义组成影响，提示未来需根据场景自适应调整权重。

Abstract: LiDAR odometry is a fundamental component of autonomous robotic systems, relying on geometric registration between consecutive point clouds to estimate ego-motion. However, traditional geometric approaches often degrade in dynamic or unstructured environments due to unreliable correspondences caused by moving objects, sparse geometric features, vegetation, and semantically ambiguous structures. Existing works have shown that, some of these limitations can be addressed by introducing semantic information from the environment in the registration process. In this work, we build on this, and show that not all elements in the environment are equally relevant for registration. Hence, we propose a semantic class-weighted ICP for LiDAR odometry. Instead of strictly filtering out points belonging to specific semantic classes, the proposed approach weights the residuals of points belonging to semantic categories based on their expected geometric stability. This strategy enables informative but potentially unstable structures, to contribute to the registration process while mitigating the influence of dynamic objects. The experimental evaluation was conducted on the SemanticKITTI and RELLIS-3D datasets, which include urban, highway, rural, and off-road environments. The empirical results show that the proposed Semantic-weighted ICP improves pose estimation, especially in challenging off-road scenarios where conventional rigid features are scarce. Furthermore, the analysis reveals that the effectiveness of this weighting strategy is highly environment-dependent, influenced by the structural and semantic composition of the scene.

</details>


### [254] [Multi-Robot Bearing-only Pose Estimation via Angle Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03931)
*J. Francisco Presenza,Leonardo J. Colombo,Ignacio Mas,Juan I. Giribet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于角度刚性的分布式轴承位姿估计器，用于时变多机器人系统，仅需角度刚性条件（弱于轴承刚性），并在持续性激励运动下实现局部一致指数稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于轴承的多机器人位姿估计方法通常需要"轴承刚性"这一较强条件，本文旨在放松这一条件，探索更弱的"角度刚性"条件是否也能实现有效的分布式位姿估计。

Method: 利用机体坐标系下的轴承角计算位置估计（无需朝向信息），再从估计位置、轴承及其导数恢复SO(3)中的朝向。仅要求有向感知拓扑满足角度刚性条件。

Result: 在持续性激励运动假设下，证明了所提观测器具有局部一致指数稳定性。仿真结果验证了方案的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 角度刚性条件足以替代更强的轴承刚性条件来实现分布式位姿估计，为多机器人系统的感知拓扑设计提供了更灵活的选择。

Abstract: This letter proposes a novel distributed bearing-based pose estimator for time-varying multi-robot systems. The method uses angles computed from body-frame bearings to estimate the robots' positions in $\mathbb{R}^3$ without knowledge of their orientations. The orientations in $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ are recovered from the estimated positions, the bearings, and the bearing derivatives. The proposed observer only requires the (directed) sensing topology to be \textit{angle-rigid}, a weaker condition than the commonly used ones like bearing rigidity. Local uniform exponential stability of the proposed observer is established under the assumption of persistently exciting motions for a subset of robots. Simulations are presented and discussed to evaluate the scheme's effectiveness and practicality.

</details>


### [255] [PointAction: 3D Points as Universal Action Representations for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03943)
*Mutian Tong,Han Jiang,Qiao Feng,Lingjie Liu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointAction提出通过显式4D点云建模桥接视频预测与机器人动作，在预训练视频扩散模型基础上联合预测RGB帧和动态3D点图，利用扩散动作解码器将点动力学映射为可执行动作，显著降低了动作歧义并支持跨任务跨本体的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-Action Models依赖RGB视频预测，但缺乏度量3D运动、接触几何和细粒度空间约束，导致动作基础不明确；同时跨任务跨本体的动作监督标注成本高昂。

Method: 1) 微调基础视频生成模型，联合预测未来RGB帧与动态3D点图（4D点云）；2) 将时间上一致的3D点动力学作为结构化、与本体无关的动作接口；3) 使用基于扩散的动作解码器将点动力学映射为可执行的机器人动作。

Result: 在机器人场景中达到SOTA的4D生成质量；在仿真中优于现有基线；可泛化到预训练中未见过的两个真实机器人手臂。

Conclusion: PointAction通过度量3D点动力学作为视频预测与动作控制之间的中间接口，有效降低了RGB-only动作基础的不确定性，并在有限动作监督下实现了跨任务、跨本体的泛化能力。

Abstract: Video-Action Models (VAMs) leverage the broad visual dynamics captured by pre-trained video diffusion models, offering a promising path toward generalizable robot manipulation. However, RGB-only video rollouts are not directly actionable: they leave metric 3D motion, contact geometry, and fine-grained spatial constraints under-specified, making action grounding ambiguous. Meanwhile, scaling action supervision across diverse tasks and embodiments remains costly. We present PointAction, a framework that bridges video predictions to robot actions through explicit point-based 4D modeling. PointAction fine-tunes a foundation video generation model to jointly predict future RGB frames and dynamic 3D pointmaps, producing temporally consistent 3D motion of task-relevant scene geometry. These point dynamics serve as a structured, embodiment-agnostic action interface, which a diffusion-based action decoder maps to executable robot actions. By using metric 3D point dynamics as the interface between video prediction and control, PointAction reduces the ambiguity of RGB-only action grounding and supports transfer across tasks and embodiments with limited action supervision. Experiments show that PointAction achieves state-of-the-art 4D generation quality on robot scenes, outperforms existing baselines in simulation, and generalizes to two real robot arms unseen during pretraining.

</details>


### [256] [Preference-Calibrated Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03949)
*Zeyi Liu,Guangyao Liu,Yinuo Qu,Yuquan Xue,Bofang Jia,Chunhua Yang,Weihua Gui,Keke Huang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACT是一个偏好校准的演员-评论家训练框架，通过利用人类干预中的隐式偏好信号，对次优轨迹段进行信用重分配，从而提升人机协同强化学习的样本效率和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HIL-RL方法对所有过渡一致应用折扣终端奖励，忽略了每个过渡对任务成功的实际贡献，导致Q值高估并误导策略朝次优行为模式更新。

Method: 1) 设计进度模型从人类示范中学习，识别次优段进行信用校正；2) 在干预状态下构建偏好对，定义反事实优势来惩罚次优段的Bellman目标；3) 在有界均值空间中直接对齐策略与人类纠正动作。

Result: 在五个真实机器人操作任务中，PACT将平均成功率提升24.5%，收敛速度提升1.3倍。

Conclusion: PACT通过偏好校准的信用分配和策略对齐，有效提升了HIL-RL的样本效率和性能。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) improves sample efficiency in real-robot manipulation through online human intervention. However, successful trajectories may include suboptimal actions that deviate from the desired task-execution path and force human intervention. Existing HIL-RL methods typically apply the consistent credit assignment principle to all transitions, uniformly propagating discounted terminal rewards through suboptimal segments, ignoring the actual contribution of each transition to task success. This overestimates Q-values for critic learning and indirectly misguides actor updates toward suboptimal behavior patterns. To this end, we propose PACT, a Preference-calibrated Actor-Critic Training framework that leverages the implicit preference signals induced by intervention to perform credit reassignment on identified suboptimal segments while directly guiding policy training for unbiased critic-actor learning. Specifically, we first design a progress model that learns from human demonstration and identifies suboptimal segments for credit correction. Then, from the human action and resampled policy action at the intervention state, we build preference pairs to define a counterfactual advantage that penalizes Bellman targets of the identified suboptimal segment, enabling directional credit calibration. Moreover, we directly align the policy with human corrective actions in the bounded mean space, providing an additional signal beyond critic-guided updates. Across five real-robot manipulation tasks, PACT improves the average success rate by 24.5% and achieves 1.3 times faster convergence, thereby improving both RL sample efficiency and performance. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HILRL-A1X-BC05.

</details>


### [257] [Self-Refining Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Conditioned UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03963)
*Roohan Ahmed Khan,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRL框架，利用多模态GPT智能体自动设计奖励函数、通过闭环反馈优化策略，并在无人机导航任务中实现从仿真到现实的高效迁移，成功率达91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在自主导航中依赖人工设计的奖励函数和反复手动调参，耗时且难以保证任务成功率，亟需提升奖励设计与策略优化的自动化程度。

Method: AgenticRL框架包含：(1)多模态GPT智能体解析任务信息与视觉场景，生成任务特定奖励函数；(2)使用PPO算法训练策略；(3)GPT智能体作为评估者，通过诊断包生成反馈，识别失败模式并闭环优化奖励函数；(4)推理阶段利用真实图像与自然语言任务信息自动选择合适策略。

Result: 闭环优化使策略性能相比初始奖励提升71%；仿真到现实迁移成功率为91%，sim-to-real准确率为94%。在门穿越、避障、越障着陆、轨迹跟踪、运动行为学习等任务中均有效。

Conclusion: AgenticRL通过大语言模型驱动的自主奖励设计与闭环优化，显著提升了无人机导航任务的自动化程度与性能，并成功实现了从仿真到现实的迁移。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has shown strong potential for enabling autonomous robots to learn complex navigational tasks. However, its practical use still depends heavily on human designed reward functions and repeated manual fine tuning, which is time consuming and does not guarantee high success in the desired task. This paper presents AgenticRL, agent guided reinforcement learning framework that increases autonomy in reward design, policy refinement, and real world deployment for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAV) navigation tasks. AgenticRL uses a multimodal generative pre-trained tansformer (GPT) agent to interpret task information and visual scene observations, generate task specific reward functions, train policies using Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, and then act as a critic by evaluating the trained policy through diagnosis packets to generate feedback. Based on this feedback, the agent identifies failure modes and refines the reward function in a closed loop self improvement process. To further leverage the multimodal GPT agent during inference, AgenticRL uses real world images and natural language task information to automatically identify the active scenario and select the appropriate trained policy for execution. The framework is evaluated on multiple navigational tasks, including gate traversal, obstacle avoidance, wall barrier crossing with landing, trajectory following, and motion behavior learning. Experimental results show that the closed loop refinement process improves policy behavior compared with initial rewards by 71%. We also demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of the proposed framework, achieving a real world success rate of 91% and a sim-to-real accuracy of 94%.

</details>


### [258] [Humanoid-GPT: Scaling Data and Structure for Zero-Shot Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03985)
*Zekun Qi,Xuchuan Chen,Dairu Liu,Chenghuai Lin,Yunrui Lian,Sikai Liang,Zhikai Zhang,Yu Guan,Jilong Wang,Wenyao Zhang,Xinqiang Yu,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Humanoid-GPT是一个基于GPT风格Transformer的人形全身控制模型，在20亿帧的运动数据上预训练，实现了对高动态行为的跟踪和前所未有的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于浅层MLP的跟踪器受限于数据稀缺和敏捷性-泛化性的权衡，缺乏大规模预训练模型来同时实现高动态运动跟踪和零样本泛化。

Method: 采用GPT风格的因果注意力Transformer，在20亿帧的重新定向运动语料库（整合所有主要动作捕捉数据集和大规模内部录制数据）上进行预训练。

Result: 模型能够跟踪高动态行为，并在未见过的运动和未见过的控制任务上实现前所未有的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过同时扩展数据和模型容量，Humanoid-GPT建立了新的性能前沿，在零样本泛化和高动态复杂运动跟踪方面均表现出色。

Abstract: We introduce Humanoid-GPT, a GPT-style Transformer with causal attention trained on a billion-scale motion corpus for whole-body control. Unlike prior shallow MLP trackers constrained by scarce data and an agility-generalization trade-off, Humanoid-GPT is pre-trained on a 2B-frame retargeted corpus that unifies all major mocap datasets with large-scale in-house recordings. Scaling both data and model capacity yields a single generative Transformer that tracks highly dynamic behaviors while achieving unprecedented zero-shot generalization to unseen motions and control tasks. Extensive experiments and scaling analyses show that our model establishes a new performance frontier, demonstrating robust zero-shot generalization to unseen tasks while simultaneously tracking highly dynamic and complex motions.

</details>
